Abstract: Speakers of under-represented languages face both a language barrier, as most online knowledge is in a few dominant languages, and a modality barrier, since information is largely text-based while many languages are primarily oral. We address this for French-Wolof by training the first bilingual speech-text Matryoshka embedding model, enabling efficient retrieval of French text from Wolof speech queries without relying on a costly ASR-translation pipelines. We introduce large-scale data curation pipelines and new benchmarks, compare modeling strategies, and show that modality fusion within a frozen text Matryoshka model performs best. Although trained only for retrieval, the model generalizes well to other tasks, such as speech intent detection, indicating the learning of general semantic representations. Finally, we analyze cost-accuracy trade-offs across Matryoshka dimensions and ranks, showing that information is concentrated only in a few components, suggesting potential for efficiency improvements.

</details>


### [174] [AgenticSum: An Agentic Inference-Time Framework for Faithful Clinical Text Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20040)
*Fahmida Liza Piya,Rahmatollah Beheshti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgenticSum是一个推理时智能体框架，通过分离上下文选择、生成、验证和有针对性的纠正来减少幻觉内容，从而改进使用大型语言模型进行临床文本摘要的事实一致性，并在各种评估指标上显示出一致的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动化临床文本摘要方面潜力巨大，但由于临床文档的长度、噪音和异构性，保持事实一致性仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 我们提出了AgenticSum，一个推理时、智能体框架，它将上下文选择、生成、验证和有针对性的纠正分开，以减少幻觉内容。该框架将摘要分解为协调阶段：压缩任务相关上下文、生成初步草稿、使用内部注意力基础信号识别支持薄弱的片段，并在监督控制下选择性地修改标记内容。我们使用基于参考的指标、LLM作为判断评估和人工评估，在两个公共数据集上对AgenticSum进行了评估。

Result: 在各种衡量标准下，AgenticSum与普通的LLM和其他强基线相比，表现出持续的改进。

Conclusion: 我们的结果表明，具有有针对性纠正的结构化智能体设计提供了一种有效的推理时解决方案，以改进使用LLM进行临床笔记摘要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer substantial promise for automating clinical text summarization, yet maintaining factual consistency remains challenging due to the length, noise, and heterogeneity of clinical documentation. We present AgenticSum, an inference-time, agentic framework that separates context selection, generation, verification, and targeted correction to reduce hallucinated content. The framework decomposes summarization into coordinated stages that compress task-relevant context, generate an initial draft, identify weakly supported spans using internal attention grounding signals, and selectively revise flagged content under supervisory control. We evaluate AgenticSum on two public datasets, using reference-based metrics, LLM-as-a-judge assessment, and human evaluation. Across various measures, AgenticSum demonstrates consistent improvements compared to vanilla LLMs and other strong baselines. Our results indicate that structured, agentic design with targeted correction offers an effective inference time solution to improve clinical note summarization using LLMs.

</details>


### [175] [Multilingual Large Language Models do not comprehend all natural languages to equal degrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20065)
*Natalia Moskvina,Raquel Montero,Masaya Yoshida,Ferdy Hubers,Paolo Morosi,Walid Irhaymi,Jin Yan,Tamara Serrano,Elena Pagliarini,Fritz Günther,Evelina Leivada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在12种不同语族语言上的语言理解能力，发现模型在类型学上多样化的语言中表现出显著的语言准确性，但均低于人类基线。与预期相反，英语并非表现最佳的语言，反而被几种罗曼语系语言超越。研究还讨论了分词、语言距离、训练数据量和数据来源等因素对LLM性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLM语言理解能力的理解有限，因为大多数基准测试主要评估高资源语言，并且默认假设英语是LLM表现最好的语言，而低资源语言的输出可靠性较低。本研究旨在追踪LLM在不同语言中的理解能力差异。

Method: 研究选取了3个流行的大型语言模型，在12种代表印欧语系、亚非语系、突厥语系、汉藏语系和日本语系的语言上执行语言理解任务。

Result: 研究结果表明，这些模型在类型学上多样化的语言中表现出显著的语言准确性，但均未能达到人类基线水平，尽管差距程度不同。与预期相反，英语并非表现最佳的语言，它系统性地被几种罗曼语系语言（甚至是资源较低的语言）超越。

Conclusion: LLM的性能受到多种因素的影响，包括分词、与西班牙语和英语的语言距离、训练数据的大小以及高/低资源语言和WEIRD/非WEIRD社区的数据来源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) play a critical role in how humans access information. While their core use relies on comprehending written requests, our understanding of this ability is currently limited, because most benchmarks evaluate LLMs in high-resource languages predominantly spoken by Western, Educated, Industrialised, Rich, and Democratic (WEIRD) communities. The default assumption is that English is the best-performing language for LLMs, while smaller, low-resource languages are linked to less reliable outputs, even in multilingual, state-of-the-art models. To track variation in the comprehension abilities of LLMs, we prompt 3 popular models on a language comprehension task across 12 languages, representing the Indo-European, Afro-Asiatic, Turkic, Sino-Tibetan, and Japonic language families. Our results suggest that the models exhibit remarkable linguistic accuracy across typologically diverse languages, yet they fall behind human baselines in all of them, albeit to different degrees. Contrary to what was expected, English is not the best-performing language, as it was systematically outperformed by several Romance languages, even lower-resource ones. We frame the results by discussing the role of several factors that drive LLM performance, such as tokenization, language distance from Spanish and English, size of training data, and data origin in high- vs. low-resource languages and WEIRD vs. non-WEIRD communities.

</details>


### [176] [BabyLM Turns 4: Call for Papers for the 2026 BabyLM Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20092)
*Leshem Choshen,Ryan Cotterell,Mustafa Omer Gul,Jaap Jumelet,Tal Linzen,Aaron Mueller,Suchir Salhan,Raj Sanjay Shah,Alex Warstadt,Ethan Gotlieb Wilcox*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BabyLM工作坊和竞赛旨在融合认知建模与语言建模，呼吁研究人员参与数据高效预训练挑战（含通用赛道和新增的多语言赛道），并征集相关领域的论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在消除认知建模和语言建模之间的界限，并通过举办BabyLM竞赛和工作坊来推动相关研究。

Method: 举办第四届BabyLM竞赛，包含数据高效预训练的通用赛道和新增的多语言赛道；同时征集与训练效率、认知合理性研究、弱模型评估等相关的论文。

Result: 摘要中未提及研究结果。

Conclusion: 呼吁研究人员积极参与BabyLM竞赛和论文投稿，以共同促进认知建模和语言建模的融合。

Abstract: BabyLM aims to dissolve the boundaries between cognitive modeling and language modeling. We call for both workshop papers and for researchers to join the 4th BabyLM competition. As in previous years, we call for participants in the data-efficient pretraining challenge in the general track. This year, we also offer a new track: Multilingual. We also call for papers outside the competition in any relevant areas. These include training efficiency, cognitively plausible research, weak model evaluation, and more.

</details>


<div id='cs.DL'></div>

# cs.DL [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Iconographic Classification and Content-Based Recommendation for Digitized Artworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19698)
*Krzysztof Kutt,Maciej Baczyński*

Main category: cs.DL

TL;DR: 该文提出了一个概念验证系统，利用Iconclass词汇和人工智能方法自动化数字化艺术品的图像志分类和内容推荐。该系统集成了YOLOv8对象检测、算法映射到Iconclass代码、抽象意义的基于规则推理以及三种互补的推荐器（分层邻近度、IDF加权重叠和Jaccard相似度）。评估表明，该解决方案有潜力加速大型文化遗产存储库的编目并增强导航，核心在于利用计算机视觉识别可见元素并使用符号结构（Iconclass层次结构）来获取意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大型文化遗产存储库中数字化艺术品图像志分类和内容推荐的挑战，以加速编目并增强导航。

Method: 该系统实现了一个四阶段的工作流程，用于分类和推荐，该流程将YOLOv8对象检测与Iconclass代码的算法映射、抽象意义的基于规则推理以及三种互补的推荐器（分层邻近度、IDF加权重叠和Jaccard相似度）相结合。

Result: 评估表明该解决方案具有潜力：Iconclass感知的计算机视觉和推荐方法可以加速大型文化遗产存储库的编目并增强导航。关键在于让计算机视觉提出可见元素并使用符号结构（Iconclass层次结构）来获取意义。

Conclusion: Iconclass感知的计算机视觉和推荐方法通过结合计算机视觉识别可见元素和利用Iconclass层次结构获取意义，在加速大型文化遗产存储库的编目和增强导航方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: We present a proof-of-concept system that automates iconographic classification and content-based recommendation of digitized artworks using the Iconclass vocabulary and selected artificial intelligence methods. The prototype implements a four-stage workflow for classification and recommendation, which integrates YOLOv8 object detection with algorithmic mappings to Iconclass codes, rule-based inference for abstract meanings, and three complementary recommenders (hierarchical proximity, IDF-weighted overlap, and Jaccard similarity). Although more engineering is still needed, the evaluation demonstrates the potential of this solution: Iconclass-aware computer vision and recommendation methods can accelerate cataloging and enhance navigation in large heritage repositories. The key insight is to let computer vision propose visible elements and to use symbolic structures (Iconclass hierarchy) to reach meaning.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [CRCC: Contrast-Based Robust Cross-Subject and Cross-Site Representation Learning for EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19138)
*Xiaobin Wong,Zhonghua Zhao,Haoran Guo,Zhengyi Liu,Yu Wu,Feng Yan,Zhiren Wang,Sen Song*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究针对脑电图（EEG）神经解码模型在不同采集地点泛化能力差的问题，提出了一种偏置分解泛化框架和名为CRCC的两阶段训练范式，通过数据标准化、表征级约束、对比学习和对抗优化，显著提高了模型在未见站点下的零样本迁移平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于结构化、站点依赖的偏置，脑电图（EEG）神经解码模型在跨采集地点泛化时常失败。

Method: 将跨站点临床EEG学习重新定义为偏置分解泛化问题，识别了三个基本偏置因子，并提出了一个通过数据标准化和表征级约束来缓解其影响的通用训练框架。构建了一个标准化的多站点MDD脑电图基准，并引入了CRCC，这是一个结合了编码器-解码器预训练与通过跨受试者/站点对比学习和站点对抗优化进行联合微调的两阶段训练范式。

Result: CRCC持续优于最先进的基线模型，在严格的零样本站点迁移下，平衡准确率提高了10.7个百分点。

Conclusion: CRCC展示了对未见环境的鲁棒泛化能力。

Abstract: EEG-based neural decoding models often fail to generalize across acquisition sites due to structured, site-dependent biases implicitly exploited during training. We reformulate cross-site clinical EEG learning as a bias-factorized generalization problem, in which domain shifts arise from multiple interacting sources. We identify three fundamental bias factors and propose a general training framework that mitigates their influence through data standardization and representation-level constraints. We construct a standardized multi-site EEG benchmark for Major Depressive Disorder and introduce CRCC, a two-stage training paradigm combining encoder-decoder pretraining with joint fine-tuning via cross-subject/site contrastive learning and site-adversarial optimization. CRCC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and achieves a 10.7 percentage-point improvement in balanced accuracy under strict zero-shot site transfer, demonstrating robust generalization to unseen environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Exploring the Ethical Concerns in User Reviews of Mental Health Apps using Topic Modeling and Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18454)
*Mohammad Masudur Rahman,Beenish Moalla Chaudhry*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个基于自然语言处理（NLP）的框架，通过分析用户评论来评估AI心理健康移动应用的伦理方面。研究发现，现有的伦理考量不足以应对现代AI技术，且新兴的伦理挑战被忽视。这项工作有助于开发一个持续评估系统，以提高AI驱动心理健康聊天机器人的公平性、透明度和可信赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的心理健康移动应用快速增长，引发了对其伦理考量和用户信任的担忧。

Method: 提出一个基于NLP的框架，用于从Google Play Store和Apple App Store的用户评论中评估伦理方面。首先，收集并清洗数据；然后，应用主题建模识别与伦理相关的潜在主题，并将其映射到已有的伦理原则；此外，采用自下而上的方法，使用基于Transformer的零样本分类模型从评论中发现新的新兴伦理；最后，使用情感分析捕获用户对每个伦理方面的感受。

Result: 结果表明，对于现代AI技术而言，众所周知的伦理考量不足，并且缺少新兴的伦理挑战，这揭示了这些应用程序如何维护或忽视关键的道德价值观。

Conclusion: 这项工作有助于开发一个持续的评估系统，可以增强AI驱动心理健康聊天机器人的公平性、透明度和可信赖性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI-driven mental health mobile apps has raised concerns about their ethical considerations and user trust. This study proposed a natural language processing (NLP)-based framework to evaluate ethical aspects from user-generated reviews from the Google Play Store and Apple App Store. After gathering and cleaning the data, topic modeling was applied to identify latent themes in the context of ethics using topic words and then map them to well-recognized existing ethical principles described in different ethical frameworks; in addition to that, a bottom-up approach is applied to find any new and emergent ethics from the reviews using a transformer-based zero-shot classification model. Sentiment analysis was then used to capture how users feel about each ethical aspect. The obtained results reveal that well-known ethical considerations are not enough for the modern AI-based technologies and are missing emerging ethical challenges, showing how these apps either uphold or overlook key moral values. This work contributes to developing an ongoing evaluation system that can enhance the fairness, transparency, and trustworthiness of AI-powered mental health chatbots.

</details>


### [180] [The Algorithmic Unconscious: Structural Mechanisms and Implicit Biases in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18468)
*Philippe Boisnard*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了“算法无意识”的概念，指出大型语言模型（LLMs）的偏差不仅来源于数据集，更直接源于其内部技术机制，如分词。研究发现阿拉伯语系在分词时相对于英语存在系统性过度分割（1.6倍至4倍），导致推断成本增加并影响模型表现。文章呼吁通过技术审计来解决这些基础设施层面的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解释LLMs中的偏差不仅仅是数据集构成或人类意图投射的结果，而是直接来源于模型的内部技术机制（如分词、注意力机制、统计优化和对齐程序），并解决围绕LLMs责任、中立性和纠正的理论模糊性。

Method: 文章引入了“算法无意识”的概念，并通过对平行语料库中不同基础设施（如OpenAI、Anthropic、SentencePiece/Mistral）的分词进行比较分析，特别关注阿拉伯语系（现代标准阿拉伯语和马格里布方言）与英语的分词差异。此外，研究还将这些实证发现与因果偏差、维度坍缩导致的少数特征擦除以及安全对齐诱导的规范性偏差等三种结构机制联系起来。

Result: 研究发现，阿拉伯语系相对于英语在分词时存在系统性地代币数量膨胀，比例范围从1.6倍到近4倍不等，这是一种可测量的基础设施偏差。这种过度分割机械地增加了推断成本，限制了对上下文空间的访问，并改变了模型表示中的注意力权重。此外，文章还识别了因果偏差、通过维度坍缩导致的少数特征擦除以及由安全对齐引起的规范性偏差等结构机制。

Conclusion: 偏差是一种基础设施现象。文章提出了一个模型技术诊所的框架，认为对分词机制、潜在空间拓扑和对齐系统进行审计是批判性地利用AI基础设施的必要条件，以解决LLMs中深层的、由技术机制引起的偏差问题。

Abstract: This article introduces the concept of the algorithmic unconscious to designate the set of structural determinations that operate within large language models (LLMs) without being accessible either to the model's own reflexivity or to that of its users. In contrast to approaches that reduce AI bias solely to dataset composition or to the projection of human intentionality, we argue that a significant class of biases emerges directly from the technical mechanisms of the models themselves: tokenization, attention, statistical optimization, and alignment procedures. By framing bias as an infrastructural phenomenon, this approach resolves a central theoretical ambiguity surrounding responsibility, neutrality, and correction in contemporary LLMs. Based on a comparative analysis of tokenization across a corpus of parallel sentences, we show that Arabic languages (Modern Standard Arabic and Maghrebi dialects) undergo a systematic inflation in token count relative to English, with ratios ranging from 1.6x to nearly 4x depending on the infrastructure (OpenAI, Anthropic, SentencePiece/Mistral). This over-segmentation constitutes a measurable infrastructural bias that mechanically increases inference costs, constrains access to contextual space, and alters attentional weighting within model representations. We relate these empirical findings to three additional structural mechanisms: causal bias (correlation vs causation), the erasure of minoritized features through dimensional collapse, and normative biases induced by safety alignment. Finally, we propose a framework for a technical clinic of models, grounded in the audit of tokenization regimes, latent space topology, and alignment systems, as a necessary condition for the critical appropriation of AI infrastructures.

</details>


### [181] [Red Teaming LLMs as Socio-Technical Practice: From Exploration and Data Creation to Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18483)
*Adriana Alvarado Garcia,Ruyuan Wan,Ozioma C. Oguine,Karla Badillo-Urquiola*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过访谈22位设计和评估红队数据集的从业者，探讨了生成式AI红队数据实践和社会技术规范，指出从业者在风险概念化中常忽视语境、交互类型和用户特定性，并为HCI研究者提出了扩展红队概念化和数据实践的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队研究侧重技术基准和攻击成功率，忽视了红队数据集定义、创建和评估的社会技术实践。

Method: 通过对22位设计和评估红队数据集的从业者进行访谈。

Result: 提供了从业者对红队概念化及其红队数据集开发和评估的实证证据；反思了从业者对风险的概念化如何导致忽视语境、交互类型和用户特定性。

Conclusion: 为HCI研究人员提出了三个扩展红队概念化和数据实践的机会。

Abstract: Recently, red teaming, with roots in security, has become a key evaluative approach to ensure the safety and reliability of Generative Artificial Intelligence. However, most existing work emphasizes technical benchmarks and attack success rates, leaving the socio-technical practices of how red teaming datasets are defined, created, and evaluated under-examined. Drawing on 22 interviews with practitioners who design and evaluate red teaming datasets, we examine the data practices and standards that underpin this work. Because adversarial datasets determine the scope and accuracy of model evaluations, they are critical artifacts for assessing potential harms from large language models. Our contributions are first, empirical evidence of practitioners conceptualizing red teaming and developing and evaluating red teaming datasets. Second, we reflect on how practitioners' conceptualization of risk leads to overlooking the context, interaction type, and user specificity. We conclude with three opportunities for HCI researchers to expand the conceptualization and data practices for red-teaming.

</details>


### [182] [Developing a Multi-Agent System to Generate Next Generation Science Assessments with Evidence-Centered Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18451)
*Yaxuan Yang,Jongchan Park,Yifan Zhou,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究提出将以证据为中心的评估设计（ECD）框架整合到多智能体系统（MAS）中，利用大型语言模型自动生成符合NGSS标准的评估项目。结果显示，AI生成的项目在质量上与人工开发的项目总体相当，在包容性方面表现出独特优势，但在清晰度、简洁性和多模态设计方面存在局限性，并且AI和人工开发的项目在证据可收集性和学生兴趣一致性方面均有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学教育改革要求评估学生运用科学知识解决问题和设计方案的能力，但这需要开发高性能评估，而现有解决方案如证据中心设计（ECD）因需要多领域专业知识而成本高昂且劳动密集。

Method: 本研究将ECD框架整合到多智能体系统（MAS）中，利用集成多个具有不同专业知识的大型语言模型的MAS系统，自动生成符合NGSS标准的评估项目。通过比较AI生成项目与人工开发项目在多个评估设计维度上的质量来验证其有效性。

Result: AI生成的项目在符合NGSS三维标准和认知需求方面，总体质量与人工开发的项目相当。AI生成的项目在包容性方面表现出明显优势，但在清晰度、简洁性和多模态设计方面存在局限性。AI和人工开发的项目在证据可收集性和学生兴趣一致性方面均显示出弱点。

Conclusion: 将ECD整合到MAS中可以支持可扩展且符合标准的评估设计，但人类专业知识仍然至关重要。

Abstract: Contemporary science education reforms such as the Next Generation Science Standards (NGSS) demand assessments to understand students' ability to use science knowledge to solve problems and design solutions. To elicit such higher-order ability, educators need performance-based assessments, which are challenging to develop. One solution that has been broadly adopted is Evidence-Centered Design (ECD), which emphasizes interconnected models of the learner, evidence, and tasks. Although ECD provides a framework to safeguard assessment validity, its implementation requires diverse expertise (e.g., content and assessment), which is both costly and labor-intensive. To address this challenge, this study proposed integrating the ECD framework into Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) to generate NGSS-aligned assessment items automatically. This integrated MAS system ensembles multiple large language models with varying expertise, enabling the automation of complex, multi-stage item generation workflows traditionally performed by human experts. We examined the quality of AI-generated NGSS-aligned items and compared them with human-developed items across multiple dimensions of assessment design. Results showed that AI-generated items have overall comparable quality to human-developed items in terms of alignment with NGSS three-dimensional standards and cognitive demands. Divergent patterns also emerged: AI-generated items demonstrated a distinct strength in inclusivity, while also exhibiting limitations in clarity, conciseness, and multimodal design. AI- and human-developed items both showed weaknesses in evidence collectability and student interest alignment. These findings suggest that integrating ECD into MAS can support scalable and standards-aligned assessment design, while human expertise remains essential.

</details>


### [183] [LLM-Assisted Replication for Quantitative Social Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18453)
*So Kubota,Hiromu Yakura,Samuel Coavoux,Sho Yamada,Yuki Nakamura*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个基于大型语言模型（LLM）的系统，用于复制社会科学论文中的统计分析并标记潜在问题，旨在应对复制危机并增强研究诚信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验研究中存在“复制危机”，即科学主张未能通过后续研究得到验证，这部分是由于激励问题。大型语言模型（LLM）加速了科学产出，但可能超越了验证能力，因此需要一个系统来解决这一问题。

Method: 开发了一个基于LLM的系统原型，该系统通过LLM驱动的文本解释、代码生成、执行和差异分析迭代工作，以复制社会科学论文中的统计分析。选择定量社会科学是因为其依赖标准统计模型、共享公共数据集和统一的报告格式。

Result: 该原型成功再现了一篇开创性社会学论文的关键结果，展示了其复制统计分析和标记潜在问题的能力。

Conclusion: 该LLM驱动的验证系统可作为辅助基础设施，应用于投稿前检查、同行评审支持和元科学审计等场景，从而加强研究诚信。

Abstract: The replication crisis, the failure of scientific claims to be validated by further research, is one of the most pressing issues for empirical research. This is partly an incentive problem: replication is costly and less well rewarded than original research. Large language models (LLMs) have accelerated scientific production by streamlining writing, coding, and reviewing, yet this acceleration risks outpacing verification. To address this, we present an LLM-based system that replicates statistical analyses from social science papers and flags potential problems. Quantitative social science is particularly well-suited to automation because it relies on standard statistical models, shared public datasets, and uniform reporting formats such as regression tables and summary statistics. We present a prototype that iterates LLM-based text interpretation, code generation, execution, and discrepancy analysis, demonstrating its capabilities by reproducing key results from a seminal sociology paper. We also outline application scenarios including pre-submission checks, peer-review support, and meta-scientific audits, positioning AI verification as assistive infrastructure that strengthens research integrity.

</details>


### [184] [Impact of AI Search Summaries on Website Traffic: Evidence from Google AI Overviews and Wikipedia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18455)
*Mehrzad Khosravi,Hema Yoganarasimhan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Google的AI Overview功能使维基百科的英文文章流量减少了约15%，文化类文章受影响最大，表明生成式答案功能可能替代信息发布商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索引擎越来越多地显示LLM生成的答案，引发了出版商（流量被蚕食）和平台（流量互补）之间的争议。本文旨在估算Google的AI Overview（AIO）对维基百科流量的因果影响。

Method: 利用AIO功能分阶段的地理推广和维基百科的多语言结构，采用双重差分设计。将暴露于AIO的英文维基百科文章与在观察期间未暴露于AIO的相同基础文章的其他语言版本（印地语、印度尼西亚语、日语和葡萄牙语）进行比较。

Result: 在161,382个匹配的文章-语言对中，AIO暴露使英文文章的每日流量减少了约15%。影响具有异质性：文化类文章的相对下降幅度最大，STEM类文章的下降幅度则小得多，这与短篇合成答案能满足信息意图时替代性更强的观点一致。

Conclusion: 搜索中的生成式答案功能能实质性地将用户注意力从信息发布商那里转移，这对内容变现、搜索平台设计和政策具有影响。

Abstract: Search engines increasingly display LLM-generated answers shown above organic links, shifting search from link lists to answer-first summaries. Publishers contend these summaries substitute for source pages and cannibalize traffic, while platforms argue they are complementary by directing users through included links. We estimate the causal impact of Google's AI Overview (AIO) on Wikipedia traffic by leveraging the feature's staggered geographic rollout and Wikipedia's multilingual structure. Using a difference-in-differences design, we compare English Wikipedia articles exposed to AIO to the same underlying articles in language editions (Hindi, Indonesian, Japanese, and Portuguese) that were not exposed to AIO during the observation period. Across 161,382 matched article-language pairs, AIO exposure reduces daily traffic to English articles by approximately 15%. Effects are heterogeneous: relative declines are largest for Culture articles and substantially smaller for STEM, consistent with stronger substitution when short synthesized answers satisfy informational intent. These findings provide early causal evidence that generative-answer features in search engines can materially reallocate attention away from informational publishers, with implications for content monetization, search platform design, and policy.

</details>


### [185] [From Bias Mitigation to Bias Negotiation: Governing Identity and Sociocultural Reasoning in Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18459)
*Zackary Okun Dunivin,Bingyi Han,John Bollenbocher*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了“偏见协商”的概念，旨在规范大型语言模型（LLMs）在使用身份信息进行社会判断时的伦理问题，超越了传统偏见缓解的局限。通过对聊天机器人的访谈，识别了协商策略和失败模式，并提出了一个用于系统性训练和评估的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLMs中身份相关危害的治理主要集中在偏见缓解上，即将身份视为可测量差异或有害关联的来源，但这忽略了身份在解释中可能存在的积极的、情境敏感的作用。因此，本文旨在解决“偏见协商”这一治理问题，即如何规范身份条件下的社会文化相关性、推断和判断。

Method: 本文通过对多个公开部署的聊天机器人进行半结构化访谈，实证探讨了偏见协商的可行性。为了支持有针对性的训练，引入了一个广泛而明确的框架，将偏见协商分解为协商动作空间（观察和评分内容）和互补的案例特征集（模型进行协商的内容），以实现系统性的测试套件设计和评估。

Result: 研究识别了聊天机器人协商身份的常见策略，包括对群体倾向进行概率性定性，以及在危害与价值之间进行平衡。同时也观察到模型在避免艰难权衡或不一致地应用原则时的失败模式。此外，本文提出了一个分解偏见协商的框架，包括“协商动作空间”和“案例特征”，以支持系统性的测试套件设计和评估。

Conclusion: 偏见协商对于实现公正至关重要，因为它需要对社会文化推理的积极作用进行识别，以纠正结构性不平等。同时，对于在异构文化背景下运行的系统而言，社会文化能力是核心模型功能所必需的。由于偏见协商是一种通过审议和互动表达的程序能力，仅凭静态基准无法验证，需要新的评估方法和本文提出的框架来支持有针对性的训练和评估。

Abstract: LLMs act in the social world by drawing upon shared cultural patterns to make social situations understandable and actionable. Because identity is often part of the inferential substrate of competent judgment, ethical alignment requires regulating when and how systems invoke identity. Yet the dominant governance regime for identity-related harm remains bias mitigation, which treats identity primarily as a source of measurable disparities or harmful associations to be detected and suppressed. This leaves underspecified a positive, context-sensitive role for identity in interpretation. We call this governance problem bias negotiation: the normative regulation of identity-conditioned judgments of sociocultural relevance, inference, and justification. Empirically, we probe the feasibility of bias negotiation through semi-structured interviews with multiple publicly deployed chatbots. We identify recurring repertoires for negotiating identity including probabilistic framing of group tendencies and harm-value balancing. We also observe failure modes in which models avoid hard tradeoffs or apply principles inconsistently. Bias negotiation matters for justice because a positive role for sociocultural reasoning is required to recognize and potentially remediate structural inequities. But it is equally implicated in core model functionality as sociocultural competence is needed for systems that operate across heterogeneous cultural contexts. Because bias negotiation is a procedural capability expressed through deliberation and interaction, it cannot be validated by static benchmarks alone. To support targeted training, we introduce a broad but explicit framework that decomposes bias negotiation into an action space of negotiation moves (what to observe and score) and a complementary set of case features (over which the model negotiates), enabling systematic test-suite design and evaluation.

</details>


### [186] [Can Multimodal LLMs See Science Instruction? Benchmarking Pedagogical Reasoning in K-12 Classroom Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18466)
*Yixuan Shen,Peng He,Honglu Liu,Yuyang Ji,Tingting Li,Tianlong Chen,Kaidi Xu,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: SciIBI是首个用于分析科学课堂话语的视频基准，通过评估LLM和多模态LLM，发现现有模型在区分教学实践方面存在局限性，并建议人机协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-12科学课堂的探究性质及其多模态复杂性使得自动化分析难以实现。现有课堂话语基准主要关注数学和文本转录，忽略了NGSS强调的视觉 H工件和基于模型的推理。

Method: 引入SciIBI，这是第一个用于分析科学课堂话语的视频基准，包含113个符合NGSS的剪辑，并标注了核心教学实践（CIP）和复杂性水平。评估了八个最先进的LLM和多模态LLM。

Result: 现有模型难以区分教学上相似的实践，表明CIP编码需要超越表面模式匹配的教学推理。增加视频输入带来的增益在不同架构上不一致。模型往往通过表面捷径而非真正的教学理解获得成功。

Conclusion: 科学课堂话语是多模态AI的一个具有挑战性的前沿领域。未来方向是人机协作，模型检索证据以加速专家评审而非取代。

Abstract: K-12 science classrooms are rich sites of inquiry where students coordinate phenomena, evidence, and explanatory models through discourse; yet, the multimodal complexity of these interactions has made automated analysis elusive. Existing benchmarks for classroom discourse focus primarily on mathematics and rely solely on transcripts, overlooking the visual artifacts and model-based reasoning emphasized by the Next Generation Science Standards (NGSS). We address this gap with SciIBI, the first video benchmark for analyzing science classroom discourse, featuring 113 NGSS-aligned clips annotated with Core Instructional Practices (CIP) and sophistication levels. By evaluating eight state-of-the-art LLMs and Multimodal LLMs, we reveal fundamental limitations: current models struggle to distinguish pedagogically similar practices, suggesting that CIP coding requires instructional reasoning beyond surface pattern matching. Furthermore, adding video input yields inconsistent gains across architectures. Crucially, our evidence-based evaluation reveals that models often succeed through surface shortcuts rather than genuine pedagogical understanding. These findings establish science classroom discourse as a challenging frontier for multimodal AI and point toward human-AI collaboration, where models retrieve evidence to accelerate expert review rather than replace it.

</details>


### [187] [Charting the Future of AI-supported Science Education: A Human-Centered Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18471)
*Xiaoming Zhai,Kent Crippen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 人工智能（AI）正在重塑科学教育；本章提出了一个人本框架（REP）以负责任地整合AI，重点关注教育目标、教学过程、学习材料、评估和成果，旨在实现伦理的AI支持学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨人工智能如何重塑科学教育，并提出一个以人为中心的框架，以负责任地整合人工智能。

Method: 借鉴国际合作和推进科学教育中的人工智能（AASE）委员会的见解，综合分析了教育目标、教学程序、学习材料、评估和成果这五个维度的发展。提出了负责任和道德原则（REP）框架。

Result: 人工智能为丰富探究、个性化学习和支持教师实践提供了变革性潜力，但前提是必须遵循负责任和道德原则（REP）。讨论包括重新定义科学素养以涵盖人工智能素养、教师和学习者在人工智能支持的课堂中不断演变的角色，以及设计保持真实性和完整性的自适应学习材料和评估。

Conclusion: 本章提出了一个愿景，即科学教育应培养学习者成为道德的探究者和负责任的公民，确保人工智能创新与人类尊严、公平和科学素养的更广泛目标相一致。

Abstract: This concluding chapter explores how artificial intelligence (AI) is reshaping the purposes, practices, and outcomes of science education, and proposes a human-centered framework for its responsible integration. Drawing on insights from international collaborations and the Advancing AI in Science Education (AASE) committee, the chapter synthesizes developments across five dimensions: educational goals, instructional procedures, learning materials, assessment, and outcomes. We argue that AI offers transformative potential to enrich inquiry, personalize learning, and support teacher practice, but only when guided by Responsible and Ethical Principles (REP). The REP framework, emphasizing fairness, transparency, privacy, accountability, and respect for human values, anchors our vision for AI-supported science education. Key discussions include the redefinition of scientific literacy to encompass AI literacy, the evolving roles of teachers and learners in AI-supported classrooms, and the design of adaptive learning materials and assessments that preserve authenticity and integrity. We highlight both opportunities and risks, stressing the need for critical engagement with AI to avoid reinforcing inequities or undermining human agency. Ultimately, this chapter advances a vision in which science education prepares learners to act as ethical investigators and responsible citizens, ensuring that AI innovation aligns with human dignity, equity, and the broader goals of scientific literacy.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [HistCAD: Geometrically Constrained Parametric History-based CAD Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19171)
*Xintong Dong,Chuanyang Li,Chuqi Han,Peng Zheng,Jiaxin Jing,Yanzhi Song,Zhouwang Yang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: HistCAD是一个新的大规模数据集，用于参数化计算机辅助设计（CAD）建模，它包含约束感知的建模序列和多模态对齐数据，旨在通过显式几何约束和细粒度功能语义来改进可编辑、符合约束的生成式CAD建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化计算机辅助设计（CAD）建模数据集缺乏显式几何约束和细粒度功能语义，限制了可编辑、符合约束的生成能力。

Method: 提出了HistCAD数据集，包含约束感知的建模序列、多视图渲染、STEP格式B-rep、原生参数化文件和文本注释五种对齐模态。开发了AM_HistCAD注解模块，用于从建模序列中提取几何和空间特征，并使用大型语言模型生成建模过程、几何结构和功能类型的补充注释。

Result: HistCAD的显式约束、扁平化序列格式和多类型注释提高了文本驱动CAD生成的鲁棒性、参数化可编辑性和准确性。数据集中包含的工业零件进一步支持复杂的现实世界设计场景。

Conclusion: HistCAD为推进可编辑、约束感知和语义丰富的生成式CAD建模提供了一个统一的基准。

Abstract: Parametric computer-aided design (CAD) modeling is fundamental to industrial design, but existing datasets often lack explicit geometric constraints and fine-grained functional semantics, limiting editable, constraint-compliant generation. We present HistCAD, a large-scale dataset featuring constraint-aware modeling sequences that compactly represent procedural operations while ensuring compatibility with native CAD software, encompassing five aligned modalities: modeling sequences, multi-view renderings, STEP-format B-reps, native parametric files, and textual annotations. We develop AM\(_\text{HistCAD}\), an annotation module that extracts geometric and spatial features from modeling sequences and uses a large language model to generate complementary annotations of the modeling process, geometric structure, and functional type. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that HistCAD's explicit constraints, flattened sequence format, and multi-type annotations improve robustness, parametric editability, and accuracy in text-driven CAD generation, while industrial parts included in HistCAD further support complex real-world design scenarios. HistCAD thus provides a unified benchmark for advancing editable, constraint-aware, and semantically enriched generative CAD modeling.

</details>


### [189] [Thin Plate Spline Surface Reconstruction via the Method of Matched Sections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19182)
*Igor Orynyak,Kirill Danylenko,Danylo Tavrov*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文进一步发展了匹配截面法（MMS），一个用于偏微分方程边界值问题的鲁棒数值框架，特别是针对曲面建模。该方法通过强制执行高达三阶导数的连续性，弥合了物理精度和几何连续性之间的差距。它在高质量曲面重建和融合方面表现出色，即使在复杂条件下也能生成能量最优的、平滑的曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该方法通过弥合物理精度和几何连续性之间的差距，解决了等几何分析和计算机图形学中的关键挑战。

Method: 匹配截面法（MMS）通过将域分解为沿其整个边界匹配的1D方向分量，固有地强制执行所有变分参数的连续性，包括二阶（曲率）和三阶（剪切）导数。

Result: 该方法在高质量曲面重建和融合方面展示了先进的能力，即使从复杂的边界条件或稀疏的内部点出发，也能始终生成能量最优的、平滑的曲面。

Conclusion: 本研究将MMS确立为一种强大的、物理信息驱动的几何工具，满足了严格数值分析和美学计算机辅助设计的双重需求。

Abstract: This paper further develops the Method of Matched Sections (MMS), a robust numerical framework for the solution of boundary value problems governed by partial differential equations, specifically for surface modeling. While originating in mechanics, the method addresses critical challenges in isogeometric analysis and computer graphics by bridging the gap between physical accuracy and geometric continuity. By decomposing the domain into an assembly of 1D directional components matched along their entire boundaries, the method inherently enforces the continuity of all variational parameters, including second-order (curvature) and third-order (shear) derivatives. We demonstrate the method's advanced capabilities in high-fidelity surface reconstruction and blending, showing that it consistently generates energetically optimal, fair surfaces even from complex boundary conditions or sparse internal points. By advancing the application of MMS, this research establishes it as a powerful, physics-informed geometric tool that satisfies the dual demands of rigorous numerical analysis and aesthetic computer-aided design.

</details>


### [190] [Spherical Hermite Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20063)
*Mohamed Abouagour,Eleftherios Garyfallidis*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为球形Hermite图的导数增强型查找表（LUT）表示，通过存储函数值和偏导数，仅用四次纹理采样就能实现高质量的双三次Hermite重建，同时免费提供连续梯度，解决了现有方法的性能与质量权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面函数在计算机图形学中广泛应用，但现有方法在效率和质量之间存在权衡：双线性采样速度快但质量低，双三次滤波质量高但需要大量采样。此外，法线计算通常需要额外的采样并引入噪声。

Method: 提出球形Hermite图，这是一种导数增强型查找表表示。通过在填充立方体的每个纹素中存储函数值和缩放后的偏导数，仅需四次纹理采样（2x2足迹）即可实现双三次Hermite重建，并从相同采样中提供连续梯度。核心思想是Hermite插值在值重建的同时附带重建了平滑导数，使得表面法线几乎“免费”获得。

Result: 在受控实验中，球形Hermite图的PSNR比双线性插值提高了8-41 dB，并以四分之一的成本达到16次采样的双三次质量。解析法线使复杂表面的平均角度误差减少了9-13%，同时产生稳定的镜面高光。

Conclusion: 球形Hermite图提供了一种高效且高质量的球面函数评估方法，通过其导数增强的特性，在多个图形应用（如球面谐波符号可视化、径向深度图冒名顶替物和程序化行星渲染）中展现出强大的通用性。

Abstract: Spherical functions appear throughout computer graphics, from spherical harmonic lighting and precomputed radiance transfer to neural radiance fields and procedural planet rendering. Efficient evaluation is critical for real-time applications, yet existing approaches face a quality-performance trade-off: bilinear LUT sampling is fast but produces faceting, while bicubic filtering requires 16 texture samples. Most implementations use finite differences for normals, requiring extra samples and introducing noise. This paper presents Spherical Hermite Maps, a derivative-augmented LUT representation that resolves this trade-off. By storing function values alongside scaled partial derivatives at each texel of a padded cubemap, bicubic-Hermite reconstruction is enabled from only four texture samples (a 2x2 footprint) while providing continuous gradients from the same samples. The key insight is that Hermite interpolation reconstructs smooth derivatives as a byproduct of value reconstruction, making surface normals effectively free. In controlled experiments, Spherical Hermite Maps improve PSNR by 8-41 dB over bilinear interpolation and match 16-tap bicubic quality at one-quarter the cost. Analytic normals reduce mean angular error by 9-13% on complex surfaces while yielding stable specular highlights. Three applications demonstrate versatility: spherical harmonic glyph visualization, radial depth-map impostors for mesh level-of-detail, and procedural planet/asteroid rendering with spherical heightfields.

</details>


<div id='cs.FL'></div>

# cs.FL [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [NILE: Formalizing Natural-Language Descriptions of Formal Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19743)
*Tristan Kneisel,Marko Schmellenkamp,Fabian Vehlken,Thomas Zeume*

Main category: cs.FL

TL;DR: 本论文探讨了如何将形式语言的自然语言描述与其形式表示进行比较，并解释语义差异。为此，引入了一种新的形式语言表示语言 Nile，旨在使 Nile 表达式能够反映自然语言描述的句法结构。实验表明，大型语言模型（LLMs）可以将自然语言描述高精度地翻译成等效且句法上接近的 Nile 表达式，从而能够算法化地提供不准确描述的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在教育场景中，学习者用自然语言描述形式语言（如有限状态自动机、正则表达式等），而教育支持系统需要判断描述是否准确，并提供不准确原因的解释。

Method: 引入了一种名为 Nile 的形式语言表示语言。Nile 语言的设计使其表达式能够镜像自然语言描述的句法结构，且足以覆盖多种形式语言。通过 LLM 将自然语言描述翻译成句法接近的 Nile 表达式，从而算法化地解释描述中的不准确之处。

Result: LLMs 能够高精度地将自然语言描述翻译成等效且句法接近的 Nile 表达式，从而可以算法化地为不正确的自然语言描述提供解释。同时发现，虽然自然语言描述也可以翻译成正则表达式（但不是上下文无关文法），但这些表达式通常在句法上不接近，因此不适合提供解释。

Conclusion: Nile 语言和 LLMs 的结合为教育场景中比较形式语言的自然语言描述与形式表示提供了一种有效方法，并能算法化地解释描述中的不准确性。

Abstract: This paper explores how natural-language descriptions of formal languages can be compared to their formal representations and how semantic differences can be explained. This is motivated from educational scenarios where learners describe a formal language (presented, e.g., by a finite state automaton, regular expression, pushdown automaton, context-free grammar or in set notation) in natural language, and an educational support system has to (1) judge whether the natural-language description accurately describes the formal language, and to (2) provide explanations why descriptions are not accurate. To address this question, we introduce a representation language for formal languages, Nile, which is designed so that Nile expressions can mirror the syntactic structure of natural-language descriptions of formal languages. Nile is sufficiently expressive to cover a broad variety of formal languages, including all regular languages and fragments of context-free languages typically used in educational contexts. Generating Nile expressions that are syntactically close to natural-language descriptions then allows to provide explanations for inaccuracies in the descriptions algorithmically. In experiments on an educational data set, we show that LLMs can translate natural-language descriptions into equivalent, syntactically close Nile expressions with high accuracy - allowing to algorithmically provide explanations for incorrect natural-language descriptions. Our experiments also show that while natural-language descriptions can also be translated into regular expressions (but not context-free grammars), the expressions are often not syntactically close and thus not suitable for providing explanations.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Nacrith: Neural Lossless Compression via Ensemble Context Modeling and High-Precision CDF Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19626)
*Roberto Tacconelli*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: Nacrith是一种无损压缩系统，结合了1.35亿参数的Transformer语言模型、在线预测器集合和32位算术编码器。它通过多项创新（如CDF精度升级、N-gram模型、自适应偏差头、基于置信度的LLM跳过、混合二进制格式、优化推理后端、并行压缩和KV缓存滑动窗口）显著提升了压缩效率和速度，在多个基准测试中超越了现有先进方法，且能在消费级GPU上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在通过结合大型语言模型改进无损压缩技术，特别是针对文本和任意二进制文件，同时解决LLM在压缩应用中的量化开销、速度和内存限制，并扩展其适用范围。

Method: Nacrith系统结合了1.35亿参数的Transformer语言模型（SmolLM2-135M）、轻量级在线预测器集合和32位算术编码器。其主要方法和创新包括：1) 将CDF精度从2^16提升至2^24，以减少量化开销。2) 引入令牌级别的N-gram模型进行快速局部预测。3) 使用自适应对数空间偏差头通过在线梯度下降校正文档级LLM错误。4) 实现基于置信度的LLM跳过机制以加速可预测令牌。5) 开发混合二进制格式（NC06），将神经压缩扩展到任意二进制文件。6) 优化推理后端，单令牌解码速度比PyTorch快约7倍。7) 支持多达8个工作线程的并行多GPU压缩。8) 采用原生KV缓存滑动窗口，将每滑动成本降低约37倍。该系统仅需约500 MB的GGUF权重和每个工作线程约1.2 GB的显存，可在消费级GPU上运行。

Result: 在Canterbury Corpus (152 KB)上，Nacrith实现了0.918 bpb的压缩率，表现优于gzip 3.1倍、bzip2 2.5倍、CMIX v21 44%和ts_zip 20%，并低于0阶、1阶和2阶字节级香农熵界限。在enwik8 (100 MB)上，Nacrith实现了0.9389 bpb (11.74%)的压缩率，超越ts_zip (~1.11 bpb) 15%，超越FineZip (1.024 bpb) 8%，尽管使用了小60倍且未经微调的模型。对模型训练截止日期后发布文档的域外评估显示，在未见文本上实现了0.723 bpb，证实性能提升并非记忆伪影。

Conclusion: Nacrith系统在文本和任意二进制文件的无损压缩方面展现了最先进的性能，实现了卓越的压缩率和效率。它在消费级硬件上运行，显著超越了现有方法，并在多个基准测试中设立了新的性能标杆。

Abstract: We present Nacrith, a lossless compression system that combines a 135M-parameter transformer language model (SmolLM2-135M) with an ensemble of lightweight online predictors and a 32-bit arithmetic coder. Beyond the base LLM-plus-arithmetic-coding paradigm, Nacrith introduces several contributions: (1) a CDF precision upgrade from 2^16 to 2^24 that eliminates ~75% of quantization overhead caused by minimum-probability floors in large vocabularies; (2) a token-level N-gram model for fast local predictions; (3) an adaptive log-space bias head correcting per-document LLM errors via online gradient descent; (4) confidence-based LLM skip for accelerating highly predictable tokens; (5) a hybrid binary format (NC06) extending neural compression to arbitrary binary files--to our knowledge a first among LLM-based compressors; (6) a inference backend achieving ~7x faster single-token decode than PyTorch; (7) parallel multi-GPU compression across up to 8 workers; and (8) native KV cache sliding window reducing per-slide cost by ~37x. The system requires only ~500 MB of GGUF weights and ~1.2 GB VRAM per worker, running on consumer GPUs. On (Canterbury Corpus, 152 KB), Nacrith achieves 0.918 bits per byte (bpb)--outperforming gzip by 3.1x, bzip2 by 2.5x, CMIX v21 by 44%, and ts_zip by 20%, while compressing below the 0th-, 1st-, and 2nd-order byte-level Shannon entropy bounds. On enwik8 (100 MB), Nacrith achieves 0.9389 bpb (11.74%), surpassing ts_zip (~1.11 bpb) by 15% and FineZip (1.024 bpb) by 8% despite using a 60x smaller model with no fine-tuning. An out-of-distribution evaluation on a document published after the model's training cutoff confirms these gains are not memorization artifacts, achieving 0.723 bpb on unseen text.

</details>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Vibe Coding on Trial: Operating Characteristics of Unanimous LLM Juries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18492)
*Muhammad Aziz Ullah,Abdul Serwadda*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 大型语言模型(LLMs)擅长编写代码，但缺少一种可靠的自动化方法来验证其生成的查询的安全性。本研究通过基准测试15个模型，并从前6个模型中构建一致委员会来审查MySQL文本到SQL任务。结果表明，小规模的强模型一致委员会可以显著减少错误接受，同时仍能通过大量良好查询，并且委员会组成至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs已能很好地生成代码初稿，但缺乏一种可靠的方式来判断模型生成的查询是否安全可接受，而无需人工审核。在“安全优先”的部署中，错误接受的成本高于错误拒绝。

Method: 研究首先通过执行验证协议，对82个MySQL文本到SQL任务上的15个开放模型进行了基准测试，以确定强模型。然后，从表现最佳的6个模型中，构建了大小为1到6的一致委员会。委员会在看到提示、模式和候选SQL后，仅当所有成员都认为其正确时才接受。研究测量了真阳性率、假阳性率和Youden J指数，并分析了每个生成器对应的委员会表现。

Result: 结果显示，单一模型判断器的表现参差不齐。由强模型组成的小型一致委员会可以有效减少错误接受，同时仍然能通过大量高质量查询。此外，委员会的精确组成对结果有显著影响。

Conclusion: LLM评审团，特别是由强模型组成的小型一致委员会，可以作为一种有效的方法，在“安全优先”的部署场景中，通过减少错误接受来提高LLM生成代码（SQL查询）的可靠性和安全性。委员会中模型的选择是关键。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are now good enough at coding that developers can describe intent in plain language and let the tool produce the first code draft, a workflow increasingly built into tools like GitHub Copilot, Cursor, and Replit. What is missing is a reliable way to tell which model written queries are safe to accept without sending everything to a human. We study the application of an LLM jury to run this review step. We first benchmark 15 open models on 82 MySQL text to SQL tasks using an execution grounded protocol to get a clean baseline of which models are strong. From the six best models we build unanimous committees of sizes 1 through 6 that see the prompt, schema, and candidate SQL and accept it only when every member says it is correct. This rule matches safety first deployments where false accepts are more costly than false rejects. We measure true positive rate, false positive rate and Youden J and we also look at committees per generator. Our results show that single model judges are uneven, that small unanimous committees of strong models can cut false accepts while still passing many good queries, and that the exact committee composition matters significantly.

</details>


### [194] [RDBLearn: Simple In-Context Prediction Over Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18495)
*Yanlin Zhang,Linjie Xu,Quan Gan,David Wipf,Minjie Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: RDBLearn通过使用关系聚合对目标行进行特征化、具体化增强表并运行现成的表格基础模型，将表格上下文学习扩展到关系预测，在RelBench和4DBInfer数据集上实现了强大的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实世界的任务存在于关系数据库中，其中预测信号分布在多个链接表中，而现有的表格上下文学习方法是为单一平面表设计的。

Method: RDBLearn通过以下方式将表格ICL扩展到关系预测：1）使用关系聚合对目标行进行自动特征化，2）具体化生成的增强表，3）在其上运行现成的表格基础模型。RDBLearn还提供了一个易于使用的工具包，带有scikit-learn风格的估计器接口。

Result: 在RelBench和4DBInfer数据集的广泛集合中，RDBLearn是评估中表现最好的基础模型方法，有时甚至优于在每个数据集上训练或微调的强大有监督基线。

Conclusion: RDBLearn有效地将表格上下文学习扩展到关系预测任务，提供了一个简单而强大的方法，与其他基础模型方法相比，实现了卓越的性能，有时甚至优于强大的有监督基线。

Abstract: Recent advances in tabular in-context learning (ICL) show that a single pretrained model can adapt to new prediction tasks from a small set of labeled examples, avoiding per-task training and heavy tuning. However, many real-world tasks live in relational databases, where predictive signal is spread across multiple linked tables rather than a single flat table. We show that tabular ICL can be extended to relational prediction with a simple recipe: automatically featurize each target row using relational aggregations over its linked records, materialize the resulting augmented table, and run an off-the-shelf tabular foundation model on it. We package this approach in \textit{RDBLearn} ( ), an easy-to-use toolkit with a scikit-learn-style estimator interface that makes it straightforward to swap different tabular ICL backends; a complementary agent-specific interface is provided as well. Across a broad collection of RelBench and 4DBInfer datasets, RDBLearn is the best-performing foundation model approach we evaluate, at times even outperforming strong supervised baselines trained or fine-tuned on each dataset.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [On the Dynamics of Observation and Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18494)
*Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为智能并非被动反映现实，而是受限于有限物理资源的智能体与高熵环境互动的结果。通过观测语义纤维丛和Landauer原理，文章推导出存在信息处理的“语义常数B”，并在此物理约束下，符号结构是建模组合世界的必然选择，语言和逻辑是防止热力学崩溃的本体论必需品。理解被定义为构建使世界可压缩和可预测的因果商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉智能范式将语义视为潜在表示的静态属性，认为意义可以通过高维嵌入空间中的几何接近来发现。本文认为这种观点在物理上是不完整的。

Method: 作者提出智能是受限于有限记忆、计算和能量的物理可实现智能体与高熵环境交互的属性。他们通过“观测语义纤维丛”的运动学结构来形式化这种交互，其中原始感觉观测数据（纤维）被投射到一个低熵因果语义流形（基）上。此外，他们应用信息处理的热力学成本（Landauer原理）来证明内部状态转换复杂度的严格限制。

Result: 论文证明对于任何有界智能体，Landauer原理对内部状态转换的复杂性施加了严格限制，并将其命名为“语义常数B”。从这些物理约束中，他们推导出了符号结构的必要性。他们表明，为了在常数B的限制内建模组合世界，语义流形必须经历相变，结晶为离散的、组合的和分解的形式。因此，语言和逻辑是本体论的必需品，而非文化产物。

Conclusion: 理解不是恢复隐藏的潜在变量，而是构建一个因果商，使世界在算法上可压缩和因果上可预测。语言和逻辑是防止热力学崩溃所必需的信息“固态”。

Abstract: A dominant paradigm in visual intelligence treats semantics as a static property of latent representations, assuming that meaning can be discovered through geometric proximity in high dimensional embedding spaces. In this work, we argue that this view is physically incomplete. We propose that intelligence is not a passive mirror of reality but a property of a physically realizable agent, a system bounded by finite memory, finite compute, and finite energy interacting with a high entropy environment. We formalize this interaction through the kinematic structure of an Observation Semantics Fiber Bundle, where raw sensory observation data (the fiber) is projected onto a low entropy causal semantic manifold (the base). We prove that for any bounded agent, the thermodynamic cost of information processing (Landauer's Principle) imposes a strict limit on the complexity of internal state transitions. We term this limit the Semantic Constant B. From these physical constraints, we derive the necessity of symbolic structure. We show that to model a combinatorial world within the bound B, the semantic manifold must undergo a phase transition, it must crystallize into a discrete, compositional, and factorized form. Thus, language and logic are not cultural artifacts but ontological necessities the solid state of information required to prevent thermal collapse. We conclude that understanding is not the recovery of a hidden latent variable, but the construction of a causal quotient that renders the world algorithmically compressible and causally predictable.

</details>


### [196] [Feedback-based Automated Verification in Vibe Coding of CAS Adaptation Built on Constraint Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18607)
*Michal Töpfer,František Plášil,Tomáš Bureš,Petr Hnětynka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In CAS adaptation, a challenge is to define the dynamic architecture of the system and changes in its behavior. Implementation-wise, this is projected into an adaptation mechanism, typically realized as an Adaptation Manager (AM). With the advances of generative LLMs, generating AM code based on system specification and desired AM behavior (partially in natural language) is a tempting opportunity. The recent introduction of vibe coding suggests a way to target the problem of the correctness of generated code by iterative testing and vibe coding feedback loops instead of direct code inspection. In this paper, we show that generating an AM via vibe coding feedback loops is a viable option when the verification of the generated AM is based on a very precise formulation of the functional requirements. We specify these as constraints in a novel temporal logic FCL that allows us to express the behavior of traces with much finer granularity than classical LTL enables. Furthermore, we show that by combining the adaptation and vibe coding feedback loops where the FCL constraints are evaluated for the current system state, we achieved good results in the experiments with generating AMs for two example systems from the CAS domain. Typically, just a few feedback loop iterations were necessary, each feeding the LLM with reports describing detailed violations of the constraints. This AM testing was combined with high run path coverage achieved by different initial settings.

</details>


### [197] [Decoding ML Decision: An Agentic Reasoning Framework for Large-Scale Ranking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18640)
*Longfei Yun,Yihan Wu,Haoran Liu,Xiaoxuan Liu,Ziyun Xu,Yi Wang,Yang Xia,Pengfei Wang,Mingze Gao,Yunxiang Wang,Changfan Chen,Junfeng Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Modern large-scale ranking systems operate within a sophisticated landscape of competing objectives, operational constraints, and evolving product requirements. Progress in this domain is increasingly bottlenecked by the engineering context constraint: the arduous process of translating ambiguous product intent into reasonable, executable, verifiable hypotheses, rather than by modeling techniques alone. We present GEARS (Generative Engine for Agentic Ranking Systems), a framework that reframes ranking optimization as an autonomous discovery process within a programmable experimentation environment. Rather than treating optimization as static model selection, GEARS leverages Specialized Agent Skills to encapsulate ranking expert knowledge into reusable reasoning capabilities, enabling operators to steer systems via high-level intent vibe personalization. Furthermore, to ensure production reliability, the framework incorporates validation hooks to enforce statistical robustness and filter out brittle policies that overfit short-term signals. Experimental validation across diverse product surfaces demonstrates that GEARS consistently identifies superior, near-Pareto-efficient policies by synergizing algorithmic signals with deep ranking context while maintaining rigorous deployment stability.

</details>


### [198] [Spilled Energy in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18671)
*Adrian Robert Minut,Hazem Dewidar,Iacopo Masi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将大型语言模型（LLM）的最终softmax分类器重新解释为基于能量的模型（EBM），在推理过程中将序列到序列的概率链分解为多个相互作用的EBM。这种无训练的方法通过追踪“能量溢出”来检测LLM中的幻觉，这些能量溢出与事实错误、偏差和失败相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型中事实错误、偏见和失败（幻觉）的问题，并提供一种无需训练的检测方法。

Method: 将LLM的最终softmax分类器重新解释为基于能量的模型（EBM），并在推理时将序列到序列的概率链分解为多个相互作用的EBM。通过跟踪“能量溢出”来识别幻觉，并引入了两个无需训练的指标：溢出能量（spilled energy）和边缘化能量（marginalized energy），它们直接从输出logits导出，用于在不依赖训练探针分类器或激活消融的情况下，定位答案token并检测幻觉。

Result: 该方法在九个基准测试中，对最先进的LLM（包括LLaMA、Mistral和Gemma）以及合成代数操作（Qwen3）进行了评估，结果表明其在幻觉检测和跨任务泛化方面表现出稳健且有竞争力的性能。这些结果适用于预训练和指令调优的变体，且无需引入任何训练开销。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种新颖的、无需训练的方法，通过将LLM的softmax重新解释为EBM来检测幻觉，并在多个LLM和任务上展示了其稳健的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: We reinterpret the final Large Language Model (LLM) softmax classifier as an Energy-Based Model (EBM), decomposing the sequence-to-sequence probability chain into multiple interacting EBMs at inference. This principled approach allows us to track "energy spills" during decoding, which we empirically show correlate with factual errors, biases, and failures. Similar to Orgad et al. (2025), our method localizes the exact answer token and subsequently tests for hallucinations. Crucially, however, we achieve this without requiring trained probe classifiers or activation ablations. Instead, we introduce two completely training-free metrics derived directly from output logits: spilled energy, which captures the discrepancy between energy values across consecutive generation steps that should theoretically match, and marginalized energy, which is measurable at a single step. Evaluated on nine benchmarks across state-of-the-art LLMs (including LLaMA, Mistral, and Gemma) and on synthetic algebraic operations (Qwen3), our approach demonstrates robust, competitive hallucination detection and cross-task generalization. Notably, these results hold for both pretrained and instruction-tuned variants without introducing any training overhead.

</details>


### [199] [Many AI Analysts, One Dataset: Navigating the Agentic Data Science Multiverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18710)
*Martin Bertran,Riccardo Fogliato,Zhiwei Steven Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于大型语言模型（LLM）的AI分析师能够廉价且大规模地重现“多分析师”研究中观察到的分析多样性，结果显示效应大小、p值和假设支持判断存在广泛差异，且这些影响是可控的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究的结论不仅依赖于数据，还依赖于一系列分析决策，而这些决策在已发表的结果中很少明确说明。以往的“多分析师”研究表明，独立的团队在相同数据集上检验同一假设时，经常得出相互冲突的结论。但此类研究需要数月协调，因此很少进行。本文旨在证明基于LLM的完全自主AI分析师能够廉价且大规模地重现类似的结构化分析多样性。

Method: 我们让AI分析师（基于LLM）在固定数据集上测试一个预设假设，并在重复运行中改变底层模型和提示框架。每个AI分析师独立构建并执行完整的分析流程；然后由AI审计师筛选每个运行的统计方法有效性。实验在涵盖实验和观察设计的三个数据集上进行。

Result: AI分析师生成的分析在效应大小、p值以及是否支持假设的二元决策上显示出广泛的分散性，经常导致对假设是否得到支持的判断反转。这种分散性是有结构的：预处理、模型规范和推断中可识别的分析选择在不同的LLM和角色条件下系统性地存在差异。关键在于，这些效应是可控的：即使在排除了方法上有缺陷的运行后，重新分配分析师角色或LLM也会改变结果的分布。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的AI分析师能够重现结构化的分析多样性，揭示了分析选择的影响。这提供了一种更廉价、可扩展的方法来研究分析多样性，并且这种多样性是可控的。

Abstract: The conclusions of empirical research depend not only on data but on a sequence of analytic decisions that published results seldom make explicit. Past ``many-analyst" studies have demonstrated this: independent teams testing the same hypothesis on the same dataset regularly reach conflicting conclusions. But such studies require months of coordination among dozens of research groups and are therefore rarely conducted. In this work, we show that fully autonomous AI analysts built on large language models (LLMs) can reproduce a similar structured analytic diversity cheaply and at scale. We task these AI analysts with testing a pre-specified hypothesis on a fixed dataset, varying the underlying model and prompt framing across replicate runs. Each AI analyst independently constructs and executes a full analysis pipeline; an AI auditor then screens each run for methodological validity. Across three datasets spanning experimental and observational designs, AI analyst-produced analyses display wide dispersion in effect sizes, $p$-values, and binary decisions on supporting the hypothesis or not, frequently reversing whether a hypothesis is judged supported. This dispersion is structured: recognizable analytic choices in preprocessing, model specification, and inference differ systematically across LLM and persona conditions. Critically, the effects are \emph{steerable}: reassigning the analyst persona or LLM shifts the distribution of outcomes even after excluding methodologically deficient runs.

</details>


### [200] [Task-Aware Exploration via a Predictive Bisimulation Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18724)
*Dayang Liang,Ruihan Liu,Lipeng Wan,Yunlong Liu,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TEB是一种任务感知探索方法，通过预测性二态性度量，将任务相关表示与探索紧密结合，以加速稀疏奖励下视觉强化学习的探索，实现卓越的探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏奖励的视觉强化学习中，由于大量的任务无关变异，加速探索仍然具有挑战性。尽管内禀探索取得了进展，但许多方法要么假设可访问低维状态，要么缺乏任务感知的探索策略，这使得它们在视觉领域中表现脆弱。

Method: TEB通过预测性二态性度量，将任务相关表示与探索紧密耦合。它利用该度量不仅学习行为基础的任务表示，还在学习到的潜在空间上衡量行为的内在新颖性。为实现这一点，TEB首先通过内部引入一个简单但有效的预测奖励微分，从理论上缓解了稀疏奖励下退化二态性度量的表示崩溃问题。在此稳健度量基础上，TEB设计了基于势的探索奖励，用于测量潜在空间中相邻观测的相对新颖性。

Result: 在MetaWorld和Maze2D上的大量实验表明，TEB实现了卓越的探索能力，并优于最近的基线方法。

Conclusion: TEB通过其预测性二态性度量和任务感知探索策略，有效解决了视觉强化学习中稀疏奖励和任务无关变异的挑战，从而实现了卓越的探索能力。

Abstract: Accelerating exploration in visual reinforcement learning under sparse rewards remains challenging due to the substantial task-irrelevant variations. Despite advances in intrinsic exploration, many methods either assume access to low-dimensional states or lack task-aware exploration strategies, thereby rendering them fragile in visual domains. To bridge this gap, we present TEB, a Task-aware Exploration approach that tightly couples task-relevant representations with exploration through a predictive Bisimulation metric. Specifically, TEB leverages the metric not only to learn behaviorally grounded task representations but also to measure behaviorally intrinsic novelty over the learned latent space. To realize this, we first theoretically mitigate the representation collapse of degenerate bisimulation metrics under sparse rewards by internally introducing a simple but effective predicted reward differential. Building on this robust metric, we design potential-based exploration bonuses, which measure the relative novelty of adjacent observations over the latent space. Extensive experiments on MetaWorld and Maze2D show that TEB achieves superior exploration ability and outperforms recent baselines.

</details>


### [201] [Federated Reasoning Distillation Framework with Model Learnability-Aware Data Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18749)
*Wei Guo,Siyuan Lu,Xiangdong Ran,Yiqi Tong,Yikun Ban,Zelong Xu,Jing Fan,Zixuan Huang,Xiao Zhang,Zhaojun Hu,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出LaDa，一个联邦推理蒸馏框架，具有模型可学习性感知数据分配功能，以解决联邦大型语言模型（LLM）和小型语言模型（SLM）协作中的双向模型可学习性差距和领域无关推理迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦大型语言模型（LLM）和小型语言模型（SLM）推理协作中存在两个主要挑战：1. 双向模型可学习性差距：客户端SLM难以识别匹配其可学习性约束的高奖励样本以有效从LLM获取知识，而LLM难以选择现有数据之外提供新颖知识的样本。2. 领域无关推理迁移：现有推理迁移方法未能灵活适应本地领域数据，阻碍SLM有效从通用LLM获取逐步推理能力。

Method: 提出LaDa框架，这是一个具有模型可学习性感知数据分配的联邦推理蒸馏框架。该框架引入了一个模型可学习性感知数据过滤器，根据每个SLM和LLM对之间的可学习性差距自适应地分配高奖励样本，促进双向知识迁移。此外，设计了一种领域自适应推理蒸馏方法，通过SLM和LLM之间的对比蒸馏学习，对过滤后的高奖励样本上的推理路径的联合概率进行对齐，使SLM能够捕获本地数据分布下的底层推理模式。LaDa可作为现有协作框架的插件模块。

Result: 有效促进了双向知识迁移，并使SLM能够捕获本地数据分布下的底层推理模式。

Conclusion: LaDa作为一个插件模块，通过根据模型可学习性差距调整知识迁移，解决了联邦LLM-SLM协作中的数据分配挑战。

Abstract: Data allocation plays a critical role in federated large language model (LLM) and small language models (SLMs) reasoning collaboration. Nevertheless, existing data allocation methods fail to address an under-explored challenge in collaboration: bidirectional model learnability gap, where client-side SLMs cannot identify high-reward samples matching their learnability constraints for effective knowledge transfer from LLMs, while LLMs struggle to select samples contributing novel knowledge beyond their existing data. Furthermore, these collaboration frameworks face another key challenge: domain-agnostic reasoning transfer, where existing reasoning transfer methods fail to flexibly adapt to the local domain data, preventing SLMs from effectively acquiring step-by-step reasoning abilities within from general LLM. To address these challenges, we propose LaDa, a federated reasoning distillation framework with model learnability-aware data allocation. It introduces a model learnability-aware data filter that adaptively allocates high-reward samples based on the learnability gap between each SLM and LLM pair, effectively facilitating bidirectional knowledge transfer. We further design a domain adaptive reasoning distillation method that aligns joint probabilities of reasoning paths on filtered high-reward samples through contrastive distillation learning between SLM and LLM, enabling SLM to capture underlying reasoning patterns under local data distribution. LaDa operates as a plug-in module for existing collaboration frameworks, adapting knowledge transfer based on model learnability gaps.

</details>


### [202] [GenPlanner: From Noise to Plans -- Emergent Reasoning in Flow Matching and Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18812)
*Agnieszka Polowczyk,Alicja Polowczyk,Michał Wieczorek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GenPlanner（基于扩散模型和流匹配），用于复杂环境下的路径规划。该方法能够迭代生成正确路径，并在实验中显著优于基线CNN模型，其中FlowPlanner在有限生成步数下表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境下的路径规划是人工智能的关键问题之一，因为它需要同时理解空间几何和问题全局结构。

Method: 提出GenPlanner方法，基于扩散模型和流匹配，并有两个变体：DiffPlanner和FlowPlanner。该方法利用描述环境结构的多通道条件（包括障碍物图和起点终点信息）来调节轨迹生成。与标准方法不同，模型从随机噪声开始迭代生成轨迹，并逐渐将其转化为正确解决方案。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的方法显著优于基线CNN模型。特别是，FlowPlanner即使在有限的生成步数下也表现出高性能。

Conclusion: 生成模型，特别是GenPlanner，在复杂环境的路径规划中作为规划和推理机制具有潜力，并优于传统方法。

Abstract: Path planning in complex environments is one of the key problems of artificial intelligence because it requires simultaneous understanding of the geometry of space and the global structure of the problem. In this paper, we explore the potential of using generative models as planning and reasoning mechanisms. We propose GenPlanner, an approach based on diffusion models and flow matching, along with two variants: DiffPlanner and FlowPlanner. We demonstrate the application of generative models to find and generate correct paths in mazes. A multi-channel condition describing the structure of the environment, including an obstacle map and information about the starting and destination points, is used to condition trajectory generation. Unlike standard methods, our models generate trajectories iteratively, starting with random noise and gradually transforming it into a correct solution. Experiments conducted show that the proposed approach significantly outperforms the baseline CNN model. In particular, FlowPlanner demonstrates high performance even with a limited number of generation steps.

</details>


### [203] [Early Evidence of Vibe-Proving with Consumer LLMs: A Case Study on Spectral Region Characterization with ChatGPT-5.2 (Thinking)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18918)
*Brecht Verbeken,Brando Vagenende,Marie-Anne Guerry,Andres Algaba,Vincent Ginis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提供了一个案例研究，展示了大型语言模型（LLMs）如何协助解决研究级数学中的猜想，通过迭代的生成、审阅和修复流程，发现LLMs在高级证明搜索中很有用，但人类专家对于正确性验证仍然至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前关于大型语言模型（LLMs）在研究级数学中的作用，特别是对个体研究人员可及的工作流程的证据有限。

Method: 通过一个可审计的案例研究，使用消费者订阅LLM（ChatGPT-5.2）来解决Ran和Teng（2024）提出的关于4-循环行随机非负矩阵族精确非实谱区域的猜想20。分析了七个可共享的ChatGPT-5.2线程和四个版本化的证明草稿，记录了一个迭代的“生成、审阅和修复”流程。

Result: 模型在高级证明搜索中最有用，而人类专家在正确性关键的闭合方面仍然不可或缺。最终定理提供了必要的和充分的区域条件以及明确的边界实现构造。

Conclusion: 除了数学结果之外，论文还对LLM协助在何处提供实质性帮助以及验证瓶颈持续存在的地方进行了过程级描述，这对评估AI辅助研究工作流程和设计人机循环定理证明系统具有启示意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as scientific copilots, but evidence on their role in research-level mathematics remains limited, especially for workflows accessible to individual researchers. We present early evidence for vibe-proving with a consumer subscription LLM through an auditable case study that resolves Conjecture 20 of Ran and Teng (2024) on the exact nonreal spectral region of a 4-cycle row-stochastic nonnegative matrix family. We analyze seven shareable ChatGPT-5.2 (Thinking) threads and four versioned proof drafts, documenting an iterative pipeline of generate, referee, and repair. The model is most useful for high-level proof search, while human experts remain essential for correctness-critical closure. The final theorem provides necessary and sufficient region conditions and explicit boundary attainment constructions. Beyond the mathematical result, we contribute a process-level characterization of where LLM assistance materially helps and where verification bottlenecks persist, with implications for evaluation of AI-assisted research workflows and for designing human-in-the-loop theorem proving systems.

</details>


### [204] [INDUCTION: Finite-Structure Concept Synthesis in First-Order Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18956)
*Serafim Batzoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文引入了INDUPTION，这是一个用于一阶逻辑中有限结构概念合成的基准测试，模型需要输出一个一阶逻辑公式来解释跨世界的扩展标记目标谓词，并通过精确模型检查验证正确性，同时惩罚公式的冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估模型在一阶逻辑中进行有限结构概念合成的能力，并理解公式冗余度对泛化性能的影响。

Method: 引入了INDUPTION基准，包含FullObs、CI和EC三种模式。模型输入是带有扩展标记目标谓词的有限关系世界，输出是一个单一的一阶逻辑公式。通过精确模型检查验证正确性，并对公式冗余度进行惩罚。

Result: 发现了明显的难度梯度、持续存在的困难结构族，并观察到低冗余公式在保留世界上的泛化能力更好。领先的最新模型在不同任务和性能指标上表现出不同的定性行为，暗示了它们在概念泛化方面的不同策略。

Conclusion: INDUPTION基准揭示了有限结构概念合成的挑战，强调了公式简洁性对泛化的重要性，并展示了当前最先进模型之间不同的泛化策略。

Abstract: We introduce INDUCTION, a benchmark for finite structure concept synthesis in first order logic. Given small finite relational worlds with extensionally labeled target predicates, models must output a single first order logical formula that explains the target uniformly across worlds, with correctness verified via exact model checking. The benchmark includes three regimes, FullObs, CI (contrastive), and EC (existential completion), nd penalizes formula bloat. We find sharp difficulty gradients, persistent hard structural families, and observe that low bloat formulas generalize far better on held out worlds. Elite recent models show qualitatively different behaviors across tasks and performance metrics, hinting to their different strategies of concept generalization.

</details>


### [205] [Modularity is the Bedrock of Natural and Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18960)
*Alessandro Salatiello*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The remarkable performance of modern AI systems has been driven by unprecedented scales of data, computation, and energy -- far exceeding the resources required by human intelligence. This disparity highlights the need for new guiding principles and motivates drawing inspiration from the fundamental organizational principles of brain computation. Among these principles, modularity has been shown to be critical for supporting the efficient learning and strong generalization abilities consistently exhibited by humans. Furthermore, modularity aligns well with the No Free Lunch Theorem, which highlights the need for problem-specific inductive biases and motivates architectures composed of specialized components that solve subproblems. However, despite its fundamental role in natural intelligence and its demonstrated benefits across a range of seemingly disparate AI subfields, modularity remains relatively underappreciated in mainstream AI research. In this work, we review several research threads in artificial intelligence and neuroscience through a conceptual framework that highlights the central role of modularity in supporting both artificial and natural intelligence. In particular, we examine what computational advantages modularity provides, how it has emerged as a solution across several AI research areas, which modularity principles the brain exploits, and how modularity can help bridge the gap between natural and artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [206] [Robust and Efficient Tool Orchestration via Layered Execution Structures with Reflective Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18968)
*Tao Zhe,Haoyu Wang,Bo Luo,Min Wu,Wei Fan,Xiao Luo,Zijun Yao,Haifeng Chen,Dongjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Tool invocation is a core capability of agentic systems, yet failures often arise not from individual tool calls but from how multiple tools are organized and executed together. Existing approaches tightly couple tool execution with stepwise language reasoning or explicit planning, leading to brittle behavior and high execution overhead. To overcome these limitations, we revisit tool invocation from the perspective of tool orchestration. Our key insight is that effective orchestration does not require precise dependency graphs or fine-grained planning. Instead, a coarse-grained layer structure suffices to provide global guidance, while execution-time errors can be corrected locally. Specifically, we model tool orchestration as learning a layered execution structure that captures high-level tool dependencies, inducing layer-wise execution through context constraints. To handle execution-time failures, we introduce a schema-aware reflective correction mechanism that detects and repairs errors locally. This design confines errors to individual tool calls and avoids re-planning entire execution trajectories. This structured execution paradigm enables a lightweight and reusable orchestration component for agentic systems. Experimental results show that our approach achieves robust tool execution while reducing execution complexity and overhead. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [207] [How Far Can We Go with Pixels Alone? A Pilot Study on Screen-Only Navigation in Commercial 3D ARPGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18981)
*Kaijie Xu,Mustafa Bugti,Clark Verbrugge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于屏幕的视觉导航智能体，用于复杂3D游戏关卡，它利用现有的视觉可供性检测器，通过识别兴趣点和有限状态控制器来探索线性关卡并尝试到达目标区域。初步实验表明该智能体可以有效导航，但底层视觉模型的局限性限制了其全面性和可靠性。本文认为该系统为复杂游戏中的视觉导航提供了一个具体的基线和评估协议，并指出纯视觉模型在理想设置中有效，但并非通用的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代3D游戏关卡的导航性难以量化，现有方法无法真实捕捉玩家在复杂真实游戏关卡中的探索方式，因为它们要么在简化环境中模拟游戏，要么分析静态截图的视觉可供性。

Method: 本文在一个现有的开源视觉可供性检测器基础上，构建了一个纯粹基于视觉可供性的屏幕探索和导航智能体。该智能体消耗实时游戏帧，识别显著的兴趣点，并通过一个简单的有限状态控制器在最小的行动空间上驱动，以探索《黑暗之魂》风格的线性关卡并尝试到达预期的目标区域。

Result: 初步实验表明，该智能体可以遍历大多数所需的游戏片段，并表现出有意义的视觉导航行为。但同时也突出表明，底层视觉模型的局限性阻碍了真正全面和可靠的自动导航。

Conclusion: 该系统为复杂游戏中的视觉导航提供了一个具体、共享的基线和评估协议。本文呼吁更多关注这一必要任务。结果表明，纯粹基于视觉的感知模型，在具有离散单模态输入且没有显式推理的情况下，可以在理想设置中有效支持导航和环境理解，但单独来看不太可能成为通用解决方案。

Abstract: Modern 3D game levels rely heavily on visual guidance, yet the navigability of level layouts remains difficult to quantify. Prior work either simulates play in simplified environments or analyzes static screenshots for visual affordances, but neither setting faithfully captures how players explore complex, real-world game levels. In this paper, we build on an existing open-source visual affordance detector and instantiate a screen-only exploration and navigation agent that operates purely from visual affordances. Our agent consumes live game frames, identifies salient interest points, and drives a simple finite-state controller over a minimal action space to explore Dark Souls-style linear levels and attempt to reach expected goal regions. Pilot experiments show that the agent can traverse most required segments and exhibits meaningful visual navigation behavior, but also highlight that limitations of the underlying visual model prevent truly comprehensive and reliable auto-navigation. We argue that this system provides a concrete, shared baseline and evaluation protocol for visual navigation in complex games, and we call for more attention to this necessary task. Our results suggest that purely vision-based sense-making models, with discrete single-modality inputs and without explicit reasoning, can effectively support navigation and environment understanding in idealized settings, but are unlikely to be a general solution on their own.

</details>


### [208] [MagicAgent: Towards Generalized Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19000)
*Xuhui Ren,Shaokang Dong,Chen Yang,Qing Gao,Yunbin Zhao,Yongsheng Liu,Xinwei Geng,Xiang Li,Demei Yan,Yanqing Li,Chenhao Huang,Dingwei Zhu,Junjie Ye,Boxuan Yue,Yingnan Fu,Mengzhe Lv,Zezeng Feng,Boshen Zhou,Bocheng Wang,Xuanjing Huang,Yu-Gang Jiang,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Yunke Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MagicAgent是一个用于通用智能体规划的基础模型系列，通过轻量级合成数据框架和两阶段训练范式，解决了大型语言模型在通用规划中数据稀缺和任务冲突的挑战，并在多项基准测试中取得了优异的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在通用规划方面面临挑战，主要原因是高质量交互数据的稀缺以及异构规划任务之间固有的冲突。这些挑战导致模型在孤立任务上表现出色，但在泛化方面表现不佳，而现有的多任务训练尝试则存在梯度干扰问题。

Method: 本研究提出了MagicAgent，一个专为通用智能体规划设计的系列基础模型。方法包括：1. 引入一个轻量级且可扩展的合成数据框架，用于在分层任务分解、工具增强规划、多约束调度、程序逻辑编排和长周期工具执行等多样化规划任务中生成高质量轨迹。2. 提出一个两阶段训练范式，包括监督微调和针对静态数据集及动态环境的多目标强化学习，以缓解训练冲突。

Result: MagicAgent-32B和MagicAgent-30B-A3B在多项基准测试中表现出卓越性能，其中Worfbench准确率达到75.1%，NaturalPlan达到55.9%，$	au^2$-Bench达到57.5%，BFCL-v3达到86.9%，ACEBench达到81.2%，并在内部MagicEval基准测试中也取得了显著成果。这些结果显著优于现有100B以下模型，甚至超越了领先的闭源模型。

Conclusion: MagicAgent通过创新的合成数据生成和两阶段训练范式，有效解决了大型语言模型在实现通用规划方面遇到的数据和训练冲突问题，显著提升了规划能力和泛化性能，为通用智能体规划领域树立了新的基准。

Abstract: The evolution of Large Language Models (LLMs) from passive text processors to autonomous agents has established planning as a core component of modern intelligence. However, achieving generalized planning remains elusive, not only by the scarcity of high-quality interaction data but also by inherent conflicts across heterogeneous planning tasks. These challenges result in models that excel at isolated tasks yet struggle to generalize, while existing multi-task training attempts suffer from gradient interference. In this paper, we present \textbf{MagicAgent}, a series of foundation models specifically designed for generalized agent planning. We introduce a lightweight and scalable synthetic data framework that generates high-quality trajectories across diverse planning tasks, including hierarchical task decomposition, tool-augmented planning, multi-constraint scheduling, procedural logic orchestration, and long-horizon tool execution. To mitigate training conflicts, we propose a two-stage training paradigm comprising supervised fine-tuning followed by multi-objective reinforcement learning over both static datasets and dynamic environments. Empirical results demonstrate that MagicAgent-32B and MagicAgent-30B-A3B deliver superior performance, achieving accuracies of $75.1\%$ on Worfbench, $55.9\%$ on NaturalPlan, $57.5\%$ on $\tau^2$-Bench, $86.9\%$ on BFCL-v3, and $81.2\%$ on ACEBench, as well as strong results on our in-house MagicEval benchmarks. These results substantially outperform existing sub-100B models and even surpass leading closed-source models.

</details>


### [209] [Evaluating Large Language Models on Quantum Mechanics: A Comparative Study Across Diverse Models and Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19006)
*S. K. Rithvik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了大型语言模型在量子力学问题解决中的表现，揭示了模型分层性能、任务难度模式、工具增强效果以及可复现性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对大型语言模型（LLMs）在量子力学问题解决能力上进行系统性评估。

Method: 评估了来自五家供应商的15个模型，涵盖三个能力层级，共20项任务，涉及推导、创新问题、非标准概念和数值计算，包括900次基线评估和75次工具增强评估。同时，进行了跨三次运行的可复现性分析。

Result: 结果显示模型性能存在明显分层：旗舰模型平均准确率为81%，优于中端模型（77%）和快速模型（67%）。任务难度模式明显：推导任务表现最佳（平均92%，旗舰模型100%），而数值计算任务最具挑战性（42%）。数值任务的工具增强效果因任务而异：整体提升不显著（+4.4pp），但令牌成本增加了3倍，且存在从+29pp到-16pp的显著异质性。可复现性分析显示平均方差为6.3pp，其中旗舰模型（如GPT-5）表现出卓越的稳定性（零方差），而专业模型需要多次运行评估。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献包括：(i) 一个具有自动验证功能的量子力学基准测试集；(ii) 量化分层性能等级的系统评估；(iii) 对工具增强权衡的实证分析；(iv) 可复现性特征描述。所有任务、验证器和结果均已公开发布。

Abstract: We present a systematic evaluation of large language models on quantum mechanics problem-solving. Our study evaluates 15 models from five providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, Alibaba, DeepSeek) spanning three capability tiers on 20 tasks covering derivations, creative problems, non-standard concepts, and numerical computation, comprising 900 baseline and 75 tool-augmented assessments. Results reveal clear tier stratification: flagship models achieve 81\% average accuracy, outperforming mid-tier (77\%) and fast models (67\%) by 4pp and 14pp respectively. Task difficulty patterns emerge distinctly: derivations show highest performance (92\% average, 100\% for flagship models), while numerical computation remains most challenging (42\%). Tool augmentation on numerical tasks yields task-dependent effects: modest overall improvement (+4.4pp) at 3x token cost masks dramatic heterogeneity ranging from +29pp gains to -16pp degradation. Reproducibility analysis across three runs quantifies 6.3pp average variance, with flagship models demonstrating exceptional stability (GPT-5 achieves zero variance) while specialized models require multi-run evaluation. This work contributes: (i) a benchmark for quantum mechanics with automatic verification, (ii) systematic evaluation quantifying tier-based performance hierarchies, (iii) empirical analysis of tool augmentation trade-offs, and (iv) reproducibility characterization. All tasks, verifiers, and results are publicly released.

</details>


### [210] [Asking the Right Questions: Improving Reasoning with Generated Stepping Stones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19069)
*Hengyuan Hu,Tingchen Fu,Minqi Jiang,Alexander H Miller,Yoram Bachrach,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究旨在通过引入“垫脚石”问题来提高大型语言模型（LLMs）解决复杂推理任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，大型语言模型在数学和编码等复杂推理任务上取得了巨大进展。然而，当面对更困难的、无法一次性解决的任务时，需要关注它们构建中间垫脚石（如简化、重新定义或子问题）的能力。

Method: 本研究通过ARQ（Asking the Right Questions）框架，在默认推理管道中引入一个问题生成器。研究还将垫脚石问题的生成视为一个后训练任务，并利用合成数据通过SFT和RL对LLMs进行微调，以生成更有用的垫脚石。

Result: 研究表明，好的垫脚石问题确实存在且具有可迁移性，这意味着可以生成高质量的问题，并且这些问题能显著帮助不同能力的LLMs解决目标任务。此外，通过SFT和RL在合成数据上对LLMs进行微调，可以使其生成更有用的垫脚石。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了垫脚石问题的存在、可迁移性及其对LLMs解决复杂推理任务的帮助，并展示了通过后训练可以有效地提升LLMs生成有用垫脚石的能力。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed tremendous progress in enabling LLMs to solve complex reasoning tasks such as math and coding. As we start to apply LLMs to harder tasks that they may not be able to solve in one shot, it is worth paying attention to their ability to construct intermediate stepping stones that prepare them to better solve the tasks. Examples of stepping stones include simplifications, alternative framings, or subproblems. We study properties and benefits of stepping stones in the context of modern reasoning LLMs via ARQ (\textbf{A}king the \textbf{R}ight \textbf{Q}uestions), our simple framework which introduces a question generator to the default reasoning pipeline. We first show that good stepping stone questions exist and are transferrable, meaning that good questions can be generated, and they substantially help LLMs of various capabilities in solving the target tasks.
