*Haoyu Yang,Haoxing Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种混合框架，利用生成式AI进行逆光刻（ILT）中的掩模合成，以克服非凸性问题，从而提高了掩模质量，减少了EPE违规，并提高了吞吐量，优于传统的ILT和其他生成式AI方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆光刻（ILT）对于现代半导体制造至关重要，但其高度非凸的目标函数常使优化陷入局部最优。现有探索生成式AI来热启动ILT的方法大多训练确定性图像到图像的转换器以模仿次优数据集，对在优化过程中逃离非凸陷阱的指导有限。

Method: 本文将掩模合成重新定义为条件采样：生成器学习在设计条件下掩模的分布并提出多个候选。生成器首先通过WGAN加重建损失进行预训练，然后使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 结合ILT引导的模仿损失进行微调。在推理时，采样一小批掩模，运行快速批量ILT精修，评估光刻指标（例如EPE、工艺窗口），并选择最佳候选。

Result: 在	exttt{LithoBench}数据集上，所提出的混合框架在3nm容差下减少了EPE违规，与强大的数值ILT基线相比，吞吐量大约翻倍，同时提高了最终掩模质量。在	exttt{ICCAD13}竞赛案例中，EPE改进超过20%，速度比最先进的数值ILT求解器快3倍。

Conclusion: 通过学习提出ILT友好的初始化，我们的方法缓解了非凸性，并超越了传统求解器或生成式AI所能达到的水平。

Abstract: Inverse lithography (ILT) is critical for modern semiconductor manufacturing but suffers from highly non-convex objectives that often trap optimization in poor local minima. Generative AI has been explored to warm-start ILT, yet most approaches train deterministic image-to-image translators to mimic sub-optimal datasets, providing limited guidance for escaping non-convex traps during refinement. We reformulate mask synthesis as conditional sampling: a generator learns a distribution over masks conditioned on the design and proposes multiple candidates. The generator is first pretrained with WGAN plus a reconstruction loss, then fine-tuned using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with an ILT-guided imitation loss. At inference, we sample a small batch of masks, run fast batched ILT refinement, evaluate lithography metrics (e.g., EPE, process window), and select the best candidate. On \texttt{LithoBench} dataset, the proposed hybrid framework reduces EPE violations under a 3\,nm tolerance and roughly doubles throughput versus a strong numerical ILT baseline, while improving final mask quality. We also present over 20\% EPE improvement on \texttt{ICCAD13} contest cases with 3$\times$ speedup over the SOTA numerical ILT solver. By learning to propose ILT-friendly initializations, our approach mitigates non-convexity and advances beyond what traditional solvers or GenAI can achieve.

</details>


### [249] [A Markovian View of Iterative-Feedback Loops in Image Generative Models: Neural Resonance and Model Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19033)
*Vibhas Kumar Vats,David J. Crandall,Samuel Goree*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究表明，AI训练数据中AI生成示例的迭代反馈会导致神经共振，即潜在空间收敛到低维不变结构，这是模型崩溃的统一解释，并提供了识别和缓解崩溃的诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI训练数据中不可避免地包含AI生成示例，导致模型间产生“反馈”效应，这种迭代反馈已知会导致模型崩溃，但其退化机制尚不明确。

Method: 通过将迭代反馈建模为马尔可夫链，研究人员探讨了神经共振发生的两个条件：反馈过程的遍历性和潜在表示的方向收缩。通过研究MNIST和ImageNet上的扩散模型、CycleGAN以及一项音频反馈实验，描绘了局部和全局流形几何的演变，并引入了八种崩溃行为的分类法。

Result: 研究发现，一类广泛的反馈过程在潜在空间中收敛到一个低维不变结构，称之为神经共振。神经共振的发生需要两个条件：反馈过程的遍历性和潜在表示的方向收缩。此外，研究还绘制了局部和全局流形几何的演变，并提出了八种崩溃行为的分类法。

Conclusion: 神经共振为生成模型中的长期退化行为提供了一个统一的解释，并为识别、表征并最终缓解模型崩溃提供了实用的诊断方法。

Abstract: AI training datasets will inevitably contain AI-generated examples, leading to ``feedback'' in which the output of one model impacts the training of another. It is known that such iterative feedback can lead to model collapse, yet the mechanisms underlying this degeneration remain poorly understood. Here we show that a broad class of feedback processes converges to a low-dimensional invariant structure in latent space, a phenomenon we call neural resonance. By modeling iterative feedback as a Markov Chain, we show that two conditions are needed for this resonance to occur: ergodicity of the feedback process and directional contraction of the latent representation. By studying diffusion models on MNIST and ImageNet, as well as CycleGAN and an audio feedback experiment, we map how local and global manifold geometry evolve, and we introduce an eight-pattern taxonomy of collapse behaviors. Neural resonance provides a unified explanation for long-term degenerate behavior in generative models and provides practical diagnostics for identifying, characterizing, and eventually mitigating collapse.

</details>


### [250] [IDLM: Inverse-distilled Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19066)
*David Li,Nikita Gushchin,Dmitry Abulkhanov,Eric Moulines,Ivan Oseledets,Maxim Panov,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为Inverse-distilled Diffusion Language Models (IDLM)的方法，通过将逆向蒸馏扩展到离散设置并解决相关理论和实践挑战，显著加速了扩散语言模型（DLMs）的推理速度（4x-64x），同时保持了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）的推理速度慢，因为其多步采样过程限制了实际应用。

Method: 将用于连续扩散模型的逆向蒸馏技术扩展到离散设置。为了解决离散空间中逆向蒸馏目标缺乏唯一性保证和反向传播不稳定等挑战，该方法提供了理论结果以确保唯一解，并引入了梯度稳定的松弛来支持有效训练。

Result: 实验表明，Inverse-distilled Diffusion Language Models (IDLM)将推理步骤减少了4到64倍，同时保持了教师模型的熵和生成困惑度。

Conclusion: IDLM通过解决逆向蒸馏在离散设置中的挑战，成功加速了扩散语言模型的推理过程，同时保持了其生成性能，使其更具实用性。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently achieved strong results in text generation. However, their multi-step sampling leads to slow inference, limiting practical use. To address this, we extend Inverse Distillation, a technique originally developed to accelerate continuous diffusion models, to the discrete setting. Nonetheless, this extension introduces both theoretical and practical challenges. From a theoretical perspective, the inverse distillation objective lacks uniqueness guarantees, which may lead to suboptimal solutions. From a practical standpoint, backpropagation in the discrete space is non-trivial and often unstable. To overcome these challenges, we first provide a theoretical result demonstrating that our inverse formulation admits a unique solution, thereby ensuring valid optimization. We then introduce gradient-stable relaxations to support effective training. As a result, experiments on multiple DLMs show that our method, Inverse-distilled Diffusion Language Models (IDLM), reduces the number of inference steps by 4x-64x, while preserving the teacher model's entropy and generative perplexity.

</details>


### [251] [Learning from Complexity: Exploring Dynamic Sample Pruning of Spatio-Temporal Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19113)
*Wei Chen,Junle Chen,Yuqian Wu,Yuxuan Liang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ST-Prune的动态样本剪枝方法，通过识别最具信息量的样本来解决时空预测中深度学习模型训练效率低下的问题，从而显著加速训练并保持或提高模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交通、气候科学和城市规划等领域的时空预测中，深度学习模型在处理海量且冗余的数据集时面临巨大的计算瓶颈。现有解决方案主要关注模型架构或优化器，但忽略了训练数据本身的效率低下问题，即每个epoch都迭代整个静态数据集，浪费了大量资源在易学或重复的样本上。

Method: 本文提出了一种新颖的训练效率技术，即ST-Prune，通过动态样本剪枝，根据模型实时的学习状态智能识别最具信息量的样本，从而加速收敛并提高训练效率。

Result: 在真实世界的时空数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，ST-Prune显著加快了训练速度，同时保持甚至提高了模型性能，并且具有可扩展性和普适性。

Conclusion: ST-Prune是一种有效且高效的训练技术，可以显著提高时空预测中深度学习模型的训练效率，同时不牺牲或甚至提升模型性能。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal forecasting is fundamental to intelligent systems in transportation, climate science, and urban planning. However, training deep learning models on the massive, often redundant, datasets from these domains presents a significant computational bottleneck. Existing solutions typically focus on optimizing model architectures or optimizers, while overlooking the inherent inefficiency of the training data itself. This conventional approach of iterating over the entire static dataset each epoch wastes considerable resources on easy-to-learn or repetitive samples. In this paper, we explore a novel training-efficiency techniques, namely learning from complexity with dynamic sample pruning, ST-Prune, for spatio-temporal forecasting. Through dynamic sample pruning, we aim to intelligently identify the most informative samples based on the model's real-time learning state, thereby accelerating convergence and improving training efficiency. Extensive experiments conducted on real-world spatio-temporal datasets show that ST-Prune significantly accelerates the training speed while maintaining or even improving the model performance, and it also has scalability and universality.

</details>


### [252] [Detecting labeling bias using influence functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19130)
*Frida Jørgensen,Nina Weng,Siavash Bigdeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出利用影响函数检测标签偏差，通过识别训练数据中的错误标签来揭示潜在的故障模式，并在MNIST和CheXpert数据集上取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据收集中，由于资源限制或无意识偏见，标签偏差会导致不同子群体的标签错误率不均或子群体代表性不足。大多数公平性约束假设训练标签反映真实分布，因此在存在标签偏差时无效，这引出了一个挑战性问题：如何检测标签偏差？

Method: 研究人员调查了影响函数在检测标签偏差方面的潜力。他们开发了一个样本评估流程，首先在MNIST数据集上进行测试，然后扩展到更复杂的CheXpert医学图像数据集。为了模拟标签噪声，他们通过翻转数据集中一个类别的20%标签来引入受控错误。方法中使用了对角线Hessian近似。

Result: 在MNIST数据集上，该方法成功检测到近90%的错误标签样本。在CheXpert数据集上，错误标签样本始终表现出更高的影响分数。这些结果突出了影响函数在识别标签错误方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 影响函数在识别标签错误方面具有巨大潜力，因此可以作为一种有效的工具来检测由于数据收集中的标签偏差。

Abstract: Labeling bias arises during data collection due to resource limitations or unconscious bias, leading to unequal label error rates across subgroups or misrepresentation of subgroup prevalence. Most fairness constraints assume training labels reflect the true distribution, rendering them ineffective when labeling bias is present; leaving a challenging question, that \textit{how can we detect such labeling bias?} In this work, we investigate whether influence functions can be used to detect labeling bias. Influence functions estimate how much each training sample affects a model's predictions by leveraging the gradient and Hessian of the loss function -- when labeling errors occur, influence functions can identify wrongly labeled samples in the training set, revealing the underlying failure mode. We develop a sample valuation pipeline and test it first on the MNIST dataset, then scaled to the more complex CheXpert medical imaging dataset. To examine label noise, we introduced controlled errors by flipping 20\% of the labels for one class in the dataset. Using a diagonal Hessian approximation, we demonstrated promising results, successfully detecting nearly 90\% of mislabeled samples in MNIST. On CheXpert, mislabeled samples consistently exhibit higher influence scores. These results highlight the potential of influence functions for identifying label errors.

</details>


### [253] [Test-Time Learning of Causal Structure from Interventional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19131)
*Wei Chen,Rui Ding,Bojun Huang,Yang Zhang,Qiang Fu,Yuxuan Liang,Han Shi,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TICL（Test-time Interventional Causal Learning）是一种新颖的因果学习方法，通过结合测试时训练和联合因果推断，解决了监督因果学习在未知干预目标的多样化干预设置中泛化能力差的问题。它采用自增强策略生成实例特定的训练数据，并开发了一种受PC启发的两阶段监督学习方案，在因果发现和干预目标检测方面表现出卓越的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督因果学习在因果发现方面已显示出前景，但在多样化的干预设置中，特别是当干预目标未知时，其泛化能力常常不足。

Method: 本文提出了TICL（Test-time Interventional Causal Learning），该方法结合了测试时训练和联合因果推断。具体来说，它设计了一种自增强策略，在测试时生成实例特定的训练数据，有效避免了分布偏移。此外，通过整合联合因果推断，开发了一种受PC启发的两阶段监督学习方案，该方案有效利用了自增强训练数据，同时确保了理论上的可识别性。

Result: 在bnlearn基准上的大量实验表明，TICL在因果发现和干预目标检测的多个方面都表现出优越性。

Conclusion: TICL通过结合测试时训练和联合因果推断，有效解决了监督因果学习在复杂干预环境中的泛化挑战，并在因果发现和干预目标检测方面取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Supervised causal learning has shown promise in causal discovery, yet it often struggles with generalization across diverse interventional settings, particularly when intervention targets are unknown. To address this, we propose TICL (Test-time Interventional Causal Learning), a novel method that synergizes Test-Time Training with Joint Causal Inference. Specifically, we design a self-augmentation strategy to generate instance-specific training data at test time, effectively avoiding distribution shifts. Furthermore, by integrating joint causal inference, we developed a PC-inspired two-phase supervised learning scheme, which effectively leverages self-augmented training data while ensuring theoretical identifiability. Extensive experiments on bnlearn benchmarks demonstrate TICL's superiority in multiple aspects of causal discovery and intervention target detection.

</details>


### [254] [Celo2: Towards Learned Optimization Free Lunch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19142)
*Abhinav Moudgil,Boris Knyazev,Eugene Belilovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的学习型优化器，通过简洁的归一化架构和元训练增强，显著降低了元训练成本（仅需4.5 GPU小时），并能稳定扩展到十亿级预训练任务（GPT-3 XL 1.3B），且在各种分布外任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习型优化器存在元泛化能力差和元训练成本高的问题，限制了其实际应用。例如，VeLO需要大量计算资源但仍无法泛化到6亿参数以上的任务。

Method: 通过设计一个简单的归一化优化器架构并增强元训练。

Result: 该学习型更新规则仅需4.5 GPU小时的元训练，就能稳定扩展到比其元训练分布大六个数量级的十亿级预训练任务（GPT-3 XL 1.3B）。它在各种分布外任务中表现出强大的性能，并与现代优化策略兼容，包括正交化、针对输入-输出和隐藏权重的不同更新规则以及解耦权重衰减。

Conclusion: 这项工作为实际应用的可学习优化算法铺平了道路，解锁了对更丰富的元训练和数据整理方案的探索，以进一步提高性能。

Abstract: Learned optimizers are powerful alternatives to hand-designed update rules like Adam, yet they have seen limited practical adoption since they often fail to meta-generalize beyond their training distribution and incur high meta-training cost. For instance, prior work, VeLO, scaled meta-training to 4,000 TPU months ($\sim$10$\times$ GPT-3 compute) to meta-train a general-purpose optimizer but it failed to generalize beyond 600M parameters tasks. In this work, we present a surprising finding: by crafting a simple normalized optimizer architecture and augmenting meta-training, it becomes feasible to meta-train a performant general-purpose learned update rule on a tiny fraction of VeLO compute, 4.5 GPU hours to be precise. Our learned update rule scales stably to a billion-scale pretraining task (GPT-3 XL 1.3B) which is six orders of magnitude larger than its meta-training distribution. Furthermore, it shows strong performance across diverse out-of-distribution tasks and is compatible with modern optimization harness that includes orthogonalization, distinct update rules for input-output and hidden weights, and decoupled weight decay. In all, this work paves the way for practically applicable learnable optimization algorithms, unlocking exploration of richer meta-training and data curation recipes to further improve performance.

</details>


### [255] [Virtual Parameter Sharpening: Dynamic Low-Rank Perturbations for Inference-Time Reasoning Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19169)
*Saba Kublashvili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了虚拟参数锐化（VPS），一种推理时技术，它通过动态的、激活条件下的低秩扰动来增强冻结的Transformer线性层，从而在不需要持久参数更新的情况下实现测试时适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介绍一种名为虚拟参数锐化（VPS）的推理时技术，该技术通过动态、激活条件的低秩扰动来增强冻结的Transformer线性层，旨在克服像LoRA等参数高效微调方法中静态低秩适配器的局限性，从而在不进行持久参数更新的情况下实现测试时适应，并探讨激活条件计算如何提升大型语言模型的推理能力。

Method: VPS通过批次激活统计和可选的梯度信号动态构建低秩扰动因子，形式为 Delta W = gamma * W^T V U^T W。其中，选择矩阵U和V通过稀疏激活引导选择或Sylvester耦合回归构建。该方法还包含一个自适应策略系统，根据激活能量和token级熵来调节扰动幅度，并结合了多目标验证和迭代细化机制。

Result: 论文提供了完整的算法框架，分析了扰动的谱特性及其数学基础，并讨论了激活条件计算可能增强大型语言模型推理能力的机制。

Conclusion: VPS作为一种新颖的推理时适应技术，通过动态、激活条件的低秩扰动来增强Transformer模型，实现了无需持久参数更新的测试时适应，并有可能提升大型语言模型的推理能力。

Abstract: I introduce Virtual Parameter Sharpening (VPS), an inference-time technique that augments frozen transformer linear layers with dynamic, activation-conditioned low-rank perturbations. Unlike parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA, which learn static low-rank adapters, VPS constructs its perturbation factors on the fly from batch activation statistics and optional gradient signals, enabling test-time adaptation without persistent parameter updates. The perturbation takes the form Delta W = gamma * W^T V U^T W, where selector matrices U and V are constructed via sparse activation-guided selection or Sylvester-coupled regression. We provide a theoretical analysis of the perturbation's spectral properties and describe an adaptive policy system that modulates perturbation magnitude based on activation energy and token-level entropy. This system incorporates multi-objective verification with iterative refinement for tasks with ground-truth supervision. We present the complete algorithmic framework, analyze its mathematical foundations, and discuss the mechanisms by which activation-conditioned computation may enhance reasoning capabilities in large language models. Implementation and experimental code are available at .

</details>


### [256] [HybridFL: A Federated Learning Approach for Financial Crime Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19207)
*Afsana Khan,Marijn ten Thij,Guangzhi Tang,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出了混合联邦学习（HybridFL），以解决数据在不同用户间水平分布和互补特征集间垂直分布的复杂混合场景。该方法在保护数据隐私的同时，实现了比本地模型显著提升的性能，并与集中式基准模型表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦学习通常只解决水平或垂直数据划分问题，但许多现实世界的场景呈现出复杂的混合数据分布。

Method: 本文提出了HybridFL，用于处理数据在不相交用户之间水平划分以及在互补特征集之间垂直划分的情况。该架构通过整合水平聚合和垂直特征融合，实现联合学习，同时严格保护数据本地性。它在金融犯罪检测的背景下进行了评估，其中一个交易方持有交易级属性，多个银行维护私有账户级特征。

Result: 在AMLSim和SWIFT数据集上的实验表明，HybridFL显著优于仅处理交易的本地模型，并实现了与集中式基准模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: HybridFL能够解决混合数据分布场景下的联合学习问题，同时严格保护数据本地性，并表现出优越的性能。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is a privacy-preserving machine learning paradigm that enables multiple parties to collaboratively train models on privately owned data without sharing raw information. While standard FL typically addresses either horizontal or vertical data partitions, many real-world scenarios exhibit a complex hybrid distribution. This paper proposes Hybrid Federated Learning (HybridFL) to address data split both horizontally across disjoint users and vertically across complementary feature sets. We evaluate HybridFL in a financial crime detection context, where a transaction party holds transaction-level attributes and multiple banks maintain private account-level features. By integrating horizontal aggregation and vertical feature fusion, the proposed architecture enables joint learning while strictly preserving data locality. Experiments on AMLSim and SWIFT datasets demonstrate that HybridFL significantly outperforms the transaction-only local model and achieves performance comparable to a centralized benchmark.

</details>


### [257] [Evaluating SAP RPT-1 for Enterprise Business Process Prediction: In-Context Learning vs. Traditional Machine Learning on Structured SAP Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19237)
*Amit Lal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAP的RPT-1是首个经过独立评估的表格基础模型，在无需特定任务训练的情况下，能达到调优GBDT模型91-96%的准确率，在有限数据下甚至表现更优。研究提出RPT-1可用于快速筛选，再结合GBDT进行选择性训练的混合工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在无需特定任务训练的情况下，使企业数据能够更便捷地应用机器学习。本文首次从实践者的角度独立评估了SAP的检索预训练Transformer (RPT-1) 模型。

Method: 将RPT-1与调优的梯度提升决策树（XGBoost, LightGBM, CatBoost）进行基准测试。评估在三个SAP业务场景：SD/MM/PP模块的需求预测、BC/MM/QM中的预测性数据完整性以及FI/CO/AR中的财务风险分类。通过对2,500至3,200行数据集进行五折交叉验证。

Result: RPT-1在无需任何训练示例的情况下，达到了调优GBDT模型91-96%的准确率。分类任务（AUC-ROC）的差距为3.6-4.1个百分点，回归任务（R方）的差距为8.9-11.1个百分点。一个有趣的发现是，在大约75-100行上下文的有限数据下，RPT-1的表现优于XGBoost。

Conclusion: 基于上述结果，本文提出了一种实用的混合工作流程：先使用RPT-1进行快速筛选，然后根据预测准确性的需求，有选择地训练GBDT模型。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models aim to make machine learning accessible for enterprise data without task-specific training. This paper presents the first independent evaluation of SAP's Retrieval Pretrained Transformer (RPT-1) from a practitioner perspective. RPT-1 is a compact 64.6 MB model pretrained on 1.34 TB of structured data across 3.1 million tables. We benchmark it against tuned gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost, LightGBM, CatBoost) on three SAP business scenarios: demand forecasting across SD/MM/PP modules, predictive data integrity in BC/MM/QM, and financial risk classification in FI/CO/AR. Across five-fold cross-validation on datasets ranging from 2,500 to 3,200 rows, RPT-1 reaches 91-96% of tuned GBDT accuracy without any training examples. The classification gap is modest at 3.6-4.1 percentage points on AUC-ROC, though regression tasks show wider gaps of 8.9-11.1 percentage points on R-squared. An interesting finding is a crossover at roughly 75-100 context rows where RPT-1 actually outperforms XGBoost under limited data. Based on these results, we propose a practical hybrid workflow: use RPT-1 for rapid screening, then train GBDT selectively where prediction accuracy justifies the effort. All experiments are reproducible through publicly available Hugging Face Spaces.

</details>


### [258] [Taming Preconditioner Drift: Unlocking the Potential of Second-Order Optimizers for Federated Learning on Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19271)
*Junkang Liu,Fanhua Shang,Hongying Liu,Jin Liu,Weixin An,Yuanyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedPAC通过预条件器对齐和校正框架解决了联邦二阶优化器在非IID数据上因“预条件器漂移”导致的性能下降，显著提高了稳定性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 朴素的联邦二阶优化器在非IID数据上不稳定甚至发散，主要原因是客户端二阶训练导致的“预条件器漂移”产生了异构的曲率定义几何结构，导致服务器端模型平均更新计算在不兼容的度量下，从而破坏了全局下降方向。

Method: 提出FedPAC（预条件器对齐和校正）框架，通过以下方式明确分离参数聚合和几何同步：(i) 对齐：将局部预条件器聚合成全局参考，并通过全局预条件器热启动客户端；(ii) 校正：使用全局预条件方向引导局部预条件更新以抑制长期漂移。

Result: 该方法提供了漂移耦合的非凸收敛保证，并在部分参与下实现线性加速。在视觉和语言任务中，FedPAC持续提高稳定性和准确性，在CIFAR-100数据集上使用ViT模型实现了高达5.8%的绝对准确率提升。

Conclusion: FedPAC框架有效解决了联邦二阶优化中因预条件器漂移引起的几何不匹配问题，从而在非IID数据环境下显著提高了模型的训练稳定性和准确性，并提供了理论收敛性保证。

Abstract: Second-order optimizers can significantly accelerate large-scale training, yet their naive federated variants are often unstable or even diverge on non-IID data. We show that a key culprit is \emph{preconditioner drift}: client-side second-order training induces heterogeneous \emph{curvature-defined geometries} (i.e., preconditioner coordinate systems), and server-side model averaging updates computed under incompatible metrics, corrupting the global descent direction. To address this geometric mismatch, we propose \texttt{FedPAC}, a \emph{preconditioner alignment and correction} framework for reliable federated second-order optimization. \texttt{FedPAC} explicitly decouples parameter aggregation from geometry synchronization by: (i) \textbf{Alignment} (i.e.,aggregating local preconditioners into a global reference and warm-starting clients via global preconditioner); and (ii) \textbf{Correction} (i.e., steering local preconditioned updates using a global preconditioned direction to suppress long-term drift). We provide drift-coupled non-convex convergence guarantees with linear speedup under partial participation. Empirically, \texttt{FedPAC} consistently improves stability and accuracy across vision and language tasks, achieving up to $5.8\%$ absolute accuracy gain on CIFAR-100 with ViTs. Code is available at .

</details>


### [259] [Wide Open Gazes: Quantifying Visual Exploratory Behavior in Soccer with Pose Enhanced Positional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18519)
*Joris Bekkers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种新颖的连续随机视觉层模型，利用姿态增强时空跟踪技术量化足球运动员的视觉感知。该模型克服了传统方法的局限性，并能预测短期的比赛成功，且可无缝集成到现有足球分析框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统足球视觉探索行为测量方法（基于头部快速移动计数）存在多项缺陷，包括球员位置偏差（侧重中场）、标注困难、二元测量限制、预测短期比赛成功能力不足，以及与底层的足球分析模型（如球场控制）不兼容。

Method: 本研究引入了一种新颖的公式化连续随机视觉层，通过姿态增强的时空跟踪来量化球员的视觉感知。它结合了头部和肩部旋转角度的概率视野和遮挡模型，为个体球员创建了速度依赖的二维俯视视觉地图。这些视觉地图与球场控制和球场价值表面相结合，用于分析球员接球前的“等待阶段”及其随后的“持球阶段”。

Result: 研究表明，聚合的视觉指标（例如在等待传球时观察到的防守区域百分比）能够预测带球结束时获得的受控球场价值。这一结论基于2024年美洲杯32场比赛的同步姿态增强跟踪数据和持球事件数据。该方法不受球员位置限制，无需手动标注，并提供可无缝集成到现有足球分析框架的连续测量结果。

Conclusion: 新的方法为足球分析中视觉感知的量化提供了一种更稳健、更集成的方案，解决了传统方法的局限性，并为比赛中的成功提供了有价值的预测性见解。相关计算工具已开源以支持进一步集成。

Abstract: Traditional approaches to measuring visual exploratory behavior in soccer rely on counting visual exploratory actions (VEAs) based on rapid head movements exceeding 125°/s, but this method suffer from player position bias (i.e., a focus on central midfielders), annotation challenges, binary measurement constraints (i.e., a player is scanning, or not), lack the power to predict relevant short-term in-game future success, and are incompatible with fundamental soccer analytics models such as pitch control. This research introduces a novel formulaic continuous stochastic vision layer to quantify players' visual perception from pose-enhanced spatiotemporal tracking. Our probabilistic field-of-view and occlusion models incorporate head and shoulder rotation angles to create speed-dependent vision maps for individual players in a two-dimensional top-down plane. We combine these vision maps with pitch control and pitch value surfaces to analyze the awaiting phase (when a player is awaiting the ball to arrive after a pass for a teammate) and their subsequent on-ball phase. We demonstrate that aggregated visual metrics - such as the percentage of defended area observed while awaiting a pass - are predictive of controlled pitch value gained at the end of dribbling actions using 32 games of synchronized pose-enhanced tracking data and on-ball event data from the 2024 Copa America. This methodology works regardless of player position, eliminates manual annotation requirements, and provides continuous measurements that seamlessly integrate into existing soccer analytics frameworks. To further support the integration with existing soccer analytics frameworks we open-source the tools required to make these calculations.

</details>


### [260] [Soft Sequence Policy Optimization: Bridging GMPO and SAPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19327)
*Svetlana Glazyrina,Maksim Kryzhanovskiy,Roman Ischenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的离策略强化学习目标，名为软序列策略优化（Soft Sequence Policy Optimization, SSPO），它在序列级重要性权重中融入了基于token级概率比率的软门控函数，旨在促进有效的策略探索并保持训练稳定性。SSPO建立在GRPO、SAPO和GMPO等现有LLM对齐研究的基础上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM对齐研究主要集中于开发基于GRPO的新策略优化方法，其中有两个主要方向：转向与序列级奖励对齐的序列级重要性采样权重，以及替代PPO风格的裁剪以避免训练信号损失和熵崩溃。现有方法如SAPO和GMPO已在这些方向上取得进展，但仍有改进空间。

Method: 本文提出软序列策略优化（Soft Sequence Policy Optimization），这是一种离策略强化学习目标，它在序列级重要性权重中引入了基于token级概率比率的软门控函数。

Result: 该方法旨在促进有效的策略探索，同时保持训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 论文通过引入软序列策略优化，为LLM对齐提供了一个新的离策略强化学习目标，该目标通过结合软门控函数与token级概率比率在序列级重要性权重中，旨在实现更有效的策略探索和更稳定的训练。

Abstract: A significant portion of recent research on Large Language Model (LLM) alignment focuses on developing new policy optimization methods based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Two prominent directions have emerged: (i) a shift toward sequence-level importance sampling weights that better align with the sequence-level rewards used in many tasks, and (ii) alternatives to PPO-style clipping that aim to avoid the associated loss of training signal and entropy collapse. Recent work, such as Soft Adaptive Policy Optimization (SAPO), reformulates the Scopic objective within the GRPO framework and achieves both sequence coherence and token adaptivity. Geometric-Mean Policy Optimization (GMPO) leverages token-wise ratio clipping within sequence importance sampling weights. Building on these ideas, this work proposes a new objective that promotes effective policy exploration while maintaining training stability. Specifically, we introduce Soft Sequence Policy Optimization, an off-policy reinforcement learning objective that incorporates soft gating functions over token-level probability ratios within sequence-level importance weights.

</details>


### [261] [Smooth Gate Functions for Soft Advantage Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19345)
*Egor Denisov,Svetlana Glazyrina,Maksim Kryzhanovskiy,Roman Ischenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大型语言模型训练中，不同门函数对策略优化稳定性与模型性能的影响。通过形式化可允许门的关键属性并实证评估多种函数，特别是在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型上进行数学推理任务实验，结果为设计更平滑、更鲁棒的策略优化目标提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在大型语言模型训练中存在不稳定性，而SAPO通过平滑的基于sigmoid的门函数解决了这一问题。本文旨在进一步探索不同门函数对训练稳定性和最终模型性能的影响。

Method: 本文形式化了可允许门函数应满足的关键属性，并识别了几类函数进行实证评估。实验在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型上针对数学推理任务进行。

Result: 本文根据在数学推理任务上使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型进行的实验，对研究结果进行了分析。

Conclusion: 这些结果为大型语言模型训练中设计更平滑、更鲁棒的策略优化目标提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has significantly advanced the training of large language models and enhanced their reasoning capabilities, while it remains susceptible to instability due to the use of hard clipping. Soft Adaptive Policy Optimization (SAPO) addresses this limitation by replacing clipping with a smooth sigmoid-based gate function, which leads to more stable updates. We have decided to push this theory further and investigate the impact of different gate functions on both training stability and final model performance. We formalize the key properties that admissible gates should satisfy and identify several families of such functions for empirical evaluation. This paper presents an analysis of our findings based on experiments conducted with the Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct model on mathematical reasoning tasks. These results provide practical guidance for designing smoother and more robust policy optimization objectives for large language model training.

</details>


### [262] [Active perception and disentangled representations allow continual, episodic zero and few-shot learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19355)
*David Rawlinson,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文描述了一种互补学习系统（CLS），其中一个快速学习器完全放弃泛化能力，以实现持续的零样本和少样本学习，并与一个慢速、统计学习器协同工作，通过主动感知系统中的上下文偏差，将新颖刺激编码为熟悉的、泛化的术语，从而实现零样本和少样本学习，并为鲁棒的持续学习提供途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统中，泛化通常导致实体或类别边界的纠缠表示，这在需要快速、大幅度更新的持续或少样本学习中可能导致破坏性干扰。现有的快速学习技术通常无法泛化。

Method: 该方法提出一个互补学习系统（CLS），其中快速学习器放弃泛化，专注于持续的零样本和少样本学习，作为一个并行的推理系统。它利用主动感知系统中的慢速、统计学习器：快速学习器提供上下文偏差，促使慢速学习器以熟悉的、泛化的方式编码新颖刺激。

Result: 该架构表明，快速、上下文驱动的推理可以与慢速、结构化泛化共存。

Conclusion: 这为鲁棒的持续学习提供了一条途径。

Abstract: Generalization is often regarded as an essential property of machine learning systems. However, perhaps not every component of a system needs to generalize. Training models for generalization typically produces entangled representations at the boundaries of entities or classes, which can lead to destructive interference when rapid, high-magnitude updates are required for continual or few-shot learning. Techniques for fast learning with non-interfering representations exist, but they generally fail to generalize. Here, we describe a Complementary Learning System (CLS) in which the fast learner entirely foregoes generalization in exchange for continual zero-shot and few-shot learning. Unlike most CLS approaches, which use episodic memory primarily for replay and consolidation, our fast, disentangled learner operates as a parallel reasoning system. The fast learner can overcome observation variability and uncertainty by leveraging a conventional slow, statistical learner within an active perception system: A contextual bias provided by the fast learner induces the slow learner to encode novel stimuli in familiar, generalized terms, enabling zero-shot and few-shot learning. This architecture demonstrates that fast, context-driven reasoning can coexist with slow, structured generalization, providing a pathway for robust continual learning.

</details>


### [263] [Phase-Consistent Magnetic Spectral Learning for Multi-View Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18728)
*Mingdong Lu,Zhikui Chen,Meng Liu,Shubin Ma,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为“相位一致磁谱学习”（Phase-Consistent Magnetic Spectral Learning, PCMSL）的新型无监督多视图聚类（MVC）方法。PCMSL通过将跨视图方向一致性建模为复值磁亲和力中的相位项，并利用埃尔米特磁拉普拉斯算子提取稳定的共享谱信号，从而指导无监督多视图表示学习和聚类。为获得鲁棒的输入，该方法构建了基于锚点的高阶共识模型，并进行了轻量级细化。在多个公共基准测试中，PCMSL始终优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督多视图聚类（MVC）面临的核心挑战是在视图差异和噪声下，如何获取可靠的共享结构信号来指导表示学习和跨视图对齐。现有方法依赖于仅有幅度的亲和力或早期伪目标，当不同视图诱导的关系具有可比强度但方向趋势矛盾时，这些方法不稳定，从而扭曲了全局谱几何并降低了聚类性能。

Method: 该论文提出了相位一致磁谱学习（PCMSL）。该方法明确将跨视图方向一致性建模为一个相位项，并将其与非负幅度骨架结合，形成一个复值磁亲和力。通过埃尔米特磁拉普拉斯算子提取稳定的共享谱信号，并将其作为结构化自监督信号，用于指导无监督多视图表示学习和聚类。为了获得可伸缩的鲁棒谱提取输入，该方法通过基于锚点的高阶共识建模构建紧凑的共享结构，并应用轻量级细化来抑制噪声或不一致的关系。

Result: 在多个公共多视图基准测试上进行的广泛实验表明，该方法始终优于强大的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该论文成功提出了相位一致磁谱学习（PCMSL），通过引入相位一致性到磁亲和力中，并利用埃尔米特磁拉普拉斯算子进行稳定的谱信号提取，有效解决了现有MVC方法在视图矛盾下的不稳定问题，并展示了优越的聚类性能。

Abstract: Unsupervised multi-view clustering (MVC) aims to partition data into meaningful groups by leveraging complementary information from multiple views without labels, yet a central challenge is to obtain a reliable shared structural signal to guide representation learning and cross-view alignment under view discrepancy and noise. Existing approaches often rely on magnitude-only affinities or early pseudo targets, which can be unstable when different views induce relations with comparable strengths but contradictory directional tendencies, thereby distorting the global spectral geometry and degrading clustering. In this paper, we propose \emph{Phase-Consistent Magnetic Spectral Learning} for MVC: we explicitly model cross-view directional agreement as a phase term and combine it with a nonnegative magnitude backbone to form a complex-valued magnetic affinity, extract a stable shared spectral signal via a Hermitian magnetic Laplacian, and use it as structured self-supervision to guide unsupervised multi-view representation learning and clustering. To obtain robust inputs for spectral extraction at scale, we construct a compact shared structure with anchor-based high-order consensus modeling and apply a lightweight refinement to suppress noisy or inconsistent relations. Extensive experiments on multiple public multi-view benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [264] [Federated Learning Playground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19489)
*Bryan Guanrong Shan,Alysa Ziying Tan,Han Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为“联邦学习游乐场”的交互式浏览器平台，它扩展了TensorFlow Playground，用于教授联邦学习（FL）核心概念，并允许用户无需编码即可实验FL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低分布式AI新手的入门门槛，促进对联邦学习（FL）的更广泛理解和采用。

Method: 提供一个交互式浏览器平台，用户可以直接在浏览器中实验异构客户端数据分布、模型超参数和聚合算法，并通过实时可视化观察其对客户端和全局模型的影响。

Result: 用户可以直观地理解非IID数据、局部过拟合和可扩展性等挑战。该平台可作为易于使用的教育工具，并为快速原型设计和比较FL方法提供沙盒。

Conclusion: “联邦学习游乐场”降低了入门门槛，普及了联邦学习的探索，并促进了这一重要范式的更广泛理解和采用。

Abstract: We present Federated Learning Playground, an interactive browser-based platform inspired by and extends TensorFlow Playground that teaches core Federated Learning (FL) concepts. Users can experiment with heterogeneous client data distributions, model hyperparameters, and aggregation algorithms directly in the browser without coding or system setup, and observe their effects on client and global models through real-time visualizations, gaining intuition for challenges such as non-IID data, local overfitting, and scalability. The playground serves as an easy to use educational tool, lowering the entry barrier for newcomers to distributed AI while also offering a sandbox for rapidly prototyping and comparing FL methods. By democratizing exploration of FL, it promotes broader understanding and adoption of this important paradigm.

</details>


### [265] [PCA-VAE: Differentiable Subspace Quantization without Codebook Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18904)
*Hao Lu,Onur C. Koyun,Yongxin Guo,Zhengjie Zhu,Abbas Alili,Metin Nafi Gurcan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PCA-VAE通过在线PCA瓶颈替代了VQ，实现了更好的重建质量、更高的比特效率和可解释的潜在空间，同时避免了VQ的固有缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量量化自编码器（VQ autoencoders）存在固有的缺陷：量化器不可微分，需要直通技巧，并且容易崩溃。

Method: 本文通过使用基于Oja规则训练的在线PCA瓶颈替换VQ，提出了PCA-VAE模型。该模型学习正交的、按方差排序的潜在基，无需码本、承诺损失或查找噪声。

Result: PCA-VAE在CelebAHQ上的重建质量超过了VQ-GAN和SimVQ，同时使用的潜在比特数减少了10-100倍。它还能在没有对抗性正则化或解缠目标的情况下，产生自然可解释的维度（例如姿势、光照、性别线索）。

Conclusion: PCA是VQ的一个可行替代方案：它具有数学基础、稳定、比特高效且语义结构化，为超越向量量化的生成模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Vector-quantized autoencoders deliver high-fidelity latents but suffer inherent flaws: the quantizer is non-differentiable, requires straight-through hacks, and is prone to collapse. We address these issues at the root by replacing VQ with a simple, principled, and fully differentiable alternative: an online PCA bottleneck trained via Oja's rule. The resulting model, PCA-VAE, learns an orthogonal, variance-ordered latent basis without codebooks, commitment losses, or lookup noise. Despite its simplicity, PCA-VAE exceeds VQ-GAN and SimVQ in reconstruction quality on CelebAHQ while using 10-100x fewer latent bits. It also produces naturally interpretable dimensions (e.g., pose, lighting, gender cues) without adversarial regularization or disentanglement objectives. These results suggest that PCA is a viable replacement for VQ: mathematically grounded, stable, bit-efficient, and semantically structured, offering a new direction for generative models beyond vector quantization.

</details>


### [266] [DeepInterestGR: Mining Deep Multi-Interest Using Multi-Modal LLMs for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18907)
*Yangchen Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepInterestGR是一个生成式推荐框架，通过多LLM兴趣挖掘、奖励标记的深度兴趣和兴趣增强的物品离散化，解决了现有方法中存在的“浅层兴趣问题”，提高了推荐的个性化深度和可解释性，并在实验中超越了现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式推荐框架主要依赖浅层行为信号和表面文本特征，导致“浅层兴趣问题”，即模型未能捕捉用户交互背后潜在的、语义丰富的兴趣，从而限制了个性化深度和推荐可解释性。

Method: DeepInterestGR引入了三项关键创新：1) 多LLM兴趣挖掘 (MLIM)：利用多个前沿LLM及其多模态变体，通过思维链提示提取深度文本和视觉兴趣表示。2) 奖励标记的深度兴趣 (RLDI)：使用轻量级二元分类器为挖掘出的兴趣分配奖励标签，为强化学习提供有效的监督信号。3) 兴趣增强的物品离散化 (IEID)：将整理好的深度兴趣编码成语义嵌入，并通过RQ-VAE量化为SID token。模型采用两阶段训练流程：首先进行有监督微调，使生成模型与深度兴趣信号和协同过滤模式对齐；随后进行强化学习，使用由兴趣感知奖励优化的GRPO。

Result: 在三个Amazon Review基准测试中，DeepInterestGR在HR@K和NDCG@K指标上始终优于现有最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: DeepInterestGR通过整合深度用户兴趣，有效解决了生成式推荐中的浅层兴趣问题，从而提高了推荐的个性化和可解释性。

Abstract: Recent generative recommendation frameworks have demonstrated remarkable scaling potential by reformulating item prediction as autoregressive Semantic ID (SID) generation. However, existing methods primarily rely on shallow behavioral signals, encoding items solely through surface-level textual features such as titles and descriptions. This reliance results in a critical Shallow Interest problem: the model fails to capture the latent, semantically rich interests underlying user interactions, limiting both personalization depth and recommendation interpretability. DeepInterestGR introduces three key innovations: (1) Multi-LLM Interest Mining (MLIM): We leverage multiple frontier LLMs along with their multi-modal variants to extract deep textual and visual interest representations through Chain-of-Thought prompting. (2) Reward-Labeled Deep Interest (RLDI): We employ a lightweight binary classifier to assign reward labels to mined interests, enabling effective supervision signals for reinforcement learning. (3) Interest-Enhanced Item Discretization (IEID): The curated deep interests are encoded into semantic embeddings and quantized into SID tokens via RQ-VAE. We adopt a two-stage training pipeline: supervised fine-tuning aligns the generative model with deep interest signals and collaborative filtering patterns, followed by reinforcement learning with GRPO optimized by our Interest-Aware Reward. Experiments on three Amazon Review benchmarks demonstrate that DeepInterestGR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across HR@K and NDCG@K metrics.

</details>


### [267] [Softmax is not Enough (for Adaptive Conformal Classification)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19498)
*Navid Akhavan Attar,Hesam Asadollahzadeh,Ling Luo,Uwe Aickelin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度共形分类器中的预测集由于softmax输出的不可靠性而缺乏适应性和效率。本文提出了一种新方法，利用来自预softmax logit空间的亥姆霍兹自由能来重新加权非一致性分数，从而提高了预测集的适应性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度共形分类器中，从softmax输出导出的非一致性分数可能不可靠，无法有效指示模型的确定性，这会限制预测集在效率和适应性方面的表现。

Method: 利用预softmax logit空间的信息，将亥姆霍兹自由能作为模型不确定性和样本难度的度量。通过对每个样本的能量分数进行单调变换来重新加权非一致性分数，以提高其对输入难度的敏感性。

Result: 实验表明，这种基于能量的增强方法提高了预测集的适应性，与基线非一致性分数相比，显著提高了效率和适应性，并且没有引入额外的后处理复杂性。

Conclusion: 通过利用预softmax logit空间中的亥姆霍兹自由能重新加权非一致性分数，可以有效提高深度共形分类器预测集的适应性和效率。

Abstract: The merit of Conformal Prediction (CP), as a distribution-free framework for uncertainty quantification, depends on generating prediction sets that are efficient, reflected in small average set sizes, while adaptive, meaning they signal uncertainty by varying in size according to input difficulty. A central limitation for deep conformal classifiers is that the nonconformity scores are derived from softmax outputs, which can be unreliable indicators of how certain the model truly is about a given input, sometimes leading to overconfident misclassifications or undue hesitation. In this work, we argue that this unreliability can be inherited by the prediction sets generated by CP, limiting their capacity for adaptiveness. We propose a new approach that leverages information from the pre-softmax logit space, using the Helmholtz Free Energy as a measure of model uncertainty and sample difficulty. By reweighting nonconformity scores with a monotonic transformation of the energy score of each sample, we improve their sensitivity to input difficulty. Our experiments with four state-of-the-art score functions on multiple datasets and deep architectures show that this energy-based enhancement improves the adaptiveness of the prediction sets, leading to a notable increase in both efficiency and adaptiveness compared to baseline nonconformity scores, without introducing any post-hoc complexity.

</details>


### [268] [A Statistical Approach for Modeling Irregular Multivariate Time Series with Missing Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19531)
*Dingyi Nie,Yixing Wu,C.-C. Jay Kuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种处理不规则多元时间序列缺失值的简单有效方法：提取时间无关的汇总统计数据作为特征，然后与标准分类器结合，在生物医学数据集上取得了最先进的性能，并降低了计算复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则多元时间序列（含缺失值）在医疗保健等领域对预测建模提出了重大挑战。现有的深度学习方法通常侧重于时间插值或复杂的架构来处理不规则性。

Method: 本方法通过提取时间无关的汇总统计数据来消除时间轴。为每个变量计算四个关键特征：观测值的均值和标准差，以及连续观测值之间变化的均值和变异性，以创建固定维度的表示。然后将这些特征与标准分类器（如逻辑回归和XGBoost）一起使用。该方法还探讨了缺失模式本身编码预测信号的场景。

Result: 在四个生物医学数据集（PhysioNet Challenge 2012, 2019, PAMAP2和MIMIC-III）上，该方法实现了最先进的性能，在AUROC/AUPRC方面超越了最近的Transformer和基于图的模型0.5-1.7%，在准确率/F1-score方面超越1.1-1.7%，同时降低了计算复杂度。消融研究表明，特征提取而非分类器选择是性能提升的主要驱动因素，且汇总统计数据在大多数基准测试中优于原始/插值输入。特别是在败血症预测（PhysioNet, 2019）中，缺失指标单独使用XGBoost即可达到94.2%的AUROC，仅比使用原始数据作为输入低1.6%。

Conclusion: 本研究结果挑战了当任务目标允许时间无关表示时复杂时间建模的必要性，为不规则时间序列分类提供了一种高效且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series with missing values present significant challenges for predictive modeling in domains such as healthcare. While deep learning approaches often focus on temporal interpolation or complex architectures to handle irregularities, we propose a simpler yet effective alternative: extracting time-agnostic summary statistics to eliminate the temporal axis. Our method computes four key features per variable-mean and standard deviation of observed values, as well as the mean and variability of changes between consecutive observations to create a fixed-dimensional representation. These features are then utilized with standard classifiers, such as logistic regression and XGBoost. Evaluated on four biomedical datasets (PhysioNet Challenge 2012, 2019, PAMAP2, and MIMIC-III), our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing recent transformer and graph-based models by 0.5-1.7% in AUROC/AUPRC and 1.1-1.7% in accuracy/F1-score, while reducing computational complexity. Ablation studies demonstrate that feature extraction-not classifier choice-drives performance gains, and our summary statistics outperform raw/imputed input in most benchmarks. In particular, we identify scenarios where missing patterns themselves encode predictive signals, as in sepsis prediction (PhysioNet, 2019), where missing indicators alone can achieve 94.2% AUROC with XGBoost, only 1.6% lower than using original raw data as input. Our results challenge the necessity of complex temporal modeling when task objectives permit time-agnostic representations, providing an efficient and interpretable solution for irregular time series classification.

</details>


### [269] [Expanding the Role of Diffusion Models for Robust Classifier Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19931)
*Pin-Han Huang,Shang-Tse Chen,Hsuan-Tien Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了将扩散模型的内部表示作为辅助学习信号引入对抗训练（AT），可以持续提高图像分类器的鲁棒性，并促进更解耦的特征，与扩散生成的合成数据互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗训练中，扩散模型生成的合成数据已被证明能显著提升图像分类器的鲁棒性。本文旨在探索扩散模型的内部表示是否能为鲁棒分类器训练带来额外益处，超越仅仅生成合成数据的应用。

Method: 通过系统实验，研究人员证明了扩散模型提供了多样且部分鲁棒的表示。他们将扩散表示作为对抗训练中的辅助学习信号明确引入，并进行了表示分析以研究其对特征解耦的影响。

Result: 1. 在对抗训练中明确引入扩散表示作为辅助学习信号，能够持续提高模型鲁棒性。2. 表示分析表明，将扩散模型整合到对抗训练中能促进更解耦的特征。3. 扩散表示和扩散生成的合成数据在塑造表示方面发挥互补作用。4. 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet上的实验验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 在对抗训练中联合利用扩散表示和合成数据是有效的。扩散模型的内部表示作为辅助学习信号，能够提供多样且部分鲁棒的特征，从而增强模型鲁棒性并促进特征解耦。

Abstract: Incorporating diffusion-generated synthetic data into adversarial training (AT) has been shown to substantially improve the training of robust image classifiers. In this work, we extend the role of diffusion models beyond merely generating synthetic data, examining whether their internal representations, which encode meaningful features of the data, can provide additional benefits for robust classifier training. Through systematic experiments, we show that diffusion models offer representations that are both diverse and partially robust, and that explicitly incorporating diffusion representations as an auxiliary learning signal during AT consistently improves robustness across settings. Furthermore, our representation analysis indicates that incorporating diffusion models into AT encourages more disentangled features, while diffusion representations and diffusion-generated synthetic data play complementary roles in shaping representations. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet validate these findings, demonstrating the effectiveness of jointly leveraging diffusion representations and synthetic data within AT.

</details>


### [270] [VecFormer: Towards Efficient and Generalizable Graph Transformer with Graph Token Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19622)
*Jingbo Zhou,Jun Xia,Siyuan Li,Yunfan Liu,Wenjun Wang,Yufei Huang,Changxi Chi,Mutian Hong,Zhuoli Ouyang,Shu Wang,Zhongqi Wang,Xingyu Wu,Chang Yu,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VecFormer是一种新的图Transformer模型，它通过矢量量化和两阶段训练范式解决了现有模型的可扩展性和泛化性问题，在节点分类任务中，特别是在OOD设置下，表现出更好的性能和速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图Transformer模型面临两大挑战：1) 计算复杂度呈指数级增长，难以扩展到大型图；2) 基于节点级操作的注意力机制限制了模型的灵活性，导致在分布外（OOD）场景中泛化性能不佳。

Method: 提出VecFormer（矢量量化图Transformer），采用两阶段训练范式：第一阶段，使用两个码本重建节点特征和图结构，以学习丰富的语义“图编码”（Graph Codes）；第二阶段，基于转换后的跨码本，在“图Token”（Graph Token）级别执行注意力机制，从而降低计算复杂度并增强模型泛化能力。

Result: 在各种规模的数据集上进行的大量实验表明，VecFormer在性能和速度上均优于现有的图Transformer模型，尤其适用于OOD设置下的节点分类任务。

Conclusion: VecFormer通过引入矢量量化和两阶段训练范式，有效解决了图Transformer模型在计算复杂度和泛化能力方面的挑战，使其更加高效和通用。

Abstract: Graph Transformer has demonstrated impressive capabilities in the field of graph representation learning. However, existing approaches face two critical challenges: (1) most models suffer from exponentially increasing computational complexity, making it difficult to scale to large graphs; (2) attention mechanisms based on node-level operations limit the flexibility of the model and result in poor generalization performance in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{VecFormer} (the \textbf{Vec}tor Quantized Graph Trans\textbf{former}), an efficient and highly generalizable model for node classification, particularly under OOD settings. VecFormer adopts a two-stage training paradigm. In the first stage, two codebooks are used to reconstruct the node features and the graph structure, aiming to learn the rich semantic \texttt{Graph Codes}. In the second stage, attention mechanisms are performed at the \texttt{Graph Token} level based on the transformed cross codebook, reducing computational complexity while enhancing the model's generalization capability. Extensive experiments on datasets of various sizes demonstrate that VecFormer outperforms the existing Graph Transformer in both performance and speed.

</details>


### [271] [Compositional Planning with Jumpy World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19634)
*Jesse Farebrother,Matteo Pirotta,Andrea Tirinzoni,Marc G. Bellemare,Alessandro Lazaric,Ahmed Touati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过学习能够捕捉多时间尺度状态占用的跳跃式世界模型，并结合新型一致性目标来提高长程预测精度，从而实现策略组合规划。实验结果显示，在挑战性任务上，其零样本性能比使用原始动作的规划方法平均提升200%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能决策的核心在于利用时间抽象进行规划。传统方法难以解决复杂任务，因为组合预训练策略进行长程预测时，误差会累积，导致难以估计策略序列引起的状态访问分布。

Method: 受到几何策略组合框架的启发，本文提出学习多步动态的预测模型，即“跳跃式世界模型”，以离线方式捕捉预训练策略在多个时间尺度上引起的状态占用。在此基础上，结合“时序差分流”，引入一个新颖的一致性目标，以对齐跨时间尺度的预测，从而提高长程预测精度。进一步展示了如何结合这些生成预测来估计在不同时间尺度上执行任意策略序列的价值。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用跳跃式世界模型的组合规划在各种基础策略的挑战性操作和导航任务上显著提升了零样本性能，在长程任务上比使用原始动作的规划方法平均相对提高了200%。

Conclusion: 通过跳跃式世界模型和一致性目标，本文成功克服了组合规划中长程预测的挑战，显著提升了复杂任务的零样本规划性能，验证了其在智能决策中的有效性。

Abstract: The ability to plan with temporal abstractions is central to intelligent decision-making. Rather than reasoning over primitive actions, we study agents that compose pre-trained policies as temporally extended actions, enabling solutions to complex tasks that no constituent alone can solve. Such compositional planning remains elusive as compounding errors in long-horizon predictions make it challenging to estimate the visitation distribution induced by sequencing policies. Motivated by the geometric policy composition framework introduced in , we address these challenges by learning predictive models of multi-step dynamics -- so-called jumpy world models -- that capture state occupancies induced by pre-trained policies across multiple timescales in an off-policy manner. Building on Temporal Difference Flows ( ), we enhance these models with a novel consistency objective that aligns predictions across timescales, improving long-horizon predictive accuracy. We further demonstrate how to combine these generative predictions to estimate the value of executing arbitrary sequences of policies over varying timescales. Empirically, we find that compositional planning with jumpy world models significantly improves zero-shot performance across a wide range of base policies on challenging manipulation and navigation tasks, yielding, on average, a 200% relative improvement over planning with primitive actions on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [272] [NEXUS : A compact neural architecture for high-resolution spatiotemporal air quality forecasting in Delhi Nationa Capital Region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19654)
*Rampunit Kumar,Aditya Maheshwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NEXUS架构以显著优于现有模型（SCINet、Autoformer、FEDformer）的参数效率和预测性能，对德里国家首都区域的CO、NO和SO2进行时空预测，并揭示了复杂的污染模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特大城市（尤其是德里国家首都区域）的城市空气污染对公众健康构成严峻挑战，影响数百万人。

Method: 提出NEXUS（Neural Extraction and Unified Spatiotemporal）架构，该架构整合了补丁嵌入、低秩投影和自适应融合机制，利用2018-2021年四年间十六个空间网格的大气数据进行预测。

Result: NEXUS在CO、NO和SO2预测中R²分别超过0.94、0.91和0.95，仅使用18,748个参数，远少于其他模型。研究揭示了明显的日变化节律和季节性变化，冬季污染严重与逆温和农业生物质燃烧有关。分析还确定了关键气象阈值，量化了风场对污染物扩散的影响，并绘制了区域内的空间异质性。

Conclusion: NEXUS以卓越的预测性能和计算效率，为空气质量监测系统的实时部署提供了可能。

Abstract: Urban air pollution in megacities poses critical public health challenges, particularly in Delhi National Capital Region (NCR) where severe degradation affects millions. We present NEXUS (Neural Extraction and Unified Spatiotemporal) architecture for forecasting carbon monoxide, nitrogen oxide, and sulfur dioxide. Working with four years (2018--2021) of atmospheric data across sixteen spatial grids, NEXUS achieves R$^2$ exceeding 0.94 for CO, 0.91 for NO, and 0.95 for SO$_2$ using merely 18,748 parameters -- substantially fewer than SCINet (35,552), Autoformer (68,704), and FEDformer (298,080). The architecture integrates patch embedding, low-rank projections, and adaptive fusion mechanisms to decode complex atmospheric chemistry patterns. Our investigation uncovers distinct diurnal rhythms and pronounced seasonal variations, with winter months experiencing severe pollution episodes driven by temperature inversions and agricultural biomass burning. Analysis identifies critical meteorological thresholds, quantifies wind field impacts on pollutant dispersion, and maps spatial heterogeneity across the region. Extensive ablation experiments demonstrate each architectural component's role. NEXUS delivers superior predictive performance with remarkable computational efficiency, enabling real-time deployment for air quality monitoring systems.

</details>


### [273] [DP-FedAdamW: An Efficient Optimizer for Differentially Private Federated Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19945)
*Jin Liu,Yinbin Miao,Ning Xi,Junkang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DP-FedAdamW是一种新型的基于AdamW的优化器，用于解决差分隐私联邦学习（DPFL）中AdamW的收敛效率和鲁棒性问题。它通过稳定二阶矩方差、消除DP引起的偏差以及调整局部更新来抑制客户端漂移，从而在语言和视觉模型上实现了优于SOTA的性能，并提供了更严格的隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习（FL）中，平衡差分隐私（DP）下的收敛效率和鲁棒性是一个核心挑战。将AdamW直接应用于差分隐私联邦学习（DPFL）会遇到三个主要问题：（i）数据异质性和隐私噪声共同放大了二阶矩估计器的方差，（ii）DP扰动导致二阶矩估计器产生偏差，以及（iii）DP放大了AdamW对局部过拟合的敏感性，从而加剧了客户端漂移。

Method: 本文提出了DP-FedAdamW，这是第一个用于DPFL的基于AdamW的优化器。它通过稳定二阶矩方差、消除DP引起的偏差以及调整局部更新以适应全局下降来抑制客户端漂移，从而在DP下恢复了AdamW的性能。在理论上，它建立了一个无偏的二阶矩估计器，并在没有任何异质性假设的情况下证明了线性加速收敛速率，同时提供了更严格的$(\varepsilon,\delta)$-DP保证。

Result: 经验结果表明，DP-FedAdamW在语言和视觉Transformer以及ResNet-18模型上均表现出有效性。在Tiny-ImageNet数据集上（Swin-Base, $\varepsilon=1$），DP-FedAdamW的性能比现有最优（SOTA）方法高出5.83%。

Conclusion: DP-FedAdamW成功解决了在差分隐私联邦学习中应用AdamW所面临的挑战，显著提高了模型的性能和隐私保护水平。

Abstract: Balancing convergence efficiency and robustness under Differential Privacy (DP) is a central challenge in Federated Learning (FL). While AdamW accelerates training and fine-tuning in large-scale models, we find that directly applying it to Differentially Private FL (DPFL) suffers from three major issues: (i) data heterogeneity and privacy noise jointly amplify the variance of second-moment estimator, (ii) DP perturbations bias the second-moment estimator, and (iii) DP amplify AdamW sensitivity to local overfitting, worsening client drift. We propose DP-FedAdamW, the first AdamW-based optimizer for DPFL. It restores AdamW under DP by stabilizing second-moment variance, removing DP-induced bias, and aligning local updates to the global descent to curb client drift. Theoretically, we establish an unbiased second-moment estimator and prove a linearly accelerated convergence rate without any heterogeneity assumption, while providing tighter $(\varepsilon,\delta)$-DP guarantees. Our empirical results demonstrate the effectiveness of DP-FedAdamW across language and vision Transformers and ResNet-18. On Tiny-ImageNet (Swin-Base, $\varepsilon=1$), DP-FedAdamW outperforms the state-of-the-art (SOTA) by 5.83\%. The code is available in Appendix.

</details>


### [274] [BarrierSteer: LLM Safety via Learning Barrier Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20102)
*Thanh Q. Tran,Arun Verma,Kiwan Wong,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Daniela Rus,Wei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BarrierSteer是一个新颖的框架，通过在大型语言模型(LLM)的潜在表示空间中嵌入学习到的非线性安全约束，并利用基于控制障碍函数(CBF)的转向机制，在推理过程中高效检测并防止不安全的响应轨迹，从而显著降低对抗性攻击成功率和不安全内容生成，同时保持模型原始能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)尽管在各种任务中表现出色，但其易受对抗性攻击和生成不安全内容的特性严重阻碍了其部署，尤其是在高风险场景中。因此，需要开发既实用有效又具有严格理论支持的安全机制。

Method: BarrierSteer通过将学习到的非线性安全约束直接嵌入到模型的潜在表示空间中，从而规范响应安全性。它采用基于控制障碍函数(CBF)的转向机制，在推理过程中高效、高精度地检测并防止不安全的响应轨迹。通过高效的约束合并，BarrierSteer可以在不修改底层LLM参数的情况下强制执行多个安全约束，从而保留模型原始能力和性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，BarrierSteer显著降低了对抗性攻击的成功率，减少了不安全内容的生成，并超越了现有方法。理论结果也证实，在潜在空间应用CBF提供了一种原则性且计算高效的强制执行安全的方法。

Conclusion: BarrierSteer框架通过在LLM潜在空间中实施基于CBF的安全约束，为解决LLM的不安全内容生成问题提供了一种有效且理论严谨的解决方案，能够在不影响模型原始性能的情况下显著提升安全性。

Abstract: Despite the state-of-the-art performance of large language models (LLMs) across diverse tasks, their susceptibility to adversarial attacks and unsafe content generation remains a major obstacle to deployment, particularly in high-stakes settings. Addressing this challenge requires safety mechanisms that are both practically effective and supported by rigorous theory. We introduce BarrierSteer, a novel framework that formalizes response safety by embedding learned non-linear safety constraints directly into the model's latent representation space. BarrierSteer employs a steering mechanism based on Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) to efficiently detect and prevent unsafe response trajectories during inference with high precision. By enforcing multiple safety constraints through efficient constraint merging, without modifying the underlying LLM parameters, BarrierSteer preserves the model's original capabilities and performance. We provide theoretical results establishing that applying CBFs in latent space offers a principled and computationally efficient approach to enforcing safety. Our experiments across multiple models and datasets show that BarrierSteer substantially reduces adversarial success rates, decreases unsafe generations, and outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [275] [Behavior Learning (BL): Learning Hierarchical Optimization Structures from Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20152)
*Zhenyao Ma,Yue Liang,Dongxu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 行为学习（BL）是一种受行为科学启发的新型通用机器学习框架，能够从数据中学习可解释和可识别的优化结构，涵盖从单一优化问题到分层组合。它统一了预测性能、内在可解释性和可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在提出一种新的机器学习框架，能够从数据中学习可解释和可识别的优化结构，并统一预测性能、内在可解释性和可识别性，适用于涉及优化的科学领域。

Method: 行为学习（BL）通过参数化由内在可解释模块化块构建的组合效用函数来工作，每个块代表一个效用最大化问题（UMP）。BL支持从单一UMP到分层组合的架构。其平滑单调变体（IBL）保证了可识别性。理论上，论文建立了BL的通用逼近性质，并分析了IBL的M估计性质。

Result: BL在经验上表现出强大的预测性能、内在可解释性以及对高维数据的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 行为学习（BL）是一个创新的机器学习框架，它通过学习可解释和可识别的优化结构，成功地统一了预测性能、内在可解释性和可识别性。它具有理论基础和强大的实证表现，适用于广泛的科学领域。

Abstract: Inspired by behavioral science, we propose Behavior Learning (BL), a novel general-purpose machine learning framework that learns interpretable and identifiable optimization structures from data, ranging from single optimization problems to hierarchical compositions. It unifies predictive performance, intrinsic interpretability, and identifiability, with broad applicability to scientific domains involving optimization. BL parameterizes a compositional utility function built from intrinsically interpretable modular blocks, which induces a data distribution for prediction and generation. Each block represents and can be written in symbolic form as a utility maximization problem (UMP), a foundational paradigm in behavioral science and a universal framework of optimization. BL supports architectures ranging from a single UMP to hierarchical compositions, the latter modeling hierarchical optimization structures. Its smooth and monotone variant (IBL) guarantees identifiability. Theoretically, we establish the universal approximation property of BL, and analyze the M-estimation properties of IBL. Empirically, BL demonstrates strong predictive performance, intrinsic interpretability and scalability to high-dimensional data. Code: ; install via pip install blnetwork.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [276] [Structured Bitmap-to-Mesh Triangulation for Geometry-Aware Discretization of Image-Derived Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19474)
*Wei Feng,Haiyong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种模板驱动的三角剖分框架，用于将图像导出的边界嵌入规则三角形网格中，以实现图像导出域上稳定的偏微分方程离散化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的约束Delaunay三角剖分(CDT)可能触发全局连通性更新，而该方法旨在提供一种更稳定、高效的偏微分方程离散化方法，特别是在图像导出的域上。

Method: 该方法仅对与边界相交的三角形进行重新三角剖分，保留了基础网格，并支持无同步并行执行。它通过对所有局部边界交集配置进行分类，生成一个有限的符号查找表，将每个情况映射到无冲突的重新三角剖分模板。

Result: 结果网格是闭合的，具有有界角度，并且与基于余切的离散化和标准有限元方法兼容。实验表明，该方法减少了狭长单元，产生了更规则的三角形，并改善了复杂边界附近的几何保真度。

Conclusion: 该框架非常适合在图像导出域上进行实时几何分析和基于物理的模拟。

Abstract: We propose a template-driven triangulation framework that embeds raster- or segmentation-derived boundaries into a regular triangular grid for stable PDE discretization on image-derived domains. Unlike constrained Delaunay triangulation (CDT), which may trigger global connectivity updates, our method retriangulates only triangles intersected by the boundary, preserves the base mesh, and supports synchronization-free parallel execution. To ensure determinism and scalability, we classify all local boundary-intersection configurations up to discrete equivalence and triangle symmetries, yielding a finite symbolic lookup table that maps each case to a conflict-free retriangulation template. We prove that the resulting mesh is closed, has bounded angles, and is compatible with cotangent-based discretizations and standard finite element methods. Experiments on elliptic and parabolic PDEs, signal interpolation, and structural metrics show fewer sliver elements, more regular triangles, and improved geometric fidelity near complex boundaries. The framework is well suited for real-time geometric analysis and physically based simulation over image-derived domains.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [Continuous Telemonitoring of Heart Failure using Personalised Speech Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19674)
*Yue Pan,Xingyao Wang,Hanyue Zhang,Liwei Liu,Changxin Li,Gang Yang,Rong Sheng,Yili Xia,Ming Chu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种纵向患者内追踪（LIPT）方案和个性化序列编码器（PSE），用于通过语音信号远程监测心力衰竭，显著优于传统方法，在识别临床状态转变方面达到99.7%的准确率，并能有效预测心衰恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于语音信号的远程心力衰竭监测的横断面分类模型，由于个体间语音特征的显著异质性，准确性有限。因此，需要一种能够捕捉个体内部相对症状变化轨迹的解决方案。

Method: 该研究提出了一种纵向患者内追踪（LIPT）方案。其核心是一个个性化序列编码器（PSE），它将纵向语音记录转换为上下文感知的潜在表示。PSE在每个时间戳整合历史数据，以实现对临床轨迹的整体评估。该研究还全面分析了不同的语音任务设计和声学特征。

Result: 对225名患者的实验结果表明，LIPT范式显著优于经典的横断面方法，在临床状态转变识别方面实现了99.7%的准确率。模型的预测心衰恶化的高灵敏度得到了额外随访数据的证实。

Conclusion: LIPT框架和PSE架构表现出卓越的性能，并已准备好整合到长期远程监测系统中，为远程心力衰竭管理提供可扩展的解决方案，并确保患者安全。该工作还通过对不同语音任务设计和声学特征的全面分析弥补了现有文献的空白。

Abstract: Remote monitoring of heart failure (HF) via speech signals provides a non-invasive and cost-effective solution for long-term patient management. However, substantial inter-individual heterogeneity in vocal characteristics often limits the accuracy of traditional cross-sectional classification models. To address this, we propose a Longitudinal Intra-Patient Tracking (LIPT) scheme designed to capture the trajectory of relative symptomatic changes within individuals. Central to this framework is a Personalised Sequential Encoder (PSE), which transforms longitudinal speech recordings into context-aware latent representations. By incorporating historical data at each timestamp, the PSE facilitates a holistic assessment of the clinical trajectory rather than modelling discrete visits independently. Experimental results from a cohort of 225 patients demonstrate that the LIPT paradigm significantly outperforms the classic cross-sectional approaches, achieving a recognition accuracy of 99.7% for clinical status transitions. The model's high sensitivity was further corroborated by additional follow-up data, confirming its efficacy in predicting HF deterioration and its potential to secure patient safety in remote, home-based settings. Furthermore, this work addresses the gap in existing literature by providing a comprehensive analysis of different speech task designs and acoustic features. Taken together, the superior performance of the LIPT framework and PSE architecture validates their readiness for integration into long-term telemonitoring systems, offering a scalable solution for remote heart failure management.

</details>


### [278] [StyleStream: Real-Time Zero-Shot Voice Style Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20113)
*Yisi Liu,Nicholas Lee,Gopala Anumanchipalli*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: StyleStream是一种实时、零样本的语音风格转换系统，通过解耦语言内容和风格，实现了最先进的性能，并支持实时转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音风格转换的核心挑战是语言内容与风格的解耦。现有工作在转换质量上仍有限制，且未能解决实时语音风格转换问题。

Method: StyleStream包含一个去风格器（Destylizer），用于去除风格属性同时保留语言内容；以及一个风格器（Stylizer），这是一个扩散变换器（DiT），根据参考语音重新引入目标风格。通过文本监督和高度受限的信息瓶颈来强制实现鲁棒的内容-风格解耦。这种设计实现了一个完全非自回归的架构。

Result: 实现了实时语音风格转换，端到端延迟为1秒，并达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: StyleStream是首个可流式传输的零样本语音风格转换系统，实现了最先进的性能和实时语音风格转换。

Abstract: Voice style conversion aims to transform an input utterance to match a target speaker's timbre, accent, and emotion, with a central challenge being the disentanglement of linguistic content from style. While prior work has explored this problem, conversion quality remains limited, and real-time voice style conversion has not been addressed. We propose StyleStream, the first streamable zero-shot voice style conversion system that achieves state-of-the-art performance. StyleStream consists of two components: a Destylizer, which removes style attributes while preserving linguistic content, and a Stylizer, a diffusion transformer (DiT) that reintroduces target style conditioned on reference speech. Robust content-style disentanglement is enforced through text supervision and a highly constrained information bottleneck. This design enables a fully non-autoregressive architecture, achieving real-time voice style conversion with an end-to-end latency of 1 second. Samples and real-time demo: .

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [279] [RPU -- A Reasoning Processing Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18568)
*Matthew Adiletta,Gu-Yeon Wei,David Brooks*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 大型语言模型 (LLM) 推理性能受内存墙瓶颈限制，特别是在推理LLM应用中对内存带宽的需求更高。RPU（推理处理单元）是一种基于小芯片的架构，通过容量优化的高带宽内存 (HBM-CO)、可扩展的小芯片架构和解耦微架构来解决这些挑战。模拟结果显示，RPU 在 Llama3-405B 上比 H100 系统具有高达 45.3 倍的低延迟和 18.6 倍的高吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型 (LLM) 推理性能日益受到内存墙的瓶颈限制。尽管 GPU 计算吞吐量不断提高，但难以满足内存带宽受限的工作负载的需求。新兴的推理型 LLM 应用（具有长输出序列、低算术强度和严格的延迟约束）对内存带宽的需求更高，导致系统利用率下降和每次推理的能耗增加，凸显了对可扩展内存带宽进行优化的系统架构的需求。

Method: 本文提出了推理处理单元 (RPU)，这是一种基于小芯片的架构，旨在解决现代内存墙的挑战。RPU 引入了：(1) 容量优化的高带宽内存 (HBM-CO)，通过牺牲容量来降低能耗和成本；(2) 一种可扩展的小芯片架构，其功率和面积配置优先考虑带宽；(3) 一种解耦的微架构，将内存、计算和通信管道分离，以维持高带宽利用率。

Result: 模拟结果显示，在 ISO-TDP 下，RPU 在 Llama3-405B 上比 H100 系统具有高达 45.3 倍的低延迟和 18.6 倍的高吞吐量。

Conclusion: RPU 是一种专门为解决 LLM 推理中的内存带宽瓶颈而设计的系统架构，通过其创新的 HBM-CO、可扩展小芯片和解耦微架构，显著提高了性能，降低了延迟并增加了吞吐量，从而优化了推理 LLM 应用的效率和能耗。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference performance is increasingly bottlenecked by the memory wall. While GPUs continue to scale raw compute throughput, they struggle to deliver scalable performance for memory bandwidth bound workloads. This challenge is amplified by emerging reasoning LLM applications, where long output sequences, low arithmetic intensity, and tight latency constraints demand significantly higher memory bandwidth. As a result, system utilization drops and energy per inference rises, highlighting the need for an optimized system architecture for scalable memory bandwidth. To address these challenges we present the Reasoning Processing Unit (RPU), a chiplet-based architecture designed to address the challenges of the modern memory wall. RPU introduces: (1) A Capacity-Optimized High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM-CO) that trades capacity for lower energy and cost; (2) a scalable chiplet architecture featuring a bandwidth-first power and area provisioning design; and (3) a decoupled microarchitecture that separates memory, compute, and communication pipelines to sustain high bandwidth utilization. Simulation results show that RPU performs up to 45.3x lower latency and 18.6x higher throughput over an H100 system at ISO-TDP on Llama3-405B.

</details>


### [280] [CORVET: A CORDIC-Powered, Resource-Frugal Mixed-Precision Vector Processing Engine for High-Throughput AIoT applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19268)
*Sonu Kumar,Mohd Faisal Khan,Mukul Lokhande,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该简报提出了一种运行时自适应、性能增强的向量引擎，其特点是采用低资源、迭代式CORDIC基MAC单元，用于边缘AI加速，支持近似和精确模式间的动态重新配置，利用延迟-精度权衡，并实现高达4倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为边缘AI加速提供一个运行时自适应、性能增强的向量引擎，该引擎能够利用延迟-精度权衡，并在有限硬件资源内提供高吞吐量。

Method: 该设计采用低资源、迭代式CORDIC基MAC单元；支持近似和精确模式间的动态重新配置；通过向量化、时分复用执行和灵活精度缩放，实现高达4倍的吞吐量提升；包含时分复用多AF模块和轻量级池化与归一化单元，支持灵活精度（4/8/16位）和高MAC密度；通过ASIC实现和在Pynq-Z2上进行目标检测与分类任务的软硬件协同设计方法进行评估。

Result: 每个MAC级可节省高达33%的时间和21%的功耗。一个256-PE配置实现了比现有最先进工作更高的计算密度（4.83 TOPS/mm2）和能效（11.67 TOPS/W）。展示了边缘AI应用的可扩展、高能效解决方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的运行时自适应向量引擎，采用CORDIC基MAC单元，为边缘AI加速提供了一个可扩展、高能效、性能增强的解决方案，在计算密度和能效方面优于现有最先进技术。

Abstract: This brief presents a runtime-adaptive, performance-enhanced vector engine featuring a low-resource, iterative CORDIC-based MAC unit for edge AI acceleration. The proposed design enables dynamic reconfiguration between approximate and accurate modes, exploiting the latency-accuracy trade-off for a wide range of workloads. Its resource-efficient approach further enables up to 4x throughput improvement within the same hardware resources by leveraging vectorised, time-multiplexed execution and flexible precision scaling. With a time-multiplexed multi-AF block and a lightweight pooling and normalisation unit, the proposed vector engine supports flexible precision (4/8/16-bit) and high MAC density. The ASIC implementation results show that each MAC stage can save up to 33% of time and 21% of power, with a 256-PE configuration that achieves higher compute density (4.83 TOPS/mm2 ) and energy efficiency (11.67 TOPS/W) than previous state-of-the-art work. A detailed hardware-software co-design methodology for object detection and classification tasks on Pynq-Z2 is discussed to assess the proposed architecture, demonstrating a scalable, energy-efficient solution for edge AI applications.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [DM4CT: Benchmarking Diffusion Models for Computed Tomography Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18589)
*Jiayang Shi,Daniel M. Pelt,K. Joost Batenburg*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 扩散模型在逆问题中表现出色，但CT重建面临独特挑战。本文介绍了DM4CT，一个全面的CT重建基准，用于系统评估扩散模型，并与现有方法进行比较，包括在真实世界高分辨率CT数据集上的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型是解决逆问题的强大先验。然而，计算机断层扫描（CT）虽然理论上是线性逆问题，但存在相关噪声、伪影结构、对系统几何形状的依赖以及值范围未对准等实际挑战，使得扩散模型难以直接应用。因此，需要系统评估扩散模型在此背景下的性能，并与现有重建方法进行比较。

Method: 本文引入了DM4CT，一个用于CT重建的综合基准。DM4CT包含来自医学和工业领域的稀疏视图和噪声配置数据集。此外，作者获取了一个高分辨率CT数据集，并在真实实验条件下评估所有方法。基准测试了十种最新的基于扩散的方法以及七种强大的基线方法（包括基于模型、无监督和有监督方法）。

Result: 分析提供了关于扩散模型在CT重建中的行为、优势和局限性的详细见解。真实世界数据集和代码库已公开发布。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入DM4CT基准，系统地评估了扩散模型在CT重建中的应用，解决了实际挑战，并与多种现有方法进行了比较，提供了深入的见解和可公开访问的资源。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently emerged as powerful priors for solving inverse problems. While computed tomography (CT) is theoretically a linear inverse problem, it poses many practical challenges. These include correlated noise, artifact structures, reliance on system geometry, and misaligned value ranges, which make the direct application of diffusion models more difficult than in domains like natural image generation. To systematically evaluate how diffusion models perform in this context and compare them with established reconstruction methods, we introduce DM4CT, a comprehensive benchmark for CT reconstruction. DM4CT includes datasets from both medical and industrial domains with sparse-view and noisy configurations. To explore the challenges of deploying diffusion models in practice, we additionally acquire a high-resolution CT dataset at a high-energy synchrotron facility and evaluate all methods under real experimental conditions. We benchmark ten recent diffusion-based methods alongside seven strong baselines, including model-based, unsupervised, and supervised approaches. Our analysis provides detailed insights into the behavior, strengths, and limitations of diffusion models for CT reconstruction. The real-world dataset is publicly available at , and the codebase is open-sourced at .

</details>


### [282] [Triggering hallucinations in model-based MRI reconstruction via adversarial perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18536)
*Suna Buğday,Yvan Saeys,Jonathan Peck*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 生成模型在医学成像领域易受幻觉影响，可能导致误诊。本研究量化了磁共振图像重建中生成模型的这种脆弱性，发现UNet和VarNet等模型易受微小扰动诱发幻觉，且传统图像质量指标无法可靠检测。这表明需要开发新的检测方法和对抗性训练来减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在提高医学成像质量方面日益普及，但它们容易产生幻觉，即在重建图像中插入原始图像中不存在的特征。在医疗环境中，这种幻觉可能导致不正确的诊断，从而危及患者健康。

Method: 本研究通过为未处理的输入图像制作类似于随机噪声的对抗性扰动，诱导生成模型在磁共振图像重建时产生幻觉。评估在fastMRI数据集的脑部和膝关节图像上进行，使用UNet和端到端VarNet架构进行图像重建。

Result: 研究结果表明，这些模型（UNet和VarNet）极易受到微小扰动的影响，并且很容易被诱导产生幻觉。此外，传统图像质量指标无法可靠地检测到这些幻觉。

Conclusion: 生成模型对小扰动的脆弱性可能部分解释了幻觉的发生，并表明精心构建的对抗性训练程序可能会降低其普遍性。鉴于传统图像质量指标无法可靠检测幻觉，因此需要开发新的方法来检测幻觉的发生。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly used to improve the quality of medical imaging, such as reconstruction of magnetic resonance images and computed tomography. However, it is well-known that such models are susceptible to hallucinations: they may insert features into the reconstructed image which are not actually present in the original image. In a medical setting, such hallucinations may endanger patient health as they can lead to incorrect diagnoses. In this work, we aim to quantify the extent to which state-of-the-art generative models suffer from hallucinations in the context of magnetic resonance image reconstruction. Specifically, we craft adversarial perturbations resembling random noise for the unprocessed input images which induce hallucinations when reconstructed using a generative model. We perform this evaluation on the brain and knee images from the fastMRI data set using UNet and end-to-end VarNet architectures to reconstruct the images. Our results show that these models are highly susceptible to small perturbations and can be easily coaxed into producing hallucinations. This fragility may partially explain why hallucinations occur in the first place and suggests that a carefully constructed adversarial training routine may reduce their prevalence. Moreover, these hallucinations cannot be reliably detected using traditional image quality metrics. Novel approaches will therefore need to be developed to detect when hallucinations have occurred.

</details>


### [283] [TIACam: Text-Anchored Invariant Feature Learning with Auto-Augmentation for Camera-Robust Zero-Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18863)
*Abdullah All Tanvir,Agnibh Dasgupta,Xin Zhong*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: TIACam是一个用于相机鲁棒零水印的文本锚定不变特征学习框架，通过自增强和跨模态对齐有效应对了相机重捕获带来的复杂光学降级，并实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度水印系统在应对相机重捕获引入的复杂光学降级（如透视扭曲、光照变化和摩尔纹干扰）时仍面临挑战。

Method: 论文提出了TIACam框架，包含三个关键创新点：1) 一个可学习的自增强器，通过可微分的几何、光度学和摩尔纹算子发现类似相机的畸变；2) 一个文本锚定不变特征学习器，通过图像和文本之间的跨模态对抗性对齐来强制执行语义一致性；3) 一个零水印头部，在不变特征空间中绑定二进制消息而不修改图像像素。该统一公式共同优化了不变性、语义对齐和水印可恢复性。

Result: 在合成和真实世界相机捕获上的大量实验表明，TIACam实现了最先进的特征稳定性和水印提取精度。

Conclusion: TIACam在多模态不变性学习和物理鲁棒零水印之间建立了一座有原则的桥梁。

Abstract: Camera recapture introduces complex optical degradations, such as perspective warping, illumination shifts, and Moiré interference, that remain challenging for deep watermarking systems. We present TIACam, a text-anchored invariant feature learning framework with auto-augmentation for camera-robust zero-watermarking. The method integrates three key innovations: (1) a learnable auto-augmentor that discovers camera-like distortions through differentiable geometric, photometric, and Moiré operators; (2) a text-anchored invariant feature learner that enforces semantic consistency via cross-modal adversarial alignment between image and text; and (3) a zero-watermarking head that binds binary messages in the invariant feature space without modifying image pixels. This unified formulation jointly optimizes invariance, semantic alignment, and watermark recoverability. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world camera captures demonstrate that TIACam achieves state-of-the-art feature stability and watermark extraction accuracy, establishing a principled bridge between multimodal invariance learning and physically robust zero-watermarking.

</details>


### [284] [Automated Disentangling Analysis of Skin Colour for Lesion Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19055)
*Wenbo Yang,Eman Rezk,Walaa M. Moursi,Zhou Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个皮肤颜色解缠框架，旨在解决机器学习模型在皮肤图像上因训练和部署之间捕获的皮肤颜色（SCCI）差异而导致的性能下降问题。该框架通过适应压缩解缠技术，从无标签皮肤病图像中学习SCCI的结构化、可操作的潜在空间，并引入随机去色映射和几何对齐后处理步骤，实现了忠实的反事实编辑、颜色迁移和受控的方向遍历。基于此框架的数据集级增强和颜色归一化在病变分类方面取得了有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在处理皮肤图像时，当训练和部署阶段捕获的皮肤颜色（SCCI）存在差异时，性能会下降。这种差异源于环境因素（如光照、相机设置）和内在因素（如肤色）的纠缠，且无法用单一的“肤色”标量准确描述。因此，需要缓解这种颜色不匹配问题。

Method: 提出了一种皮肤颜色解缠框架，该框架：1. 适应“通过压缩解缠”技术，从无标签皮肤病图像中学习SCCI的结构化、可操作的潜在空间。2. 引入了一种随机的、基本单调的去色映射，以防止信息泄露，确保正确学习深色特征。3. 提出了一种几何对齐的后处理步骤，以抑制颜色操作过程中局部图案（如墨迹、疤痕）意外的颜色偏移。

Result: 该框架能够实现忠实的反事实编辑（例如，“在不同的SCCI下，这种皮肤状况会是什么样子？”），以及图像间的直接颜色迁移和沿物理有意义方向（例如，血液灌注、相机白平衡）的受控遍历。这还有助于在不同SCCI下进行皮肤状况的教育可视化。基于该框架的数据集级增强和颜色归一化实现了有竞争力的病变分类性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的皮肤颜色解缠框架有效地缓解了皮肤图像分析中的颜色不匹配问题。它不仅支持先进的图像编辑和颜色转换功能，还能通过数据增强和颜色归一化显著提升机器学习模型在病变分类任务中的性能。

Abstract: Machine-learning models working on skin images often have degraded performance when the skin colour captured in images (SCCI) differs between training and deployment. Such differences arise from entangled environmental factors (e.g., illumination, camera settings), and intrinsic factors (e.g., skin tone) that cannot be accurately described by a single "skin tone" scalar. To mitigate such colour mismatch, we propose a skin-colour disentangling framework that adapts disentanglement-by-compression to learn a structured, manipulable latent space for SCCI from unlabelled dermatology images. To prevent information leakage that hinders proper learning of dark colour features, we introduce a randomized, mostly monotonic decolourization mapping. To suppress unintended colour shifts of localized patterns (e.g., ink marks, scars) during colour manipulation, we further propose a geometry-aligned post-processing step. Together, these components enable faithful counterfactual editing and answering an essential question: "What would this skin condition look like under a different SCCI?", as well as direct colour transfer between images and controlled traversal along physically meaningful directions (e.g., blood perfusion, camera white balance), enabling educational visualization of skin conditions under varying SCCI. We demonstrate that dataset-level augmentation and colour normalization based on our framework achieve competitive lesion classification performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [285] [1D-Bench: A Benchmark for Iterative UI Code Generation with Visual Feedback in Real-World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18548)
*Qiao Xu,Yipeng Yu,Chengxiao Feng,Xu Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了1D-Bench，这是一个新的设计到代码基准，用于将高保真UI设计转换为可执行的React前端实现。该基准基于真实的电子商务工作流程，通过迭代编辑和执行反馈来评估模型对中间表示缺陷的鲁棒性。实验表明，迭代编辑通常能提高性能，而基于强化学习的后期训练效果有限且不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计到代码的进展因数据集、工具链和评估协议不一致而难以比较，缺乏一个能模拟真实世界场景并处理中间表示中潜在错误的鲁棒基准。

Method: 引入了1D-Bench基准，它：1) 基于真实电子商务工作流程；2) 为每个实例提供参考渲染和可能包含提取错误的中间表示；3) 模型以两者作为输入，将中间表示作为结构线索，并根据参考渲染进行评估，以测试对中间表示缺陷的鲁棒性；4) 要求在固定工具链和显式组件层次结构下生成可执行的React代码库；5) 定义了一个多轮设置，模型利用执行反馈迭代应用组件级编辑。还在合成修复轨迹和基于强化学习的编辑上进行了后期训练的初步研究。

Result: 1) 对商业和开源多模态模型的实验表明，迭代编辑通常通过提高渲染成功率并改善视觉相似性来提高最终性能。2) 基于合成修复轨迹和强化学习的编辑的后期训练初步研究观察到有限且不稳定的收益，这可能源于稀疏的终端奖励和高方差的文件级更新。

Conclusion: 1D-Bench基准及其迭代编辑范式有望提高设计到代码模型的性能，特别是在处理中间表示缺陷的鲁棒性方面。然而，像基于强化学习的后期训练等高级训练技术需要进一步完善，以解决稀疏奖励和高方差更新等问题。

Abstract: Design-to-code translates high-fidelity UI designs into executable front-end implementations, but progress remains hard to compare due to inconsistent datasets, toolchains, and evaluation protocols. We introduce 1D-Bench, a benchmark grounded in real e-commerce workflows, where each instance provides a reference rendering and an exported intermediate representation that may contain extraction errors. 1D is short for one day, representing the efficient completion of design-to-code tasks in less than one day. Models take both as input, using the intermediate representation as structural cues while being evaluated against the reference rendering, which tests robustness to intermediate representation defects rather than literal adherence. 1D-Bench requires generating an executable React codebase under a fixed toolchain with an explicit component hierarchy, and defines a multi-round setting in which models iteratively apply component-level edits using execution feedback. Experiments on commercial and open-weight multimodal models show that iterative editing generally improves final performance by increasing rendering success and often improving visual similarity. We further conduct a pilot study on post-training with synthetic repair trajectories and reinforcement learning based editing, and observe limited and unstable gains that may stem from sparse terminal rewards and high-variance file-level updates.

</details>


### [286] [Operational Robustness of LLMs on Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18800)
*Debalina Ghosh Paul,Hong Zhu,Ian Bayley*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLMs）常用于生成代码，本论文旨在评估其对编码任务描述变化的鲁棒性。提出了一种名为“情境域分析”的鲁棒性评估方法，以找到导致LLMs输出错误的自然语言描述的最小预期变化。实验评估了四种LLMs，并发现鲁棒性对于更复杂和更高级的任务（如多线程、数据结构）较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
