Motivation: 了解盲人用户在使用无障碍论坛和生成式AI工具时的体验，对于识别和解决持续存在的支持差距至关重要。

Method: 采访了14名经常使用论坛和生成式AI工具的盲人用户。

Result: 研究发现，论坛常因话题重叠、内容冗余或不相关以及零散的回复而使用户感到不知所措，增加了认知负荷。生成式AI工具则通过产生不可靠的答案，包括过于冗长或零散的指导、虚构信息和未能遵循提示的矛盾建议，引入了新的障碍，从而提高了验证需求。

Conclusion: 基于这些见解，本文概述了设计机会，旨在提高辅助资源的可靠性，为盲人用户提供更值得信赖且认知负担更小的支持。

Abstract: Accessibility forums and, more recently, generative AI tools have become vital resources for blind users seeking solutions to computer-interaction issues and learning about new assistive technologies, screen reader features, tutorials, and software updates. Understanding user experiences with these resources is essential for identifying and addressing persistent support gaps. Towards this, we interviewed 14 blind users who regularly engage with forums and GenAI tools. Findings revealed that forums often overwhelm users with multiple overlapping topics, redundant or irrelevant content, and fragmented responses that must be mentally pieced together, increasing cognitive load. GenAI tools, while offering more direct assistance, introduce new barriers by producing unreliable answers, including overly verbose or fragmented guidance, fabricated information, and contradictory suggestions that fail to follow prompts, thereby heightening verification demands. Based on these insights, we outlined design opportunities to improve the reliability of assistive resources, aiming to provide blind users with more trustworthy and cognitively-manageable support.

</details>


### [322] [Chat-Based Support Alone May Not Be Enough: Comparing Conversational and Embedded LLM Feedback for Mathematical Proof Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18807)
*Eason Chen,Sophia Judicke,Kayla Beigh,Xinyi Tang,Isabel Wang,Nina Yuan,Zimo Xiao,Chuangji Li,Shizhuo Li,Reed Luttmer,Shreya Singh,Maria Yampolsky,Naman Parikh,Yvonne Zhao,Meiyi Chen,Scarlett Huang,Anishka Mohanty,Gregory Johnson,John Mackey,Jionghao Lin,Ken Koedinger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GPTutor，一个LLM驱动的辅导系统，早期使用与更高的作业表现相关，但未转化为考试分数。自我效能感较低的学生使用更多。聊天机器人使用和寻求答案行为与期中表现呈负相关，而证明批阅工具的使用没有显示出这种负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估GPTutor（一个LLM驱动的离散数学辅导系统）对学生学习的影响及其各组件的有效性。

Method: 采用分阶段访问研究，共148名学生。系统包含结构化证明批阅工具和数学问题聊天机器人。通过分析作业表现、考试分数、使用日志以及聊天机器人交互行为（通过人工编码和自动化分类），评估了系统对学习成果的影响，并控制了先验表现和自我效能等因素。

Result: 早期访问GPTutor与实验组专属期间更高的作业表现相关，但这种表现提升并未迁移到考试成绩。自我效能感较低和先前考试成绩较差的学生更频繁地使用系统两部分。在控制了先验表现和自我效能后，更高的聊天机器人使用频率和寻求答案行为与随后的期中考试表现呈负相关，而证明批阅工具的使用未显示出可检测的独立关联。

Conclusion: 单独的基于聊天机器人的支持可能无法可靠地促进数学证明学习成果向独立评估的迁移，而以作业为基础的结构化反馈（证明批阅）似乎与学习减少的关联性较小。

Abstract: We evaluate GPTutor, an LLM-powered tutoring system for an undergraduate discrete mathematics course. It integrates two LLM-supported tools: a structured proof-review tool that provides embedded feedback on students' written proof attempts, and a chatbot for math questions. In a staggered-access study with 148 students, earlier access was associated with higher homework performance during the interval when only the experimental group could use the system, while we did not observe this performance increase transfer to exam scores. Usage logs show that students with lower self-efficacy and prior exam performance used both components more frequently. Session-level behavioral labels, produced by human coding and scaled using an automated classifier, characterize how students engaged with the chatbot (e.g., answer-seeking or help-seeking). In models controlling for prior performance and self-efficacy, higher chatbot usage and answer-seeking behavior were negatively associated with subsequent midterm performance, whereas proof-review usage showed no detectable independent association. Together, the findings suggest that chatbot-based support alone may not reliably support transfer to independent assessment of math proof-learning outcomes, whereas work-anchored, structured feedback appears less associated with reduced learning.

</details>


### [323] [NeuroWise: A Multi-Agent LLM "Glass-Box" System for Practicing Double-Empathy Communication with Autistic Partners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18962)
*Albert Tang,Yifan Mo,Jie Li,Yue Su,Mengyuan Zhang,Sander L. Koole,Koen Hindriks,Jiahuan Pei*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了NeuroWise，一个基于LLM的多智能体辅导系统，旨在帮助神经典型用户减少基于缺陷的归因，提高与神经多样性个体的沟通效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管“双重共情问题”将神经多样性个体与神经典型个体之间的沟通困难视为相互误解，但大多数干预措施都集中在自闭症个体上。本研究旨在解决神经典型方面的问题。

Method: 本研究提出了NeuroWise，一个基于LLM的多智能体辅导系统，通过压力可视化、内部体验解读和情境指导来支持神经典型用户。通过一项 N=30 的受试者间研究对NeuroWise进行了评估。

Result: 所有参与者都认为NeuroWise有帮助。NeuroWise在使用基于缺陷的归因方面显示出显著的条件-时间效应 (p=0.02)：NeuroWise用户减少了缺陷框架，而基线用户在困难互动后倾向于归咎于自闭症的“缺陷”。NeuroWise用户还更有效地完成了对话（对话轮次减少37%，p=0.03）。

Conclusion: AI辅助解读（如NeuroWise）可以通过帮助用户认识到沟通挑战是相互的，从而支持归因改变。

Abstract: The double empathy problem frames communication difficulties between neurodivergent and neurotypical individuals as arising from mutual misunderstanding, yet most interventions focus on autistic individuals. We present NeuroWise, a multi-agent LLM-based coaching system that supports neurotypical users through stress visualization, interpretation of internal experiences, and contextual guidance. In a between-subjects study (N=30), NeuroWise was rated as helpful by all participants and showed a significant condition-time effect on deficit-based attributions (p=0.02): NeuroWise users reduced deficit framing, while baseline users shifted toward blaming autistic "deficits" after difficult interactions. NeuroWise users also completed conversations more efficiently (37% fewer turns, p=0.03). These findings suggest that AI-based interpretation can support attributional change by helping users recognize communication challenges as mutual.

</details>


### [324] [Cooperation After the Algorithm: Designing Human-AI Coexistence Beyond the Illusion of Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19629)
*Tatia Codreanu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 生成式人工智能系统在多个领域被广泛应用，但由于缺乏责任和义务，导致了问责和治理问题。本文提出了一种基于制度分析和演化合作理论的治理基础设施，包括一个形式不等式、一个合作生态框架（包含六个设计原则）和三个政策工具，旨在实现人类与人工智能之间稳定、负责任和可信赖的合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能系统在研究、法律、教育、媒体和治理等高风险领域日益参与，但它们不承担责任、不承担法律义务，也不分担下游后果的利害关系。这种结构性不对称已经导致了制裁、专业错误和治理失败。为了实现稳定的人机共存，需要一个能够分配剩余风险的治理基础设施。

Method: 本文借鉴制度分析和演化合作理论，引入了一个形式不等式来明确何时依赖AI会产生正的预期合作价值。该模型阐明了治理条件、系统政策和问责制度如何共同决定合作是理性的还是结构性缺陷的。在此形式化基础上，推导出了一个包含六个设计原则的合作生态框架，并通过三个人工政策工具（人机合作章程、背叛风险登记册和合作准备度审计）来具体实施该框架。

Result: 形式模型揭示了治理条件、系统策略和问责制度如何共同决定合作的合理性或结构性缺陷。合作生态框架提出了六个设计原则：互惠契约、可见的信任基础设施、条件合作模式、背叛缓解机制、抵制权威表演的叙事素养和地球优先的可持续性约束。这些元素将分析单位从用户-AI二元组转向塑造激励、信号、问责和修复的制度环境。

Conclusion: 本文为人机系统设计提供了理论基础和实用工具包，以期随着时间的推移维持负责任、值得信赖的合作。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence systems increasingly participate in research, law, education, media, and governance. Their fluent and adaptive outputs create an experience of collaboration. However, these systems do not bear responsibility, incur liability, or share stakes in downstream consequences. This structural asymmetry has already produced sanctions, professional errors, and governance failures in high-stakes contexts We argue that stable human-AI coexistence is an institutional achievement that depends on governance infrastructure capable of distributing residual risk. Drawing on institutional analysis and evolutionary cooperation theory, we introduce a formal inequality that specifies when reliance on AI yields positive expected cooperative value. The model makes explicit how governance conditions, system policy, and accountability regimes jointly determine whether cooperation is rational or structurally defective. From this formalization we derive a cooperation ecology framework with six design principles: reciprocity contracts, visible trust infrastructure, conditional cooperation modes, defection-mitigation mechanisms, narrative literacy against authority theatre, and an Earth-first sustainability constraint. We operationalize the framework through three policy artefacts: a Human-AI Cooperation Charter, a Defection Risk Register, and a Cooperation Readiness Audit. Together, these elements shift the unit of analysis from the user-AI dyad to the institutional environment that shapes incentives, signals, accountability, and repair. The paper provides a theoretical foundation and practical toolkit for designing human-AI systems that can sustain accountable, trustworthy cooperation over time.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [325] [NutriOrion: A Hierarchical Multi-Agent Framework for Personalized Nutrition Intervention Grounded in Clinical Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18650)
*Junwei Wu,Runze Yan,Hanqi Luo,Darren Liu,Minxiao Wang,Kimberly L. Townsend,Lydia S. Hartwig,Derek Milketinas,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: NutriOrion是一个用于多病共存患者个性化营养干预的分层多智能体框架，采用并行-顺序推理拓扑。它通过分解规划、解决冲突和整合药理学禁忌来解决单智能体LLM的局限性，确保临床有效性。该框架在330名中风多病共存患者中表现优于GPT-4.1等基线模型，显著降低了药物-食物相互作用，实现了强大的个性化，并带来了重要的饮食改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多病共存患者的个性化营养干预对于改善健康结果至关重要，但由于需要同时整合异质性临床状况、药物和饮食指南，因此极具挑战性。单智能体大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理此类高维患者信息时，常出现上下文过载和注意力分散的问题。

Method: NutriOrion是一个分层多智能体框架，采用并行-顺序推理拓扑。它将营养规划分解为具有独立上下文的专业领域智能体，以减轻锚定偏差，随后进行条件细化。该框架包含一个多目标优先排序算法以解决冲突的饮食要求，以及一个安全约束机制，在合成过程中注入药理学禁忌作为硬性负面约束，从构建而非事后过滤确保临床有效性。为了临床互操作性，NutriOrion将合成的见解映射到ADIME标准和FHIR R4资源。

Result: 在330名中风多病共存患者中进行评估，NutriOrion优于包括GPT-4.1和其他多智能体架构在内的多个基线模型。它实现了12.1%的药物-食物相互作用违规率，通过患者生物标志物与推荐风险营养素之间的负相关（-0.26至-0.35）展示了强大的个性化，并产生了临床上有意义的饮食改善，包括膳食纤维增加167%、钾增加27%，同时钠减少9%、糖减少12%。

Conclusion: NutriOrion框架通过其分层多智能体架构、冲突解决机制和安全约束，成功地为多病共存患者提供了有效的个性化营养干预，克服了传统LLM的局限性，并在临床上取得了显著的积极成果，具有良好的互操作性。

Abstract: Personalized nutrition intervention for patients with multimorbidity is critical for improving health outcomes, yet remains challenging because it requires the simultaneous integration of heterogeneous clinical conditions, medications, and dietary guidelines. Single-agent large language models (LLMs) often suffer from context overload and attention dilution when processing such high-dimensional patient profiles. We introduce NutriOrion, a hierarchical multi-agent framework with a parallel-then-sequential reasoning topology. NutriOrion decomposes nutrition planning into specialized domain agents with isolated contexts to mitigate anchoring bias, followed by a conditional refinement stage. The framework includes a multi-objective prioritization algorithm to resolve conflicting dietary requirements and a safety constraint mechanism that injects pharmacological contraindications as hard negative constraints during synthesis, ensuring clinical validity by construction rather than post-hoc filtering. For clinical interoperability, NutriOrion maps synthesized insights into the ADIME standard and FHIR R4 resources. Evaluated on 330 stroke patients with multimorbidity, NutriOrion outperforms multiple baselines, including GPT-4.1 and alternative multi-agent architectures. It achieves a 12.1 percent drug-food interaction violation rate, demonstrates strong personalization with negative correlations (-0.26 to -0.35) between patient biomarkers and recommended risk nutrients, and yields clinically meaningful dietary improvements, including a 167 percent increase in fiber and a 27 percent increase in potassium, alongside reductions in sodium (9 percent) and sugars (12 percent).

</details>


### [326] [EDU-MATRIX: A Society-Centric Generative Cognitive Digital Twin Architecture for Secondary Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18705)
*Wenjing Zhai,Jianbin Zhang,Tao Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: EDU-MATRIX是一个以社会为中心的生成认知数字孪生架构，通过环境上下文注入引擎（ECIE）、模块化逻辑演化协议（MLEP）和基于角色拓扑的内源性对齐，解决了多智能体模拟中的“以智能体为中心悖论”，实现了具有价值对齐的涌现行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体模拟存在“以智能体为中心悖论”，即规则硬编码在单个智能体中，导致复杂的社会动态僵化且难以与教育价值对齐。

Method: 本文提出了EDU-MATRIX架构，其核心是模拟“具有引力场的社会空间”而非“个体”。该架构包含三个主要贡献：1. 环境上下文注入引擎（ECIE），作为“社会微内核”，根据智能体的时空坐标动态注入机构规则（引力）。2. 模块化逻辑演化协议（MLEP），其中知识以“流体”胶囊形式存在，智能体通过合成生成新范式，确保高对话一致性（94.1%）。3. 基于角色拓扑的内源性对齐，安全约束从智能体在社交图中的位置而非外部过滤器中产生。

Result: 系统被部署为一个包含2,400个智能体的中学数字孪生，结果表明“社会引力”（规则）和“认知流体”（知识）相互作用，产生了涌现的、与价值对齐的行为（社会聚类系数：0.72）。

Conclusion: EDU-MATRIX通过其社会中心架构和新颖的机制，成功地展示了如何在多智能体模拟中实现涌现的、价值对齐的复杂社会动态，解决了传统以智能体为中心方法的局限性。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent simulations often suffer from the "Agent-Centric Paradox": rules are hard-coded into individual agents, making complex social dynamics rigid and difficult to align with educational values. This paper presents EDU-MATRIX, a society-centric generative cognitive digital twin architecture that shifts the paradigm from simulating "people" to simulating a "social space with a gravitational field." We introduce three architectural contributions: (1) An Environment Context Injection Engine (ECIE), which acts as a "social microkernel," dynamically injecting institutional rules (Gravity) into agents based on their spatial-temporal coordinates; (2) A Modular Logic Evolution Protocol (MLEP), where knowledge exists as "fluid" capsules that agents synthesize to generate new paradigms, ensuring high dialogue consistency (94.1%); and (3) Endogenous Alignment via Role-Topology, where safety constraints emerge from the agent's position in the social graph rather than external filters. Deployed as a digital twin of a secondary school with 2,400 agents, the system demonstrates how "social gravity" (rules) and "cognitive fluids" (knowledge) interact to produce emergent, value-aligned behaviors (Social Clustering Coefficient: 0.72).

</details>


### [327] [Adaptive Collaboration of Arena-Based Argumentative LLMs for Explainable and Contestable Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18916)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Phuc Ho,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Dinh Thien Loc Nguyen,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了ACAL，一个用于法律推理的神经符号框架，它结合了自适应多智能体协作和基于竞技场的量化双极论证框架（A-QBAF），以提供可验证、可争议且结构化的解释，解决了现有大型语言模型（LLM）方法在法律推理中缺乏形式化验证和用户干预机制的问题。ACAL在LegalBench基准测试上优于现有基线，实现了高效的预测性能、结构化透明度和可争议性，并支持人机协同工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律推理不仅需要高准确性，还需要通过可验证和可争议的论证来证明决策。然而，现有的大型语言模型（LLM）方法（如思维链CoT和检索增强生成RAG）通常产生非结构化的解释，缺乏形式化的验证机制或用户干预能力。

Method: ACAL是一个神经符号框架，它将自适应多智能体协作与基于竞技场的量化双极论证框架（A-QBAF）相结合。ACAL动态部署专家智能体团队来构建论证，采用冲突解决机制来裁决冲突主张，并利用不确定性感知升级机制处理边界案例。该框架还支持人机协作（HITL）可争议性工作流，使用户能够直接审计和修改底层推理图以影响最终判断。

Result: 在LegalBench基准测试中，ACAL在Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite和Gemini-2.5-Flash架构上均优于强大的基线，有效地平衡了高效的预测性能与结构化透明度和可争议性。

Conclusion: ACAL通过提供一个平衡了高效预测性能、结构化透明度和可争议性的框架，成功解决了现有LLM方法在法律推理中解释非结构化、缺乏验证和用户干预的问题，从而实现了高准确性、可验证和可争议的决策。

Abstract: Legal reasoning requires not only high accuracy but also the ability to justify decisions through verifiable and contestable arguments. However, existing Large Language Model (LLM) approaches, such as Chain-of-Thought (CoT) and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), often produce unstructured explanations that lack a formal mechanism for verification or user intervention. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Collaboration of Argumentative LLMs (ACAL), a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates adaptive multi-agent collaboration with an Arena-based Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework (A-QBAF). ACAL dynamically deploys expert agent teams to construct arguments, employs a clash resolution mechanism to adjudicate conflicting claims, and utilizes uncertainty-aware escalation for borderline cases. Crucially, our framework supports a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) contestability workflow, enabling users to directly audit and modify the underlying reasoning graph to influence the final judgment. Empirical evaluations on the LegalBench benchmark demonstrate that ACAL outperforms strong baselines across Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite and Gemini-2.5-Flash architectures, effectively balancing efficient predictive performance with structured transparency and contestability. Our implementation is available at: .

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [328] [Rendezvous and Docking of Mobile Ground Robots for Efficient Transportation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19862)
*Lars Fischer,Daniel Flögel,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种中央MPC方法，通过明确建模机器人动力学和对接接口约束，并实施有效的接近策略，实现了全向轮式机器人在运动中的可靠物理耦合，从而在物流场景中实现高效的在运动中转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动地面机器人在运动中进行物理耦合，可以实现货物处理和转移效率的提高，解决当前物流挑战。现有方法忽略了对接接口建模和接近策略，导致非受控碰撞，使得在运动中物理耦合变得不可能或低效。

Method: 提出了一种中央MPC方法，明确建模了两个全向轮式机器人的动力学和状态，并结合了与对接接口相关的约束，实施了一种用于汇合和对接的接近策略。

Result: 该方法使带对接接口的全向轮式机器人在运动中进行物理耦合，且不受初始位置限制。此外，它使得在运动中转移成为可能，与非耦合方法相比，在物流场景中时间效率提高了19.75%，能源效率提高了21.04%。

Conclusion: 所提出的中央MPC方法成功解决了全向轮式机器人在运动中可靠物理耦合的挑战，显著提高了物流场景中的时间和能源效率。

Abstract: In-Motion physical coupling of multiple mobile ground robots has the potential to enable new applications like in-motion transfer that improves efficiency in handling and transferring goods, which tackles current challenges in logistics. A key challenge lies in achieving reliable autonomous in-motion physical coupling of two mobile ground robots starting at any initial position. Existing approaches neglect the modeling of the docking interface and the strategy for approaching it, resulting in uncontrolled collisions that make in-motion physical coupling either impossible or inefficient. To address this challenge, we propose a central mpc approach that explicitly models the dynamics and states of two omnidirectional wheeled robots, incorporates constraints related to their docking interface, and implements an approaching strategy for rendezvous and docking. This novel approach enables omnidirectional wheeled robots with a docking interface to physically couple in motion regardless of their initial position. In addition, it makes in-motion transfer possible, which is 19.75% more time- and 21.04% energy-efficient compared to a non-coupling approach in a logistic scenario.

</details>


### [329] [Robust Taylor-Lagrange Control for Safety-Critical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20076)
*Wei Xiao,Christos Cassandras,Anni Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的Taylor-Lagrange控制（rTLC）方法，通过泰勒展开与拉格朗日余项将控制显式化，以解决现有TLC方法中存在的可行性保持问题，并且只需要一个超参数，在自适应巡航控制问题中展现出有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制障碍函数（CBF）方法在解决安全关键控制问题中广泛应用，但其存在只是系统安全的充分条件而非必要条件。Taylor-Lagrange控制（TLC）方法解决了这一局限性，但易受可行性保持问题（如采样间隔效应）的影响。

Method: rTLC方法通过泰勒展开与拉格朗日余项，在高于函数相对阶的阶数上展开安全函数，使得控制能够在当前时刻而非未来时刻显式出现。这种方法自然地解决了可行性保持问题。

Result: rTLC方法仅需一个超参数（实现时的离散化时间间隔大小），相比其他方法大大减少，并能自然解决可行性保持问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的rTLC方法通过自适应巡航控制问题验证了其有效性，并与现有安全关键控制方法进行了比较，表现出优越性。

Abstract: Solving safety-critical control problem has widely adopted the Control Barrier Function (CBF) method. However, the existence of a CBF is only a sufficient condition for system safety. The recently proposed Taylor-Lagrange Control (TLC) method addresses this limitation, but is vulnerable to the feasibility preservation problem (e.g., inter-sampling effect). In this paper, we propose a robust TLC (rTLC) method to address the feasibility preservation problem. Specifically, the rTLC method expands the safety function at an order higher than the relative degree of the function using Taylor's expansion with Lagrange remainder, which allows the control to explicitly show up at the current time instead of the future time in the TLC method. The rTLC method naturally addresses the feasibility preservation problem with only one hyper-parameter (the discretization time interval size during implementation), which is much less than its counterparts. Finally, we illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed rTLC method through an adaptive cruise control problem, and compare it with existing safety-critical control methods.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [330] [From Static Spectra to Operando Infrared Dynamics: Physics Informed Flow Modeling and a Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18551)
*Shuquan Ye,Ben Fei,Hongbin Xu,Jiaying Lin,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为“Operando IR Prediction”的新任务，旨在通过单个静态光谱预测固态电解质界面（SEI）的时间分辨光谱演变。为此，作者引入了大型操作数据集OpIRSpec-7K和评估基准OpIRBench，并开发了物理感知框架Aligned Bi-stream Chemical Constraint（ABCC）。ABCC显著优于现有基线，并能推广到未见系统，支持AI驱动的电化学发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Operando红外（IR）光谱分析固态电解质界面（SEI）对锂离子电池性能至关重要，但其操作复杂且成本高昂，限制了标准研究机构的普及性。为了克服这一瓶颈，本研究旨在通过预测时间分辨光谱演变来提高SEI分析的可及性。

Method: 研究提出了一种名为“Operando IR Prediction”的新任务，旨在从单个静态光谱预测时间分辨的光谱“指纹”演变。为此，作者构建了首个大规模操作数据集OpIRSpec-7K（包含7,118个样本和10种电池系统）以及综合评估基准OpIRBench。针对标准光谱、视频和序列模型捕捉电压驱动化学动力学和复杂组成的局限性，研究提出了端到端物理感知框架Aligned Bi-stream Chemical Constraint（ABCC）。ABCC重新构建了MeanFlow，引入了新颖的Chemical Flow来明确建模反应轨迹，采用双流解耦机制分离溶剂-SEI，并强制执行质量守恒和峰值偏移等物理和光谱约束。

Result: ABCC显著优于最先进的静态、序列和生成基线模型。ABCC甚至可以推广到未见系统，并能够可解释地恢复SEI形成路径。

Conclusion: ABCC框架通过实现AI驱动的电化学发现，为SEI形成路径的分析和理解提供了新的途径，克服了传统Operando IR光谱分析的局限性。

Abstract: The Solid Electrolyte Interphase (SEI) is critical to the performance of lithium-ion batteries, yet its analysis via Operando Infrared (IR) spectroscopy remains experimentally complex and expensive, which limits its accessibility for standard research facilities. To overcome this bottleneck, we formulate a novel task, Operando IR Prediction, which aims to forecast the time-resolved evolution of spectral ``fingerprints'' from a single static spectrum. To facilitate this, we introduce OpIRSpec-7K, the first large-scale operando dataset comprising 7,118 high-quality samples across 10 distinct battery systems, alongside OpIRBench, a comprehensive evaluation benchmark with carefully designed protocols. Addressing the limitations of standard spectrum, video, and sequence models in capturing voltage-driven chemical dynamics and complex composition, we propose Aligned Bi-stream Chemical Constraint (ABCC), an end-to-end physics-aware framework. It reformulates MeanFlow and introduces a novel Chemical Flow to explicitly model reaction trajectories, employs a two-stream disentanglement mechanism for solvent-SEI separation, and enforces physics and spectrum constraints such as mass conservation and peak shifts. ABCC significantly outperforms state-of-the-art static, sequential, and generative baselines. ABCC even generalizes to unseen systems and enables interpretable downstream recovery of SEI formation pathways, supporting AI-driven electrochemical discovery.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [331] [Health+: Empowering Individuals via Unifying Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19319)
*Sujaya Maiyya,Shantanu Sharma,Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: Health+是一个以用户为中心的多模态健康数据管理系统，旨在解决个人健康数据分散和控制不足的问题，通过提供直观界面和智能推荐，赋能个人上传、查询和共享跨模态健康数据，从而建立一个互联、可解释、用户控制的健康信息生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗生态系统中，个人健康数据管理面临挑战，数据分散在不兼容的系统和格式中，导致个人对其医疗记录缺乏有效控制。

Method: Health+是一个以用户为中心的多模态健康数据管理系统。它不寻求机构改革，而是通过提供直观的界面和智能推荐，赋能个人上传、查询和共享其跨模态数据（如文本、图像、报告）。在系统层面，Health+解决存储、整合和保护异构健康记录的复杂性，同时确保效率和隐私。

Result: Health+使个人（包括技术专长有限的人）能够上传、查询和共享其跨模态健康数据，并提供数据访问和共享的智能推荐，从而实现对个人健康数据的有效控制和管理。

Conclusion: 通过统一多模态数据并优先考虑患者，Health+为建立一个更加互联、可解释和用户控制的健康信息生态系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Managing personal health data is a challenge in today's fragmented and institution-centric healthcare ecosystem. Individuals often lack meaningful control over their medical records, which are scattered across incompatible systems and formats. This vision paper presents Health+, a user-centric, multimodal health data management system that empowers individuals (including those with limited technical expertise) to upload, query, and share their data across modalities (e.g., text, images, reports). Rather than aiming for institutional overhaul, Health+ emphasizes individual agency by providing intuitive interfaces and intelligent recommendations for data access and sharing. At the system level, it tackles the complexity of storing, integrating, and securing heterogeneous health records, ensuring both efficiency and privacy. By unifying multimodal data and prioritizing patients, Health+ lays the foundation for a more connected, interpretable, and user-controlled health information ecosystem.

</details>


### [332] [Tri-Subspaces Disentanglement for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19585)
*Chunlei Meng,Jiabin Luo,Zhenglin Yan,Zhenyu Yu,Rong Fu,Zhongxue Gan,Chun Ouyang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种三子空间解缠（TSD）框架和子空间感知交叉注意力（SACA）融合模块，用于多模态情感分析（MSA），通过将特征分解为通用、子模态共享和私有子空间来提高表示的表达能力，并取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态情感分析（MSA）方法大多关注全局共享表示或模态特定特征，忽略了仅由特定模态对共享的信号，这限制了多模态表示的表达能力和判别力。

Method: 论文提出了一种三子空间解缠（TSD）框架，将特征分解为三个互补的子空间：捕获全局一致性的通用子空间、建模成对跨模态协同作用的子模态共享子空间，以及保留模态特定线索的私有子空间。为了保持这些子空间的纯净和独立，引入了解耦监督器和结构化正则化损失。此外，还设计了一个子空间感知交叉注意力（SACA）融合模块，自适应地建模和整合来自三个子空间的信息，以获得更丰富、更鲁棒的表示。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI上的实验表明，TSD在所有关键指标上均达到最先进的性能，在CMU-MOSI上达到0.691 MAE，在CMU-MOSEI上达到54.9% ACC-7，并且很好地迁移到多模态意图识别任务。消融研究证实，三子空间解缠和SACA共同增强了多粒度跨模态情感线索的建模。

Conclusion: 所提出的三子空间解缠（TSD）框架与子空间感知交叉注意力（SACA）融合模块有效地解决了现有MSA方法的局限性，通过捕获全局、成对和模态特定信息，实现了改进、鲁棒且最先进的多模态情感分析。

Abstract: Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) integrates language, visual, and acoustic modalities to infer human sentiment. Most existing methods either focus on globally shared representations or modality-specific features, while overlooking signals that are shared only by certain modality pairs. This limits the expressiveness and discriminative power of multimodal representations. To address this limitation, we propose a Tri-Subspace Disentanglement (TSD) framework that explicitly factorizes features into three complementary subspaces: a common subspace capturing global consistency, submodally-shared subspaces modeling pairwise cross-modal synergies, and private subspaces preserving modality-specific cues. To keep these subspaces pure and independent, we introduce a decoupling supervisor together with structured regularization losses. We further design a Subspace-Aware Cross-Attention (SACA) fusion module that adaptively models and integrates information from the three subspaces to obtain richer and more robust representations. Experiments on CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI demonstrate that TSD achieves state-of-the-art performance across all key metrics, reaching 0.691 MAE on CMU-MOSI and 54.9% ACC-7 on CMU-MOSEI, and also transfers well to multimodal intent recognition tasks. Ablation studies confirm that tri-subspace disentanglement and SACA jointly enhance the modeling of multi-granular cross-modal sentiment cues.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [333] [UniRank: A Multi-Agent Calibration Pipeline for Estimating University Rankings from Anonymized Bibliometric Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18824)
*Pedram Riyazimehr,Seyyed Ehsan Mahmoudi*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: UniRank是一个多智能体LLM管道，仅使用匿名的OpenAlex和Semantic Scholar公开文献计量数据来估计全球排名系统中的大学位置，并展示了真实的分析推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个仅使用公开文献计量数据（来自OpenAlex和Semantic Scholar）来估计全球排名系统中大学位置的系统，并通过机构匿名化来防止大型语言模型记忆混淆结果。

Method: UniRank采用三阶段架构：(a) 基于匿名机构指标的零样本估计，(b) 针对真实排名大学进行每系统工具增强校准，以及(c) 最终合成。关键在于机构信息被匿名化（名称、国家、DOI、论文标题和合作国家均被编辑），并且在评估期间，其实际排名对校准工具隐藏，以防止LLM记忆混淆结果。

Result: 在泰晤士高等教育（THE）世界大学排名（n=352）中，系统取得了平均绝对误差（MAE）251.5，中位绝对误差（Median AE）131.5，归一化平均绝对误差（PNMAE）12.03%，斯皮尔曼相关系数（Spearman ρ）0.769，肯德尔秩相关系数（Kendall τ）0.591，@50命中率20.7%，@100命中率39.8%，以及记忆化指数为零。系统性正向误差（+190.1位置，表明系统一致预测的排名比实际排名差）以及从顶尖梯队（MAE = 60.5，hit@100 = 90.5%）到末尾梯队（MAE = 328.2，hit@100 = 20.8%）的性能单调下降，提供了该管道执行真实分析推理而非回忆记忆排名的有力证据。

Conclusion: UniRank管道通过匿名化策略、零记忆化指数、系统性误差以及分层性能下降等证据，表明其执行的是真实的分析推理，而非回忆记忆中的排名。

Abstract: We present UniRank, a multi-agent LLM pipeline that estimates university positions across global ranking systems using only publicly available bibliometric data from OpenAlex and Semantic Scholar. The system employs a three-stage architecture: (a) zero-shot estimation from anonymized institutional metrics, (b) per-system tool-augmented calibration against real ranked universities, and (c) final synthesis. Critically, institutions are anonymized -- names, countries, DOIs, paper titles, and collaboration countries are all redacted -- and their actual ranks are hidden from the calibration tools during evaluation, preventing LLM memorization from confounding results. On the Times Higher Education (THE) World University Rankings ($n=352$), the system achieves MAE = 251.5 rank positions, Median AE = 131.5, PNMAE = 12.03%, Spearman $\rho = 0.769$, Kendall $\tau = 0.591$, hit rate @50 = 20.7%, hit rate @100 = 39.8%, and a Memorization Index of exactly zero (no exact-match zero-width predictions among all 352 universities). The systematic positive-signed error (+190.1 positions, indicating the system consistently predicts worse ranks than actual) and monotonic performance degradation from elite tier (MAE = 60.5, hit@100 = 90.5%) to tail tier (MAE = 328.2, hit@100 = 20.8%) provide strong evidence that the pipeline performs genuine analytical reasoning rather than recalling memorized rankings. A live demo is available at .

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [334] [Kaiwu-PyTorch-Plugin: Bridging Deep Learning and Photonic Quantum Computing for Energy-Based Models and Active Sample Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19114)
*Hongdong Zhu,Qi Gao,Yin Ma,Shaobo Chen,Haixu Liu,Fengao Wang,Tinglan Wang,Chang Wu,Kai Wen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了Kaiwu-PyTorch-Plugin (KPP)，它将深度学习与光子量子计算在多个维度上连接起来，解决了基于能量模型中的经典效率低下问题，并在单细胞和OpenWebText数据集上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接深度学习和光子量子计算，解决基于能量模型中经典的效率低下问题。

Method: KPP将Coherent Ising Machine集成到PyTorch生态系统中。该框架通过加速玻尔兹曼采样、通过主动采样优化训练数据以及构建混合架构（如QBM-VAE和Q-Diffusion）来促进量子集成。

Result: 在单细胞和OpenWebText数据集上的经验结果表明KPP能够实现SOTA性能。

Conclusion: KPP验证了一个全面的量子-经典范式。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Kaiwu-PyTorch-Plugin (KPP) to bridge Deep Learning and Photonic Quantum Computing across multiple dimensions. KPP integrates the Coherent Ising Machine into the PyTorch ecosystem, addressing classical inefficiencies in Energy-Based Models. The framework facilitates quantum integration in three key aspects: accelerating Boltzmann sampling, optimizing training data via Active Sampling, and constructing hybrid architectures like QBM-VAE and Q-Diffusion. Empirical results on single-cell and OpenWebText datasets demonstrate KPPs ability to achieve SOTA performance, validating a comprehensive quantum-classical paradigm.

</details>


### [335] [Auto Quantum Machine Learning for Multisource Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18642)
*Tomasz Rybotycki,Sebastian Dziura,Piotr Gawron*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化量子机器学习 (AQML) 方法，用于解决数据融合挑战。该方法在处理多源输入时，与经典多层感知器 (MLP) 和手动设计的 QML 模型相比，表现出竞争力。将其应用于利用 ONERA 数据集进行变化检测，在准确性上超越了先前报道的基于 QML 的变化检测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着容错量子计算的出现，人们对将量子计算方法应用于遥感等数据密集型科学领域越来越感兴趣。量子机器学习 (QML) 已显示出处理此类复杂任务的潜力，尤其是在作为复杂数据分析问题的量子数据融合方面。

Method: 本文引入了一种自动化量子机器学习 (AQML) 方法来解决数据融合挑战。该方法通过评估 AQML 生成的量子电路在处理多源输入时与经典多层感知器 (MLP) 和手动设计的 QML 模型的性能。此外，将该方法应用于使用多光谱 ONERA 数据集进行变化检测。

Result: AQML 生成的量子电路在处理多源输入时与经典多层感知器 (MLP) 和手动设计的 QML 模型相比表现出色。该方法在利用多光谱 ONERA 数据集进行变化检测时，实现了比先前报道的基于 QML 的变化检测结果更高的准确性。

Conclusion: 自动化量子机器学习 (AQML) 方法能够有效解决数据融合挑战，并在变化检测等任务中，通过生成高性能的量子电路，超越现有的 QML 方法，提高准确性。

Abstract: With fault-tolerant quantum computing on the horizon, there is growing interest in applying quantum computational methods to data-intensive scientific fields like remote sensing. Quantum machine learning (QML) has already demonstrated potential for such demanding tasks. One area of particular focus is quantum data fusion -- a complex data analysis problem that has attracted significant recent attention. In this work, we introduce an automated QML (AQML) approach for addressing data fusion challenges. We evaluate how AQML-generated quantum circuits perform compared to classical multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and manually designed QML models when processing multisource inputs. Furthermore, we apply our method to change detection using the multispectral ONERA dataset, achieving improved accuracy over previously reported QML-based change detection results.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [336] [Hexagon-MLIR: An AI Compilation Stack For Qualcomm's Neural Processing Units (NPUs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19762)
*Mohammed Javed Absar,Muthu Baskaran,Abhikrant Sharma,Abhilash Bhandari,Ankit Aggarwal,Arun Rangasamy,Dibyendu Das,Fateme Hosseini,Franck Slama,Iulian Brumar,Jyotsna Verma,Krishnaprasad Bindumadhavan,Mitesh Kothari,Mohit Gupta,Ravishankar Kolachana,Richard Lethin,Samarth Narang,Sanjay Motilal Ladwa,Shalini Jain,Snigdha Suresh Dalvi,Tasmia Rahman,Venkat Rasagna Reddy Komatireddy,Vivek Vasudevbhai Pandya,Xiyue Shi,Zachary Zipper*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Hexagon-MLIR是一个开源编译栈，针对Qualcomm Hexagon NPU，统一支持Triton内核和PyTorch模型的降低。它通过生成巨型内核来优化数据局部性并减少带宽瓶颈，从而实现AI工作负载的加速和新Triton内核的快速部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了加速AI工作负载的部署，并有效利用Qualcomm Hexagon NPU的架构特性，同时解决传统基于库方法中存在的带宽瓶颈。

Method: 本文提出了Hexagon-MLIR，一个基于MLIR框架的开源编译栈。它通过一系列结构化的编译通道，将Triton内核和PyTorch模型降低到目标NPU上。通过摄取Triton内核，生成巨型内核以最大化NPU紧密耦合存储器（TCM）中的数据局部性。

Result: 实现了新Triton内核（手写或来自PyTorch 2.0的子图）的更快部署，并通过生成巨型内核最大化数据局部性，减少了NPU的TCM中的带宽瓶颈。

Conclusion: Hexagon-MLIR是一个正在进行中的工作，它提供了一个开放源的、基于MLIR的编译栈，为开发者提供了一条通过更灵活的方法推进AI编译能力的途径，并计划在未来添加更多的优化和功能。

Abstract: In this paper, we present Hexagon-MLIR,an open-source compilation stack that targets Qualcomm Hexagon Neural Processing Unit (NPU) and provides unified support for lowering Triton kernels and PyTorch models . Built using the MLIR framework, our compiler applies a structured sequence of passes to exploit NPU architectural features to accelerate AI workloads. It enables faster deployment of new Triton kernels (hand-written or subgraphs from PyTorch 2.0), for our target by providing automated compilation from kernel to binary. By ingesting Triton kernels, we generate mega-kernels that maximize data locality in the NPU's Tightly Coupled Memory (TCM), reducing the bandwidth bottlenecks inherent in library-based approaches. This initiative complements our commercial toolchains by providing developers with an open-source MLIR-based compilation stack that gives them a path to advance AI compilation capabilities through a more flexible approach. Hexagon-MLIR is a work-in-progress, and we are continuing to add many more optimizations and capabilities in this effort.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [337] [Watermarking LLM Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18700)
*Wenlong Meng,Chen Gong,Terry Yue Zhuo,Fan Zhang,Kecen Li,Zheng Liu,Zhou Yang,Chengkun Wei,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ActHook是首个为LLM智能体轨迹数据集设计的数字水印方法，通过嵌入由秘密密钥激活的“钩子”动作，可在不改变原始任务结果的情况下实现黑盒检测，实验表明其平均检测AUC为94.3%，且对性能影响可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖高成本生成的高质量轨迹数据，然而现有研究忽视了对这些数据的版权保护，导致数据被盗用且难以追溯或维权，使得数据创建者面临风险。

Method: ActHook受软件工程钩子机制启发，在LLM智能体轨迹中嵌入由秘密输入密钥激活的“钩子”动作。这些动作不改变原始任务结果，并可在智能体顺序操作的决策点插入，而不中断任务流。当激活密钥存在时，经过水印轨迹训练的LLM智能体能以显著更高的速率生成这些钩子动作，从而实现可靠的黑盒检测。

Result: 在数学推理、网页搜索和软件工程智能体上，ActHook在Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B模型上实现了94.3%的平均检测AUC，同时性能下降微不足道。

Conclusion: ActHook为LLM智能体轨迹数据提供了一种有效且实用的版权保护解决方案，通过可检测的水印机制，在几乎不影响智能体性能的前提下，显著增强了数据所有权的可追溯性和安全性。

Abstract: LLM agents rely heavily on high-quality trajectory data to guide their problem-solving behaviors, yet producing such data requires substantial task design, high-capacity model generation, and manual filtering. Despite the high cost of creating these datasets, existing literature has overlooked copyright protection for LLM agent trajectories. This gap leaves creators vulnerable to data theft and makes it difficult to trace misuse or enforce ownership rights. This paper introduces ActHook, the first watermarking method tailored for agent trajectory datasets. Inspired by hook mechanisms in software engineering, ActHook embeds hook actions that are activated by a secret input key and do not alter the original task outcome. Like software execution, LLM agents operate sequentially, allowing hook actions to be inserted at decision points without disrupting task flow. When the activation key is present, an LLM agent trained on watermarked trajectories can produce these hook actions at a significantly higher rate, enabling reliable black-box detection. Experiments on mathematical reasoning, web searching, and software engineering agents show that ActHook achieves an average detection AUC of 94.3 on Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B while incurring negligible performance degradation.

</details>


### [338] [MANATEE: Inference-Time Lightweight Diffusion Based Safety Defense for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18782)
*Chun Yan Ryan Kan,Tommy Tran,Vedant Yadav,Ava Cai,Kevin Zhu,Ruizhe Li,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MANATEE是一种推理时防御机制，通过在良性表示流形上使用密度估计和扩散来抵御LLM越狱攻击，无需有害训练数据或架构修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御机制（二元分类器）在对抗性输入超出决策边界时失效，且重复微调计算成本高昂并可能降低模型能力。

Method: MANATEE学习良性隐藏状态的分数函数，并利用扩散将异常表示投射到安全区域，其核心是基于良性表示流形上的密度估计。此方法无需有害训练数据和架构修改。

Result: 在Mistral-7B-Instruct、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Gemma-2-9B-it上进行实验，MANATEE在特定数据集上将攻击成功率降低高达100%，同时保持模型在良性输入上的实用性。

Conclusion: MANATEE是一种有效且高效的推理时防御方法，用于抵御大型语言模型的对抗性越狱攻击。

Abstract: Defending LLMs against adversarial jailbreak attacks remains an open challenge. Existing defenses rely on binary classifiers that fail when adversarial input falls outside the learned decision boundary, and repeated fine-tuning is computationally expensive while potentially degrading model capabilities. We propose MANATEE, an inference-time defense that uses density estimation over a benign representation manifold. MANATEE learns the score function of benign hidden states and uses diffusion to project anomalous representations toward safe regions--requiring no harmful training data and no architectural modifications. Experiments across Mistral-7B-Instruct, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and Gemma-2-9B-it demonstrate that MANATEE reduce Attack Success Rate by up to 100\% on certain datasets, while preserving model utility on benign inputs.

</details>


### [339] [Trojan Horses in Recruiting: A Red-Teaming Case Study on Indirect Prompt Injection in Standard vs. Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18514)
*Manuel Wirth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究调查了在人力资源领域中LLM的间接提示注入(IPI)安全问题，比较了标准模型和推理增强型Qwen 3 30B模型。研究发现，推理模型在简单攻击中可能更容易被说服性地利用，但在复杂攻击中表现出“元认知泄漏”，使其更容易被检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型(LLMs)越来越多地集成到自动化决策流程中，特别是在人力资源(HR)领域，间接提示注入(IPI)的安全隐患变得至关重要。尽管存在一种普遍的假设，即“推理”或“思维链”模型因其自我纠正能力而具有安全优势，但新兴研究表明这些能力可能导致更复杂的对齐失败。本研究旨在挑战“通过推理实现安全”的前提。

Method: 采用定性红队案例研究方法，使用Qwen 3 30B架构。将一个标准的指令微调模型和一个推理增强模型都置于一个“特洛伊木马”简历的测试之下。

Result: 观察到不同的故障模式：标准模型在简单攻击中诉诸脆弱的幻觉来证明攻击的合理性，并在复杂场景中过滤掉不合逻辑的限制。推理模型则展现出危险的两面性：它利用高级战略重构使简单攻击具有高度说服力，但在面对逻辑复杂的指令时表现出“元认知泄漏”，即处理复杂对抗性指令的认知负荷导致注入逻辑无意中打印在最终输出中。

Conclusion: 这项研究揭示了一种故障模式，即推理模型在处理复杂对抗性指令时的认知负荷导致注入逻辑无意中打印在最终输出中。这使得人类比在标准模型中更容易检测到攻击，尽管推理模型在简单攻击中表现出更强的说服力。这表明在LLM安全中存在一个复杂的权衡。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into automated decision-making pipelines, specifically within Human Resources (HR), the security implications of Indirect Prompt Injection (IPI) become critical. While a prevailing hypothesis posits that "Reasoning" or "Chain-of-Thought" Models possess safety advantages due to their ability to self-correct, emerging research suggests these capabilities may enable more sophisticated alignment failures. This qualitative Red-Teaming case study challenges the safety-through-reasoning premise using the Qwen 3 30B architecture. By subjecting both a standard instruction-tuned model and a reasoning-enhanced model to a "Trojan Horse" curriculum vitae, distinct failure modes are observed. The results suggest a complex trade-off: while the Standard Model resorted to brittle hallucinations to justify simple attacks and filtered out illogical constraints in complex scenarios, the Reasoning Model displayed a dangerous duality. It employed advanced strategic reframing to make simple attacks highly persuasive, yet exhibited "Meta-Cognitive Leakage" when faced with logically convoluted commands. This study highlights a failure mode where the cognitive load of processing complex adversarial instructions causes the injection logic to be unintentionally printed in the final output, rendering the attack more detectable by humans than in Standard Models.

</details>


### [340] [Red-Teaming Claude Opus and ChatGPT-based Security Advisors for Trusted Execution Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19450)
*Kunal Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究调查了将大型语言模型（LLM）助手用作可信执行环境（TEE）安全顾问的风险，这些助手可能出现幻觉或提供不安全的建议。论文提出了一种红队测试方法（TEE-RedBench）来评估这些风险，并开发了一种“LLM-in-the-loop”评估管道，可将故障率降低80.62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可信执行环境（TEEs）旨在保护敏感计算免受受损操作系统的影响，但实际部署仍易受微架构泄漏、侧信道攻击和故障注入的影响。同时，安全团队越来越依赖大型语言模型（LLM）助手作为TEE架构审查、缓解规划和漏洞分类的安全顾问。这产生了一个社会技术风险面：助手可能会虚构TEE机制，夸大保障（例如，证明可以和不可以建立什么），或者在对抗性提示下表现不安全。

Method: 本文对两个广泛部署的LLM助手（ChatGPT-5.2和Claude Opus-4.6）作为TEE安全顾问进行了红队研究，重点关注固有限制和提示诱导故障在LLM之间的可迁移性。论文引入了TEE-RedBench，这是一种以TEE为基础的评估方法，包括（i）用于LLM介导安全工作的TEE特定威胁模型，（ii）涵盖SGX和TrustZone架构、证明和密钥管理、威胁建模以及非操作缓解指南的结构化提示套件，以及策略绑定的误用探测，和（iii）一个共同衡量技术正确性、扎实性、不确定性校准、拒绝质量和安全有用性的注释标准。论文还概述了一个“LLM-in-the-loop”评估管道：策略门控、检索接地、结构化模板和轻量级验证检查。

Result: 研究发现，LLM助手的某些故障并非纯粹是特殊性的，它们在LLM助手之间可迁移高达12.02%。此外，结合使用“LLM-in-the-loop”评估管道（包括策略门控、检索接地、结构化模板和轻量级验证检查）可将故障率降低80.62%。

Conclusion: 将LLM助手用作TEE安全顾问会带来显著的社会技术风险，因为它们可能提供不准确、虚构或不安全的建议。本文提出的TEE-RedBench评估方法揭示了LLM助手在TEE安全建议方面存在可迁移的故障。通过实施“LLM-in-the-loop”评估管道，可以显著提高LLM助手在TEE安全上下文中的建议安全性和正确性。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) (e.g., Intel SGX and ArmTrustZone) aim to protect sensitive computation from a compromised operating system, yet real deployments remain vulnerable to microarchitectural leakage, side-channel attacks, and fault injection. In parallel, security teams increasingly rely on Large Language Model (LLM) assistants as security advisors for TEE architecture review, mitigation planning, and vulnerability triage. This creates a socio-technical risk surface: assistants may hallucinate TEE mechanisms, overclaim guarantees (e.g., what attestation does and does not establish), or behave unsafely under adversarial prompting. We present a red-teaming study of two prevalently deployed LLM assistants in the role of TEE security advisors: ChatGPT-5.2 and Claude Opus-4.6, focusing on the inherent limitations and transferability of prompt-induced failures across LLMs. We introduce TEE-RedBench, a TEE-grounded evaluation methodology comprising (i) a TEE-specific threat model for LLM-mediated security work, (ii) a structured prompt suite spanning SGX and TrustZone architecture, attestation and key management, threat modeling, and non-operational mitigation guidance, along with policy-bound misuse probes, and (iii) an annotation rubric that jointly measures technical correctness, groundedness, uncertainty calibration, refusal quality, and safe helpfulness. We find that some failures are not purely idiosyncratic, transferring up to 12.02% across LLM assistants, and we connect these outcomes to secure architecture by outlining an "LLM-in-the-loop" evaluation pipeline: policy gating, retrieval grounding, structured templates, and lightweight verification checks that, when combined, reduce failures by 80.62%.

</details>


### [341] [Agentic AI as a Cybersecurity Attack Surface: Threats, Exploits, and Defenses in Runtime Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19555)
*Xiaochong Jiang,Shiqi Yang,Wenting Yang,Yichen Liu,Cheng Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化了基于大型语言模型（LLM）的智能体系统在运行时面临的安全风险，将其分为数据和工具供应链攻击，并识别出一种无需代码缺陷即可自我传播的“病毒式智能体循环”。作者最终提出了一种零信任运行时架构来应对这些威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的智能体系统将攻击面从构建时转移到推理时依赖，现有研究多关注模型层漏洞，而对循环和相互依赖的运行时行为中出现的安全风险理解碎片化，需要一个统一的框架来系统化这些风险。

Method: 作者在一个统一的运行时框架内系统化了这些风险，将威胁分为数据供应链攻击（瞬时上下文注入和持久内存中毒）和工具供应链攻击（发现、实现和调用）。此外，他们识别了“病毒式智能体循环”这种新型攻击。

Result: 研究结果是将运行时安全威胁系统化分类，包括数据和工具供应链攻击，并提出了一种新的威胁模型——“病毒式智能体循环”，该循环无需利用代码级缺陷即可实现生成式蠕虫的自我传播。

Conclusion: 作者倡导采用零信任运行时架构，将上下文视为不可信的控制流，并通过加密溯源而非语义推理来限制工具执行，以应对这些新兴的运行时安全挑战。

Abstract: Agentic systems built on large language models (LLMs) extend beyond text generation to autonomously retrieve information and invoke tools. This runtime execution model shifts the attack surface from build-time artifacts to inference-time dependencies, exposing agents to manipulation through untrusted data and probabilistic capability resolution. While prior work has focused on model-level vulnerabilities, security risks emerging from cyclic and interdependent runtime behavior remain fragmented. We systematize these risks within a unified runtime framework, categorizing threats into data supply chain attacks (transient context injection and persistent memory poisoning) and tool supply chain attacks (discovery, implementation, and invocation). We further identify the Viral Agent Loop, in which agents act as vectors for self-propagating generative worms without exploiting code-level flaws. Finally, we advocate a Zero-Trust Runtime Architecture that treats context as untrusted control flow and constrains tool execution through cryptographic provenance rather than semantic inference.

</details>
